@@ -0,0 +1,14 @@
+/**
+ * CSV/TSV (DDE) injection: a text cell written to a file another app opens is never run as a
+ * formula there. Spreadsheet apps trim leading whitespace (and a BOM) before deciding, and treat
+ * a leading `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, tab or CR as a formula, so such text gets a leading `'`.
+ *
+ * THE RULE: anything labelled export, download or "copy as CSV/TSV for other apps" is guarded
+ * with `formulaSafeCell`. An in-app lossless copy (the sheet's clipboard, pasted back into our own
+ * grid) stays raw. Only TEXT is guarded: a number such as -5 is a value, never prefixed.
+ */
+const FORMULA_START = /^[\p{White_Space}﻿]*[=+\-@]|^[\t\r]/u
+
+export function formulaSafeCell(text: string): string {
+  return FORMULA_START.test(text) ? `'${text}` : text
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,47 @@
+/**
+ * The one link allow-list. Links built from artifact content (a doc or data-page link, a comment,
+ * a slide link, a whiteboard link card, a sheet hyperlink cell, a pasted or exported link) come
+ * from agents, link editors and other people, so they are data. Only web and mail links survive;
+ * `javascript:`, `data:`, `vbscript:`, `file:`, protocol-relative `//host` and the rest render as
+ * plain text. Every rendered link to another page also opens in a new tab with `SAFE_LINK_ATTRS`
+ * (no opener, no referrer).
+ */
+export const SAFE_LINK_SCHEMES = ['http:', 'https:', 'mailto:'] as const
+
+export type SafeLinkOptions = {
+  /** Also keep an in-page anchor (`#heading`, `#tab=…`) as typed. */
+  anchor?: boolean
+  /** Also keep a same-origin path (`/#artifact=…`) as typed; never `//host` or `/\host`. */
+  path?: boolean
+}
+
+/** C0 and C1 control characters: browsers drop some of them mid-scheme (`java\tscript:`). */
+const CONTROL = /[\u0000-\u001f\u007f-\u009f]/
+const ANCHOR = /^#[^\s]*$/
+const SAME_ORIGIN_PATH = /^\/(?![/\\])[^\s\\]*$/
+
+/**
+ * The normalised href when `url` is an absolute http(s) or mailto URL, else null. With
+ * `anchor` / `path`, an in-page anchor or a same-origin path is kept as typed.
+ */
+export function safeLinkHref(url: string | null | undefined, options: SafeLinkOptions = {}): string | null {
+  if (typeof url !== 'string') return null
+  const value = url.trim()
+  if (!value || CONTROL.test(value)) return null
+  if (value.startsWith('#')) return options.anchor && ANCHOR.test(value) ? value : null
+  if (value.startsWith('/') || value.startsWith('\\'))
+    return options.path && SAME_ORIGIN_PATH.test(value) ? value : null
+  try {
+    const parsed = new URL(value)
+    return (SAFE_LINK_SCHEMES as readonly string[]).includes(parsed.protocol) ? parsed.href : null
+  } catch {
+    return null
+  }
+}
+
+/** Spread onto every `<a>` whose href came from content. */
+export const SAFE_LINK_ATTRS = {
+  target: '_blank',
+  rel: 'noopener noreferrer',
+  referrerPolicy: 'no-referrer',
+} as const

new file mode 100644
index 000000000..8a708540a
--- /dev/null

@@ -0,0 +1,54 @@
+import type { DesignBoard, DesignNode } from './design'
+
+/**
+ * What changed on one board between two revisions, by node id: added, removed and changed
+ * layers (a change is any own field: text, colour, size, place; children order counts on the
+ * frame). Pure; the compare view lists these beside the visual overlay.
+ */
+export type BoardDiff = {
+  added: Array<{ id: string; label: string }>
+  removed: Array<{ id: string; label: string }>
+  changed: Array<{ id: string; label: string; fields: string[] }>
+  /** Board fields (size, background, theme…) that changed. */
+  board: string[]
+}
+
+const labelOf = (node: DesignNode) => node.name ?? (node.type === 'text' ? `“${node.text.slice(0, 32)}”` : node.type === 'button' ? `button “${node.label}”` : `${node.type} ${node.id}`)
+
+function flatten(board: DesignBoard | undefined): Map<string, DesignNode> {
+  const out = new Map<string, DesignNode>()
+  if (!board) return out
+  const stack: DesignNode[] = [board.root]
+  while (stack.length) {
+    const node = stack.pop()!
+    out.set(node.id, node)
+    if (node.type === 'frame') stack.push(...node.children)
+  }
+  return out
+}
+
+export function diffBoard(before: DesignBoard | undefined, after: DesignBoard | undefined): BoardDiff {
+  const a = flatten(before)
+  const b = flatten(after)
+  const diff: BoardDiff = { added: [], removed: [], changed: [], board: [] }
+  for (const [id, node] of b) if (!a.has(id)) diff.added.push({ id, label: labelOf(node) })
+  for (const [id, node] of a) if (!b.has(id)) diff.removed.push({ id, label: labelOf(node) })
+  for (const [id, node] of b) {
+    const old = a.get(id)
+    if (!old) continue
+    const fields = new Set<string>()
+    const own = (value: DesignNode) => Object.entries(value).filter(([key]) => key !== 'children')
+    const left = new Map(own(old))
+    const right = new Map(own(node))
+    for (const key of new Set([...left.keys(), ...right.keys()])) if (JSON.stringify(left.get(key)) !== JSON.stringify(right.get(key))) fields.add(key)
+    if (old.type === 'frame' && node.type === 'frame' && old.children.map((c) => c.id).join() !== node.children.map((c) => c.id).join()) fields.add('children')
+    if (fields.size) diff.changed.push({ id, label: labelOf(node), fields: [...fields].sort() })
+  }
+  if (before && after) {
+    for (const key of new Set([...Object.keys(before), ...Object.keys(after)])) {
+      if (key === 'root') continue
+      if (JSON.stringify((before as Record<string, unknown>)[key]) !== JSON.stringify((after as Record<string, unknown>)[key])) diff.board.push(key)
+    }
+  }
+  return diff
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,91 @@
+/**
+ * Freebuff Sheets — TSV (the clipboard format Excel and Google Sheets exchange)
+ * and CSV (RFC 4180). Pure.
+ *
+ * Writing quotes a field only when it must (it holds the delimiter, a quote, a
+ * newline, or leading/trailing spaces for CSV). Reading accepts quoted fields,
+ * doubled quotes, CRLF or LF, and drops the single trailing newline Excel adds.
+ */
+
+function quoteField(value: string, delimiter: string, quoteSpaces: boolean): string {
+  const needs =
+    value.includes(delimiter) ||
+    value.includes('"') ||
+    value.includes('\n') ||
+    value.includes('\r') ||
+    (quoteSpaces && value !== value.trim())
+  return needs ? `"${value.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : value
+}
+
+export function writeDelimited(rows: string[][], delimiter: '\t' | ','): string {
+  const lineEnd = delimiter === ',' ? '\r\n' : '\n'
+  return rows.map((row) => row.map((field) => quoteField(field, delimiter, delimiter === ',')).join(delimiter)).join(lineEnd)
+}
+
+export const toTsv = (rows: string[][]) => writeDelimited(rows, '\t')
+export const toCsv = (rows: string[][]) => writeDelimited(rows, ',')
+
+/** Parse delimited text into rows of fields. Ragged rows are kept ragged. */
+export function readDelimited(text: string, delimiter: '\t' | ','): string[][] {
+  const rows: string[][] = []
+  let row: string[] = []
+  let field = ''
+  let i = 0
+  let quoted = false
+  // Strip a UTF-8 BOM, which Excel's CSV export puts first.
+  if (text.charCodeAt(0) === 0xfeff) i = 1
+  const n = text.length
+  let fieldStarted = false
+  while (i < n) {
+    const ch = text[i]
+    if (quoted) {
+      if (ch === '"') {
+        if (text[i + 1] === '"') {
+          field += '"'
+          i += 2
+          continue
+        }
+        quoted = false
+        i += 1
+        continue
+      }
+      field += ch
+      i += 1
+      continue
+    }
+    if (ch === '"' && !fieldStarted) {
+      quoted = true
+      fieldStarted = true
+      i += 1
+      continue
+    }
+    if (ch === delimiter) {
+      row.push(field)
+      field = ''
+      fieldStarted = false
+      i += 1
+      continue
+    }
+    if (ch === '\r' || ch === '\n') {
+      row.push(field)
+      rows.push(row)
+      row = []
+      field = ''
+      fieldStarted = false
+      i += ch === '\r' && text[i + 1] === '\n' ? 2 : 1
+      continue
+    }
+    field += ch
+    fieldStarted = true
+    i += 1
+  }
+  // The last line, unless the text ended with its newline.
+  if (fieldStarted || field !== '' || row.length > 0) {
+    row.push(field)
+    rows.push(row)
+  }
+  return rows
+}
+
+export const parseTsv = (text: string) => readDelimited(text, '\t')
+export const parseCsv = (text: string) => readDelimited(text, ',')

+/**
+ * The chart spec shared by artifact kinds: the JSON a Docs ```chart block holds,
+ * and the `chart` of a slide. Pure (zod only), so the server validates a body
+ * with exactly the rules the app draws by.
+ *
+ *   { "type": "bar" | "line" | "area", "title"?: string, "x": "<row key for the x axis>",
+ *     "series": [{ "key": "<row key>", "label": "<legend label>" }],   // 1-8, unique, not x
+ *     "data": [{ "<x>": "Week 1", "<series key>": 40 }, ...] }           // 1-500 rows
+ *
+ * Every row has the x key; every value is a string or a finite number, and
+ * series values are numbers. It mirrors `parseChartSpec` (freebuff/web doc/
+ * DocChart.tsx) rule for rule and is never looser: it also rejects unknown keys
+ * on a series entry. `slides-model.test.ts` checks both agree.
+ *
+ * Sheet charts (a range of cells) and data charts (a dataset's columns) are not
+ * this spec: sheet.ts shares `CHART_TYPES`, data.ts the series limit.
+ */
+
+import { z } from 'zod/v4'
+
+export const CHART_TYPES = ['bar', 'line', 'area'] as const
+export type ChartSpecType = (typeof CHART_TYPES)[number]
+
+export const CHART_LIMITS = { series: 8, rows: 500 } as const
+
+export type ChartSpec = {
+  type: ChartSpecType
+  title?: string
+  /** The key in each row that labels the x axis. */
+  x: string
+  series: Array<{ key: string; label: string }>
+  data: Array<Record<string, string | number>>
+}
+
+const chartCell = z.union([z.string(), z.number().finite()])
+
+export const chartSpecSchema = z
+  .strictObject({
+    type: z.enum(CHART_TYPES),
+    title: z.string().optional(),
+    x: z.string().refine((v) => v.trim() !== '', 'x must name a row key'),
+    series: z
+      .array(
+        z.strictObject({
+          key: z.string().refine((v) => v.trim() !== '', 'series key is empty'),
+          label: z.string(),
+        }),
+      )
+      .min(1)
+      .max(CHART_LIMITS.series),
+    data: z.array(z.record(z.string(), chartCell)).min(1).max(CHART_LIMITS.rows),
+  })
+  .superRefine((spec, ctx) => {
+    const keys = new Set<string>()
+    spec.series.forEach((s, i) => {
+      if (s.key === spec.x || keys.has(s.key)) {
+        ctx.addIssue({
+          code: 'custom',
+          path: ['series', i, 'key'],
+          message: 'series keys must be unique and differ from x',
+        })
+      }
+      keys.add(s.key)
+    })
+    spec.data.forEach((row, i) => {
+      if (!(spec.x in row)) {
+        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['data', i], message: `row lacks "${spec.x}"` })
+      }
+      for (const key of keys) {
+        if (key in row && typeof row[key] !== 'number') {
+          ctx.addIssue({
+            code: 'custom',
+            path: ['data', i, key],
+            message: 'series values must be numbers',
+          })
+        }
+      }
+    })
+  })
+
+// The schema's output is exactly the spec type.
+type _SpecFits = z.infer<typeof chartSpecSchema> extends ChartSpec ? true : never
+const _specFits: _SpecFits = true
+void _specFits

@@ -0,0 +1,71 @@
+import type { Design, DesignNode, Transition } from './design'
+import { resolveInstance } from './design-components'
+import { collectMains } from './design'
+import { boardsOnPage, pagesOf } from './design-pages'
+
+/**
+ * Prototype flows: every click-through link in a design (from which node on which board, to which
+ * board, with which transition) and the board the prototype starts on. Pure.
+ */
+
+export type PrototypeLink = {
+  fromBoardId: string
+  /** The node carrying `linkTo` (inside an instance: the instance's drawn id `<instance>~<node>`). */
+  nodeId: string
+  /** A readable label: the layer name, the button label or the node id. */
+  label: string
+  toBoardId: string
+  transition: Transition
+  trigger: 'click'
+}
+
+const labelOf = (node: DesignNode): string =>
+  node.name ?? (node.type === 'button' ? node.label : node.type === 'text' ? node.text.slice(0, 40) : node.id)
+
+/** Every link, board by board in page order, depth first (instances drawn as they would be). */
+export function prototypeLinks(design: Design): PrototypeLink[] {
+  const mains = collectMains(design.boards)
+  const out: PrototypeLink[] = []
+  const ordered = pagesOf(design).flatMap((page) => boardsOnPage(design, page.id))
+  for (const board of ordered) {
+    const visit = (node: DesignNode, depth: number) => {
+      if (depth > 40 || node.hidden) return
+      if ((node.type === 'frame' || node.type === 'button') && node.linkTo !== undefined) {
+        out.push({ fromBoardId: board.id, nodeId: node.id, label: labelOf(node), toBoardId: node.linkTo, transition: node.transition ?? 'instant', trigger: 'click' })
+      }
+      if (node.type !== 'frame') return
+      const shown = node.instanceOf !== undefined ? resolveInstance(node, mains) : node
+      if (shown !== node && shown.linkTo !== undefined && node.linkTo === undefined) {
+        out.push({ fromBoardId: board.id, nodeId: node.id, label: labelOf(shown), toBoardId: shown.linkTo, transition: shown.transition ?? 'instant', trigger: 'click' })
+      }
+      for (const child of shown.children) visit(child, depth + 1)
+    }
+    visit(board.root, 0)
+  }
+  return out
+}
+
+/** Where Present and the device preview start: `startBoard`, else the first board in page order. */
+export function startBoardOf(design: Design): string | null {
+  if (design.startBoard && design.boards.some((board) => board.id === design.startBoard)) return design.startBoard
+  const first = pagesOf(design).flatMap((page) => boardsOnPage(design, page.id))[0]
+  return first?.id ?? null
+}
+
+/** Sets (or with null clears) the prototype's start board; unchanged for an unknown board. */
+export function setStartBoard(design: Design, boardId: string | null): Design {
+  if (boardId === null) {
+    if (design.startBoard === undefined) return design
+    const { startBoard: _gone, ...rest } = design
+    return rest
+  }
+  if (!design.boards.some((board) => board.id === boardId)) return design
+  return { ...design, startBoard: boardId }
+}
+
+/** Boards no link reaches and that are not the start: dead ends of a flow to review. */
+export function unreachableBoards(design: Design): string[] {
+  const start = startBoardOf(design)
+  const reached = new Set(prototypeLinks(design).map((link) => link.toBoardId))
+  return design.boards.filter((board) => board.id !== start && !reached.has(board.id)).map((board) => board.id)
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-address.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-address.ts

@@ -0,0 +1,103 @@
+/**
+ * A1 addresses: column letters, cell keys and ranges. Pure, no React.
+ *
+ * Rows and columns are 0-based internally (`{ row: 0, col: 0 }` is `A1`).
+ */
+
+export type CellPos = { row: number; col: number }
+
+/** An inclusive rectangle, normalised so `top <= bottom` and `left <= right`. */
+export type CellRect = { top: number; left: number; bottom: number; right: number }
+
+const ADDRESS = /^([A-Z]{1,3})([1-9][0-9]{0,6})$/
+
+/** `0` → `A`, `25` → `Z`, `26` → `AA`. */
+export function colName(col: number): string {
+  let n = col + 1
+  let name = ''
+  while (n > 0) {
+    const rem = (n - 1) % 26
+    name = String.fromCharCode(65 + rem) + name
+    n = Math.floor((n - 1) / 26)
+  }
+  return name
+}
+
+/** `A` → `0`, `AA` → `26`. Letters must be upper case. */
+export function colIndex(name: string): number {
+  let n = 0
+  for (let i = 0; i < name.length; i += 1) n = n * 26 + (name.charCodeAt(i) - 64)
+  return n - 1
+}
+
+export function toAddress(pos: CellPos): string {
+  return `${colName(pos.col)}${pos.row + 1}`
+}
+
+/** `B3` → `{ row: 2, col: 1 }`; anything else (lower case, `$`, `A0`) → `null`. */
+export function parseAddress(address: string): CellPos | null {
+  const match = ADDRESS.exec(address)
+  if (!match) return null
+  return { row: Number(match[2]) - 1, col: colIndex(match[1]) }
+}
+
+export function rectOf(a: CellPos, b: CellPos): CellRect {
+  return {
+    top: Math.min(a.row, b.row),
+    bottom: Math.max(a.row, b.row),
+    left: Math.min(a.col, b.col),
+    right: Math.max(a.col, b.col),
+  }
+}
+
+export function rectContains(rect: CellRect, pos: CellPos): boolean {
+  return pos.row >= rect.top && pos.row <= rect.bottom && pos.col >= rect.left && pos.col <= rect.right
+}
+
+export function rectSize(rect: CellRect): { rows: number; cols: number } {
+  return { rows: rect.bottom - rect.top + 1, cols: rect.right - rect.left + 1 }
+}
+
+/** `A1:C3`, or `A1` for a single cell. */
+export function rectToRange(rect: CellRect): string {
+  const start = toAddress({ row: rect.top, col: rect.left })
+  if (rect.top === rect.bottom && rect.left === rect.right) return start
+  return `${start}:${toAddress({ row: rect.bottom, col: rect.right })}`
+}
+
+/** `A1:C3` or `A1` → a rect; `null` when malformed. (No sheet prefix, no `$`.) */
+export function parseRange(range: string): CellRect | null {
+  const [first, second, extra] = range.split(':')
+  if (extra !== undefined || !first) return null
+  const a = parseAddress(first)
+  const b = second === undefined ? a : parseAddress(second)
+  if (!a || !b) return null
+  return rectOf(a, b)
+}
+
+/**
+ * A range with an optional tab prefix: `Summary!A1:C3`, `'Q4 plan'!B2`.
+ * Returns the tab name (unquoted) when present.
+ */
+export function parseSheetRange(source: string): { tab?: string; rect: CellRect } | null {
+  const bang = source.lastIndexOf('!')
+  if (bang === -1) {
+    const rect = parseRange(source)
+    return rect ? { rect } : null
+  }
+  let tab = source.slice(0, bang)
+  if (tab.startsWith("'")) {
+    if (!tab.endsWith("'") || tab.length < 3) return null
+    tab = tab.slice(1, -1).replace(/''/g, "'")
+  }
+  if (!tab) return null
+  const rect = parseRange(source.slice(bang + 1))
+  return rect ? { tab, rect } : null
+}
+
+/** A tab name as a formula writes it: bare when it is a plain word, otherwise quoted. */
+export function quoteTabName(name: string): string {
+  return /^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.]*$/.test(name) && !/^[A-Za-z]{1,3}[0-9]+$/.test(name)
+    ? name
+    : `'${name.replace(/'/g, "''")}'`
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-richtext.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-richtext.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..1bb461f87
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-richtext.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,114 @@
+/**
+ * Freebuff Sheets — rich text inside one cell, as styled runs (`cell.runs`) over the plain text
+ * `cell.v`. The editor types it Markdown-ish: **bold**, *italic*, ~~strike~~, ++underline++ and
+ * [label](https://url); a cell that holds runs edits back as the same markup. Pure.
+ *
+ * `v` stays the plain text, so formulas, find, sort and CSV read the words, never the markup.
+ */
+
+import { safeLinkHref } from './safe-link'
+import type { SheetCell, TextRun } from './sheet'
+
+const LINK = /^\[([^\]\n]+)\]\(([^)\s]+)\)/
+const WORD = /[\p{L}\p{N}]/u
+const MARKS: Array<{ open: string; key: 'bold' | 'italic' | 'strike' | 'underline' }> = [
+  { open: '**', key: 'bold' },
+  { open: '~~', key: 'strike' },
+  { open: '++', key: 'underline' },
+  { open: '*', key: 'italic' },
+]
+
+/** A run with no style of its own folds into its plain neighbours. */
+function push(runs: TextRun[], run: TextRun) {
+  if (!run.text) return
+  const last = runs[runs.length - 1]
+  const plain = (r: TextRun) => Object.keys(r).length === 1
+  if (last && plain(last) && plain(run)) last.text += run.text
+  else runs.push(run)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Markup → plain text plus runs; `null` when `raw` holds no markup (so it is ordinary text).
+ * An unsafe link (`javascript:`) stays literal text.
+ */
+export function parseRichText(raw: string): { text: string; runs: TextRun[] } | null {
+  const runs: TextRun[] = []
+  let plain = ''
+  let styled = false
+  let i = 0
+  while (i < raw.length) {
+    const rest = raw.slice(i)
+    const link = LINK.exec(rest)
+    if (link) {
+      const href = safeLinkHref(link[2])
+      if (href) {
+        push(runs, { text: plain })
+        plain = ''
+        push(runs, { text: link[1], link: href })
+        styled = true
+        i += link[0].length
+        continue
+      }
+    }
+    const mark = MARKS.find((m) => rest.startsWith(m.open))
+    if (mark) {
+      const close = rest.indexOf(mark.open, mark.open.length + 1)
+      const inner = close > 0 ? rest.slice(mark.open.length, close) : ''
+      // Stricter than Markdown so arithmetic and globs stay text ("2*3*4", "*.ts,*.tsx"): the
+      // markers sit at word edges, the marked text starts and ends with a letter or digit,
+      // and never crosses a line.
+      const before = i === 0 ? '' : raw[i - 1]
+      const after = raw[i + close + mark.open.length] ?? ''
+      const edge = (ch: string) => ch === '' || (!WORD.test(ch) && ch !== '*' && ch !== '~' && ch !== '+')
+      if (
+        inner &&
+        WORD.test(inner[0]) &&
+        WORD.test(inner[inner.length - 1]) &&
+        edge(before) &&
+        edge(after) &&
+        !inner.includes('\n') &&
+        !(mark.open === '*' && inner.includes('*'))
+      ) {
+        push(runs, { text: plain })
+        plain = ''
+        const run: TextRun = { text: inner }
+        run[mark.key] = true
+        push(runs, run)
+        styled = true
+        i += close + mark.open.length
+        continue
+      }
+    }
+    plain += raw[i]
+    i += 1
+  }
+  if (!styled) return null
+  push(runs, { text: plain })
+  return { text: runs.map((run) => run.text).join(''), runs }
+}
+
+/** Runs → the markup the editor shows (the inverse of `parseRichText` for what it makes). */
+export function richTextToMarkup(runs: readonly TextRun[]): string {
+  return runs
+    .map((run) => {
+      let text = run.text
+      if (run.link) return `[${text}](${run.link})`
+      if (run.italic) text = `*${text}*`
+      if (run.strike) text = `~~${text}~~`
+      if (run.underline) text = `++${text}++`
+      if (run.bold) text = `**${text}**`
+      return text
+    })
+    .join('')
+}
+
+/** The runs a cell shows, or null when they no longer match its text (a stale edit). */
+export function cellRuns(cell: SheetCell | undefined): TextRun[] | null {
+  if (!cell?.runs || typeof cell.v !== 'string') return null
+  return cell.runs.map((run) => run.text).join('') === cell.v ? cell.runs : null
+}
+
+/** `@Maya` or `@maya.lee`: a person mention, shown as a chip (no directory behind it). */
+export function isMention(text: string): boolean {
+  return /^@[\p{L}\p{N}._-]{1,40}(?: [\p{L}\p{N}._-]{1,40})?$/u.test(text)
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-image-src.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-image-src.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..de793bd79
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-image-src.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,106 @@
+/**
+ * Which image addresses an artifact may hold, shared by the kinds' validators
+ * (artifact-kinds/slides.ts, whiteboard.ts, design.ts) and the renderers'
+ * load decision (freebuff/web …/artifacts/remote-images.ts).
+ *
+ * Validation stays permissive on purpose (save and restore re-validate whole
+ * stored bodies, so tightening would strand existing artifacts): an https URL,
+ * or a same-origin path. Only the forms a browser would resolve OFF this
+ * origin are refused: `//host`, `/\host`, a backslash anywhere, and an encoded
+ * slash or backslash (`/%2F…`, `/%5C…`), plus markup characters in a path. Loading is the renderers' stricter
+ * call: an uploaded asset (`/_asset/<uuid>`) always, a third-party https image
+ * only after the reader opts in, anything else never.
+ *
+ * What an AGENT may add is stricter still (`unstoredImageSrcsAdded`, enforced by the store's write
+ * pipeline for agent writes): only this artifact's stored assets, or the built-in placeholder. A
+ * src the stored body already holds is kept, so existing bodies and people's own https images
+ * still save; the agent imports a picture (import_artifact_image) instead of hotlinking it.
+ */
+
+import { ARTIFACT_ASSET_REF_PATTERN } from './artifacts'
+
+const CONTROL_OR_SPACE = /[\u0000- \u007f-\u009f]/
+const ENCODED_SEPARATOR = /%(?:2f|5c)/i
+/** Never part of a real path (a browser percent-encodes them); refused so no export can be broken out of. */
+const MARKUP = /[<>"'`]/
+
+/** A path on this origin: one leading `/`, then a letter, digit, `_` or `.`; nothing that leaves the origin. */
+export function isSameOriginImagePath(src: string): boolean {
+  return (
+    /^\/[A-Za-z0-9_.]/.test(src) &&
+    !src.includes('\\') &&
+    !CONTROL_OR_SPACE.test(src) &&
+    !ENCODED_SEPARATOR.test(src) &&
+    !MARKUP.test(src)
+  )
+}
+
+/** An absolute `https:` URL with a host and no credentials, as the browser will read it. */
+export function isRemoteImageUrl(src: string): boolean {
+  if (!/^https:\/\//i.test(src) || src.includes('\\') || CONTROL_OR_SPACE.test(src)) return false
+  try {
+    const url = new URL(src)
+    return url.protocol === 'https:' && url.hostname !== '' && url.username === '' && url.password === ''
+  } catch {
+    return false
+  }
+}
+
+/** Image srcs that are ours to serve without a remote fetch: the placeholder new slides use. */
+export const BUILT_IN_IMAGE_SRCS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['/Placeholder.svg'])
+
+/** An uploaded asset ref (`/_asset/<uuid>`) or a built-in image: what an agent write may add. */
+export function isStoredImageSrc(src: string): boolean {
+  return ARTIFACT_ASSET_REF_PATTERN.test(src) || BUILT_IN_IMAGE_SRCS.has(src)
+}
+
+const MARKDOWN_IMAGE = /!\[[^\]]*\]\(\s*<?([^\s)>]+)/g
+const HTML_IMAGE = /<img\b[^>]*?\bsrc\s*=\s*["']([^"']+)["']/gi
+
+/**
+ * Every image src in a body, in order, distinct: a Markdown body's Markdown and HTML images, and
+ * every `src` in a JSON kind (slide images and elements, design nodes and fills, whiteboard
+ * images), except inside free-form records (`FREE_FORM_KEYS`: a chart's `data`, a design data
+ * list's `rows`), whose columns are named by the author and may well be called "src". Empty for
+ * an unreadable JSON body.
+ */
+export function bodyImageSrcs(format: 'markdown' | 'json', body: string): string[] {
+  const found = new Set<string>()
+  const note = (src: unknown) => {
+    if (typeof src === 'string' && src) found.add(src)
+  }
+  if (format !== 'json') {
+    for (const match of body.matchAll(MARKDOWN_IMAGE)) note(match[1])
+    for (const match of body.matchAll(HTML_IMAGE)) note(match[1])
+    return [...found]
+  }
+  let parsed: unknown
+  try {
+    parsed = JSON.parse(body)
+  } catch {
+    return []
+  }
+  const walk = (value: unknown, depth: number) => {
+    if (depth > 64 || !value || typeof value !== 'object') return
+    if (Array.isArray(value)) for (const item of value) walk(item, depth + 1)
+    else
+      for (const [key, field] of Object.entries(value)) {
+        if (key === 'src') note(field)
+        else if (!FREE_FORM_KEYS.has(key)) walk(field, depth + 1)
+      }
+  }
+  walk(parsed, 0)
+  return [...found]
+}
+
+/** Keys whose value is author-named data rows (chart.ts `data`, design.ts data lists' `rows`), never image holders. */
+const FREE_FORM_KEYS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['data', 'rows'])
+
+/**
+ * The image srcs `next` adds that are not stored images (`isStoredImageSrc`) and that `stored`
+ * did not already hold: what an agent write may not introduce. `stored` is null for a new body.
+ */
+export function unstoredImageSrcsAdded(format: 'markdown' | 'json', stored: string | null, next: string): string[] {
+  const had = new Set(stored === null ? [] : bodyImageSrcs(format, stored))
+  return bodyImageSrcs(format, next).filter((src) => !isStoredImageSrc(src) && !had.has(src))
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/slides-templates.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/slides-templates.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..42f186857
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/slides-templates.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,136 @@
+/**
+ * Slide templates: ready-made starting points (a layout, placeholder text and, where the layout
+ * alone cannot draw it, free-position elements) for "Add slide" and the agent's
+ * `addSlideFromTemplate`. Pure: the same template, id and aspect give the same slide, and every
+ * slide satisfies `slideSchema`.
+ */
+
+import { DESIGN_SIZE, LIMITS, type Deck, type DeckAspect, type Slide, type SlideElement } from './slides'
+
+export const SLIDE_TEMPLATES = [
+  'title',
+  'agenda',
+  'comparison',
+  'timeline',
+  'big-number',
+  'quote',
+  'team',
+  'closing',
+] as const
+export type SlideTemplate = (typeof SLIDE_TEMPLATES)[number]
+
+export const SLIDE_TEMPLATE_LABEL: Record<SlideTemplate, string> = {
+  title: 'Title',
+  agenda: 'Agenda',
+  comparison: 'Comparison',
+  timeline: 'Timeline',
+  'big-number': 'Big number',
+  quote: 'Quote',
+  team: 'Team',
+  closing: 'Closing',
+}
+
+const r = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 10) / 10
+
+/** A new slide from `template`, under `id`, laid out for the deck's `aspect`. */
+export function slideFromTemplate(template: SlideTemplate, id: string, aspect: DeckAspect): Slide {
+  const { width, height } = DESIGN_SIZE[aspect]
+  switch (template) {
+    case 'title':
+      return { id, layout: 'title', title: 'Presentation title', subtitle: 'Presenter · date' }
+    case 'agenda':
+      return {
+        id,
+        layout: 'title-body',
+        title: 'Agenda',
+        body: '1. Where we are\n2. What we learned\n3. What we propose\n4. Next steps',
+      }
+    case 'comparison':
+      return {
+        id,
+        layout: 'two-column',
+        title: 'Option A vs option B',
+        columns: ['**Option A**\n\n- Strength\n- Trade-off', '**Option B**\n\n- Strength\n- Trade-off'],
+      }
+    case 'timeline': {
+      const left = 80
+      const right = width - 80
+      const axis = r(height * 0.55)
+      const steps = 4
+      const gap = (right - left) / (steps - 1)
+      const elements: SlideElement[] = [
+        { id: 'axis', type: 'line', x: left, y: axis - 10, w: right - left, h: 20, stroke: 'soft', strokeWidth: 3 },
+      ]
+      for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
+        const cx = r(left + gap * i)
+        elements.push({ id: `dot-${i + 1}`, type: 'ellipse', x: r(cx - 10), y: axis - 10, w: 20, h: 20, fill: 'accent' })
+        elements.push({
+          id: `step-${i + 1}`,
+          type: 'text',
+          x: r(cx - gap / 2 + 8),
+          y: axis + 24,
+          w: r(gap - 16),
+          h: 70,
+          text: `Q${i + 1}\nMilestone`,
+          size: 18,
+          align: 'center',
+        })
+      }
+      return { id, layout: 'title-body', title: 'Timeline', elements }
+    }
+    case 'big-number':
+      return {
+        id,
+        layout: 'blank',
+        elements: [
+          { id: 'number', type: 'text', x: 64, y: r(height * 0.2), w: width - 128, h: 190, text: '42%', size: 140, bold: true, align: 'center', color: 'accent' },
+          { id: 'caption', type: 'text', x: 64, y: r(height * 0.2 + 206), w: width - 128, h: 60, text: 'What this number means', size: 26, align: 'center', color: 'soft' },
+        ],
+      }
+    case 'quote':
+      return { id, layout: 'quote', body: 'A sentence worth repeating.', subtitle: 'Name, role' }
+    case 'team': {
+      const people = 4
+      const size = 120
+      const gap = (width - 128 - people * size) / (people - 1)
+      const top = r(height * 0.36)
+      const elements: SlideElement[] = []
+      for (let i = 0; i < people; i++) {
+        const x = r(64 + i * (size + gap))
+        elements.push({ id: `photo-${i + 1}`, type: 'ellipse', x, y: top, w: size, h: size, fill: 'tint', stroke: 'accent', strokeWidth: 2 })
+        elements.push({ id: `name-${i + 1}`, type: 'text', x: r(x - 30), y: top + size + 14, w: size + 60, h: 64, text: 'Name\nRole', size: 18, align: 'center' })
+      }
+      return { id, layout: 'title-body', title: 'The team', elements }
+    }
+    case 'closing':
+      return { id, layout: 'section', title: 'Thank you', subtitle: 'Questions? name@example.com' }
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Insert a slide from `template` after `afterIndex` (-1: first), under `id` (default: a fresh
+ * `<template>-N`). A full deck or a taken id leaves the deck unchanged with `ok` false.
+ */
+export function addSlideFromTemplate(
+  deck: Deck,
+  afterIndex: number,
+  template: SlideTemplate,
+  id?: string,
+): { ok: true; deck: Deck; id: string } | { ok: false; error: string } {
+  if (deck.slides.length >= LIMITS.slides) return { ok: false, error: `a deck has at most ${LIMITS.slides} slides` }
+  const used = new Set(deck.slides.map((slide) => slide.id))
+  let slideId = id
+  if (slideId === undefined) {
+    for (let n = 1; ; n++) {
+      const candidate = n === 1 ? template : `${template}-${n}`
+      if (!used.has(candidate)) {
+        slideId = candidate
+        break
+      }
+    }
+  } else if (used.has(slideId)) return { ok: false, error: `a slide "${slideId}" exists; give the new one another id` }
+  const at = Math.max(0, Math.min(afterIndex + 1, deck.slides.length))
+  const slides = [...deck.slides]
+  slides.splice(at, 0, slideFromTemplate(template, slideId, deck.aspect))
+  return { ok: true, deck: { ...deck, slides }, id: slideId }
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-vector.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-vector.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..d5b2897ca
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-vector.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,130 @@
+import type { ShapeNode } from './design'
+
+/**
+ * Vector shape geometry: an SVG path (`d`) for a shape's kind inside its w×h box. The renderer,
+ * the code export and the board export all draw vectors from this, so they match. Pure.
+ */
+
+const r = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 100) / 100
+
+/** The shape kinds drawn as an SVG path rather than a CSS box. */
+export const VECTOR_KINDS = ['arrow', 'polygon', 'star', 'path'] as const
+export function isVectorShape(node: ShapeNode): boolean {
+  return (VECTOR_KINDS as readonly string[]).includes(node.kind)
+}
+
+/** Regular polygon points inscribed in the box (first vertex at the top). */
+export function polygonPoints(sides: number, w: number, h: number): Array<[number, number]> {
+  const n = Math.max(3, Math.min(24, Math.round(sides)))
+  const out: Array<[number, number]> = []
+  for (let i = 0; i < n; i += 1) {
+    const angle = -Math.PI / 2 + (i * 2 * Math.PI) / n
+    out.push([r(w / 2 + (w / 2) * Math.cos(angle)), r(h / 2 + (h / 2) * Math.sin(angle))])
+  }
+  return out
+}
+
+/** Star points (outer and inner alternating, first point at the top). */
+export function starPoints(points: number, innerRatio: number, w: number, h: number): Array<[number, number]> {
+  const n = Math.max(3, Math.min(24, Math.round(points)))
+  const inner = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(0.95, innerRatio))
+  const out: Array<[number, number]> = []
+  for (let i = 0; i < n * 2; i += 1) {
+    const angle = -Math.PI / 2 + (i * Math.PI) / n
+    const k = i % 2 === 0 ? 1 : inner
+    out.push([r(w / 2 + (w / 2) * k * Math.cos(angle)), r(h / 2 + (h / 2) * k * Math.sin(angle))])
+  }
+  return out
+}
+
+const pathOf = (points: Array<[number, number]>, closed: boolean) =>
+  points.map(([x, y], i) => `${i === 0 ? 'M' : 'L'}${r(x)} ${r(y)}`).join(' ') + (closed ? ' Z' : '')
+
+/**
+ * A path whose segment i (point i to i + 1; with `closed`, the last back to the first) is a cubic
+ * curve when `curves[i]` gives its two control points (shares of the box), else a line.
+ */
+export function curvedPathOf(
+  points: Array<[number, number]>,
+  curves: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number, number, number] | null>,
+  closed: boolean,
+  w: number,
+  h: number,
+): string {
+  if (points.length === 0) return ''
+  const parts = [`M${r(points[0][0])} ${r(points[0][1])}`]
+  const segments = closed ? points.length : points.length - 1
+  for (let i = 0; i < segments; i += 1) {
+    const [x, y] = points[(i + 1) % points.length]
+    const curve = curves[i]
+    parts.push(curve ? `C${r(curve[0] * w)} ${r(curve[1] * h)} ${r(curve[2] * w)} ${r(curve[3] * h)} ${r(x)} ${r(y)}` : `L${r(x)} ${r(y)}`)
+  }
+  return parts.join(' ') + (closed ? ' Z' : '')
+}
+
+/**
+ * The SVG path of a vector shape in its w×h box, plus whether it is filled (closed) or only
+ * stroked. An arrow runs left to right through the box's middle with its head at the end.
+ */
+export function shapeGeometry(node: ShapeNode, w: number, h: number): { d: string; closed: boolean } {
+  switch (node.kind) {
+    case 'polygon':
+      return { d: pathOf(polygonPoints(node.sides ?? 6, w, h), true), closed: true }
+    case 'star':
+      return { d: pathOf(starPoints(node.sides ?? 5, node.innerRatio ?? 0.5, w, h), true), closed: true }
+    case 'path': {
+      const points = (node.vertices ?? [[0, 0], [1, 1]]).map(([x, y]) => [x * w, y * h] as [number, number])
+      if (!node.curves?.some(Boolean)) return { d: pathOf(points, node.closed === true), closed: node.closed === true }
+      return { d: curvedPathOf(points, node.curves, node.closed === true, w, h), closed: node.closed === true }
+    }
+    case 'arrow': {
+      const stroke = node.strokeWidth ?? 2
+      const head = Math.min(w / 2, Math.max(8, stroke * 4))
+      const mid = h / 2
+      return {
+        d: `M0 ${r(mid)} L${r(w)} ${r(mid)} M${r(w - head)} ${r(mid - head / 2)} L${r(w)} ${r(mid)} L${r(w - head)} ${r(mid + head / 2)}`,
+        closed: false,
+      }
+    }
+    default:
+      return { d: `M0 0 L${r(w)} 0 L${r(w)} ${r(h)} L0 ${r(h)} Z`, closed: true }
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * A pen path from board px points (and per-segment cubic control points, also board px): its box
+ * at (x, y), and the points and controls as shares of that box, which is how a path shape stores
+ * them. The editor's pen and the agent's addPath both build paths with this.
+ */
+export function pathShapeFromPoints(
+  points: Array<[number, number]>,
+  curves: Array<[number, number, number, number] | null> | undefined,
+  taken: ReadonlySet<string>,
+  id: string,
+): ShapeNode {
+  const minX = Math.min(...points.map(([x]) => x))
+  const minY = Math.min(...points.map(([, y]) => y))
+  const w = Math.max(1, Math.max(...points.map(([x]) => x)) - minX)
+  const h = Math.max(1, Math.max(...points.map(([, y]) => y)) - minY)
+  const share = (value: number, from: number, size: number) => Math.round(((value - from) / size) * 1000) / 1000
+  const clampShare = (value: number) => Math.min(2, Math.max(-1, value))
+  let nodeId = id
+  for (let n = 2; taken.has(nodeId); n += 1) nodeId = `${id}-${n}`
+  return {
+    type: 'shape',
+    id: nodeId,
+    kind: 'path',
+    x: Math.round(minX),
+    y: Math.round(minY),
+    w: Math.round(w),
+    h: Math.round(h),
+    vertices: points.map(([x, y]) => [share(x, minX, w), share(y, minY, h)] as [number, number]),
+    ...(curves?.some(Boolean)
+      ? {
+          curves: curves.map((curve) =>
+            curve ? ([clampShare(share(curve[0], minX, w)), clampShare(share(curve[1], minY, h)), clampShare(share(curve[2], minX, w)), clampShare(share(curve[3], minY, h))] as [number, number, number, number]) : null,
+          ),
+        }
+      : {}),
+  }
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-summary.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-summary.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..5892a8ce5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-summary.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,127 @@
+/**
+ * Freebuff Sheets — "Summarise": a pivot-style summary of a range on a new tab,
+ * written as LIVE formulas, never a static copy. The group keys are one spilled
+ * formula (`SORT(UNIQUE(FILTER(...)))`) and each measure one spilled *IFS
+ * formula whose criteria are the key spill (`SUMIFS(..., A2#)`), so editing the
+ * source updates the summary, and new keys appear on their own.
+ *
+ * As in Excel, a text key that reads as a criterion (">5", "a*") matches by
+ * that criterion.
+ */
+
+import { colName, quoteTabName, toAddress, type CellRect } from './sheet-address'
+import { addTab, tabNameProblem } from './sheet-ops'
+import { SHEET_LIMITS, type SheetCell, type Workbook } from './sheet'
+import type { SheetEngine } from './sheet-formula'
+
+export const SUMMARY_AGGS = ['sum', 'count', 'average', 'min', 'max'] as const
+export type SummaryAgg = (typeof SUMMARY_AGGS)[number]
+
+export const SUMMARY_AGG_LABEL: Record<SummaryAgg, string> = {
+  sum: 'Sum',
+  count: 'Count',
+  average: 'Average',
+  min: 'Min',
+  max: 'Max',
+}
+
+const AGG_FUNCTION: Record<SummaryAgg, string> = {
+  sum: 'SUMIFS',
+  count: 'COUNTIFS',
+  average: 'AVERAGEIFS',
+  min: 'MINIFS',
+  max: 'MAXIFS',
+}
+
+export type SummarySpec = {
+  /** The source tab and block, header row included. */
+  tabId: string
+  rect: CellRect
+  /** One or two absolute column indexes to group by. */
+  groupBy: number[]
+  /** What to compute per group; `count` ignores its column's values. */
+  values: Array<{ col: number; agg: SummaryAgg }>
+  /** The new tab's name (default "Summary of <tab>"). */
+  name?: string
+}
+
+export type SummaryResult = { ok: true; workbook: Workbook; tabId: string } | { ok: false; error: string }
+
+/** The header text of a source column, else its letter. */
+function headerOf(workbook: Workbook, engine: SheetEngine | null, tabId: string, row: number, col: number): string {
+  const tab = workbook.tabs.find((t) => t.id === tabId)
+  const cell = tab?.cells[toAddress({ row, col })]
+  const shown = engine?.value(tabId, row, col)
+  const text = typeof shown === 'string' || typeof shown === 'number' ? String(shown) : typeof cell?.v === 'string' ? cell.v : ''
+  return text.trim() || `Column ${colName(col)}`
+}
+
+/** The formulas of a summary, by address on the new tab (exported for tests and the panel preview). */
+export function summaryCells(workbook: Workbook, spec: SummarySpec, engine: SheetEngine | null = null): Record<string, SheetCell> | string {
+  const tab = workbook.tabs.find((t) => t.id === spec.tabId)
+  if (!tab) return 'no such tab'
+  const { rect } = spec
+  if (rect.bottom - rect.top < 1) return 'select a header row and at least one row of data'
+  const groups = [...new Set(spec.groupBy)]
+  if (groups.length < 1 || groups.length > 2) return 'group by one or two columns'
+  if (!spec.values.length) return 'add at least one value to compute'
+  if (spec.values.length > 20) return 'at most 20 values'
+  const inside = (col: number) => col >= rect.left && col <= rect.right
+  if (![...groups, ...spec.values.map((v) => v.col)].every(inside)) return 'every column must be inside the range'
+
+  const prefix = `${quoteTabName(tab.name)}!`
+  const column = (col: number) =>
+    `${prefix}${toAddress({ row: rect.top + 1, col })}:${toAddress({ row: rect.bottom, col })}`
+  const keyCol = column(groups[0])
+  // The keys: distinct (non-blank) rows of the group columns, sorted.
+  const source =
+    groups.length === 1
+      ? keyCol
+      : `CHOOSECOLS(${prefix}${toAddress({ row: rect.top + 1, col: Math.min(...groups) })}:${toAddress({ row: rect.bottom, col: Math.max(...groups) })}, ${groups
+          .map((g) => g - Math.min(...groups) + 1)
+          .join(', ')})`
+  const cells: Record<string, SheetCell> = {}
+  groups.forEach((g, i) => {
+    cells[toAddress({ row: 0, col: i })] = { v: headerOf(workbook, engine, tab.id, rect.top, g), bold: true, bg: 'gray' }
+  })
+  cells.A2 = { f: `=SORT(UNIQUE(FILTER(${source}, ${keyCol}<>"")))` }
+  const criteria =
+    groups.length === 1
+      ? `${keyCol}, A2#`
+      : groups.map((g, i) => `${column(g)}, INDEX(A2#, 0, ${i + 1})`).join(', ')
+  spec.values.forEach((value, i) => {
+    const col = groups.length + i
+    const label = `${SUMMARY_AGG_LABEL[value.agg]} of ${headerOf(workbook, engine, tab.id, rect.top, value.col)}`
+    cells[toAddress({ row: 0, col })] = { v: label.slice(0, 200), bold: true, bg: 'gray' }
+    const fn = AGG_FUNCTION[value.agg]
+    const formula = value.agg === 'count' ? `=${fn}(${criteria})` : `=${fn}(${column(value.col)}, ${criteria})`
+    cells[toAddress({ row: 1, col })] = { f: formula, ...(value.agg === 'count' ? {} : { fmt: 'number' as const }) }
+  })
+  return cells
+}
+
+/** Add the summary as a new tab after the others. */
+export function summarise(workbook: Workbook, spec: SummarySpec, engine: SheetEngine | null = null): SummaryResult {
+  if (workbook.tabs.length >= SHEET_LIMITS.tabs) return { ok: false, error: `a workbook has at most ${SHEET_LIMITS.tabs} tabs` }
+  const cells = summaryCells(workbook, spec, engine)
+  if (typeof cells === 'string') return { ok: false, error: cells }
+  const source = workbook.tabs.find((t) => t.id === spec.tabId)!
+  let name = (spec.name?.trim() || `Summary of ${source.name}`).slice(0, 31).trim()
+  if (tabNameProblem(workbook, name) !== null) {
+    for (let n = 2; n < 100; n += 1) {
+      const candidate = `${name.slice(0, 27)} ${n}`
+      if (tabNameProblem(workbook, candidate) === null) {
+        name = candidate
+        break
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  const width = spec.groupBy.length + spec.values.length
+  const result = addTab(workbook, {
+    name,
+    cells,
+    frozenRows: 1,
+    columns: Array.from({ length: width }, () => ({ width: 150 })),
+  })
+  return { ok: true, workbook: result.workbook, tabId: result.tabId }
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-filter.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-filter.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..c687706ca

+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-filter.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,138 @@
+/**
+ * Freebuff Sheets — column filters, shared by the page and the server.
+ *
+ * A tab's filter row (`tab.filter`) may carry a SAVED filter (`columns`, by column
+ * letter) that everyone sees. A viewer may also keep a private filter view on
+ * top (page state, never stored); when they have one for a filter, it replaces
+ * the saved one for them until they reset it.
+ */
+
+import { colIndex, colName } from './sheet-address'
+import { dateToSerial, isError, isoToSerial, type CellValue } from './sheet-formula'
+import type { ColumnFilter, DatePreset, SheetTab, Workbook } from './sheet'
+
+export type { ColumnFilter, DatePreset }
+
+export const DATE_PRESET_LABELS: Record<DatePreset, string> = {
+  today: 'Today',
+  yesterday: 'Yesterday',
+  last7: 'Last 7 days',
+  last30: 'Last 30 days',
+  thisWeek: 'This week',
+  thisMonth: 'This month',
+  lastMonth: 'Last month',
+  thisYear: 'This year',
+}
+
+export function columnFilterActive(filter: ColumnFilter | undefined): boolean {
+  return Boolean(filter && (filter.values || filter.contains || filter.op || filter.date))
+}
+
+const serialParts = (serial: number) => {
+  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(1899, 11, 30) + Math.floor(serial) * 86_400_000)
+  return { y: date.getUTCFullYear(), m: date.getUTCMonth() + 1, d: date.getUTCDate(), weekday: date.getUTCDay() }
+}
+
+/** The inclusive day serials a date filter keeps, given today's serial. */
+export function dateWindow(date: NonNullable<ColumnFilter['date']>, today: number): [number, number] {
+  let from = -Infinity
+  let to = Infinity
+  if (date.preset) {
+    const { y, m, weekday } = serialParts(today)
+    switch (date.preset) {
+      case 'today':
+        ;[from, to] = [today, today]
+        break
+      case 'yesterday':
+        ;[from, to] = [today - 1, today - 1]
+        break
+      case 'last7':
+        ;[from, to] = [today - 6, today]
+        break
+      case 'last30':
+        ;[from, to] = [today - 29, today]
+        break
+      case 'thisWeek':
+        // Weeks start on Monday.
+        from = today - ((weekday + 6) % 7)
+        to = from + 6
+        break
+      case 'thisMonth':
+        ;[from, to] = [dateToSerial(y, m, 1), dateToSerial(y, m + 1, 1) - 1]
+        break
+      case 'lastMonth':
+        ;[from, to] = [dateToSerial(y, m - 1, 1), dateToSerial(y, m, 1) - 1]
+        break
+      case 'thisYear':
+        ;[from, to] = [dateToSerial(y, 1, 1), dateToSerial(y + 1, 1, 1) - 1]
+        break
+    }
+  }
+  const a = date.from ? isoToSerial(date.from) : null
+  const b = date.to ? isoToSerial(date.to) : null
+  if (a !== null) from = Math.max(from, a)
+  if (b !== null) to = Math.min(to, b)
+  return [from, to]
+}
+
+const filterNumber = (raw: string | undefined): number | null => {
+  if (raw === undefined || raw.trim() === '') return null
+  const iso = isoToSerial(raw.trim())
+  if (iso !== null) return iso
+  const n = Number(raw.replace(/[$,%\s]/g, ''))
+  return Number.isFinite(n) ? (raw.trim().endsWith('%') ? n / 100 : n) : null
+}
+
+/** Whether a cell (its value and shown text) passes one column filter. */
+export function passesFilter(filter: ColumnFilter, value: CellValue, text: string, today: number): boolean {
+  if (filter.values && !filter.values.includes(text)) return false
+  if (filter.contains && !text.toLowerCase().includes(filter.contains.toLowerCase())) return false
+  if (filter.op) {
+    const a = filterNumber(filter.a)
+    const b = filterNumber(filter.b)
+    if (typeof value !== 'number' || a === null) return false
+    if (filter.op === 'gt' && !(value > a)) return false
+    if (filter.op === 'lt' && !(value < a)) return false
+    if (filter.op === 'eq' && value !== a) return false
+    if (filter.op === 'between' && (b === null || value < Math.min(a, b) || value > Math.max(a, b))) return false
+  }
+  if (filter.date) {
+    // Dates are day serials; an ISO text date counts too.
+    const serial = typeof value === 'number' ? value : typeof value === 'string' ? isoToSerial(value) : null
+    if (serial === null || isError(value)) return false
+    const [from, to] = dateWindow(filter.date, today)
+    const day = Math.floor(serial)
+    if (day < from || day > to) return false
+  }
+  return true
+}
+
+/** A tab's saved filter as absolute column index → filter. */
+export function savedColumns(tab: SheetTab): Record<number, ColumnFilter> {
+  const out: Record<number, ColumnFilter> = {}
+  for (const [letters, filter] of Object.entries(tab.filter?.columns ?? {})) out[colIndex(letters)] = filter
+  return out
+}
+
+/** Save `columns` (absolute column index → filter) on the tab's filter for everyone; null clears it. */
+export function saveFilter(workbook: Workbook, tabId: string, columns: Record<number, ColumnFilter> | null): Workbook {
+  const tabs = workbook.tabs.map((tab) => {
+    if (tab.id !== tabId || !tab.filter) return tab
+    const saved: Record<string, ColumnFilter> = {}
+    for (const [col, filter] of Object.entries(columns ?? {})) if (columnFilterActive(filter)) saved[colName(Number(col))] = filter
+    const { columns: _old, ...rest } = tab.filter
+    const filter = Object.keys(saved).length ? { ...rest, columns: saved } : rest
+    return JSON.stringify(filter) === JSON.stringify(tab.filter) ? tab : { ...tab, filter }
+  })
+  return tabs.some((tab, i) => tab !== workbook.tabs[i]) ? { ...workbook, tabs } : workbook
+}
+
+/** Remember the filter's sort (shown on its column's button). */
+export function setFilterSort(workbook: Workbook, tabId: string, sort: { col: number; desc?: boolean } | null): Workbook {
+  const tabs = workbook.tabs.map((tab) => {
+    if (tab.id !== tabId || !tab.filter) return tab
+    const { sort: _old, ...rest } = tab.filter
+    return { ...tab, filter: sort ? { ...rest, sort: { col: colName(sort.col), ...(sort.desc ? { desc: true } : {}) } } : rest }
+  })
+  return { ...workbook, tabs }
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/doc-blocks.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/doc-blocks.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..4075fbbe6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/doc-blocks.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,161 @@
+/**
+ * A stored doc's body as top-level blocks with their exact byte ranges, and block edits spliced
+ * back over exactly those bytes. This is the server's copy of the doc view's splice
+ * (freebuff/web/.../doc/doc-edit.ts `fileDocLayout` / `applyDocEdit`, for a body without a title
+ * line): the same split (blank lines, fences kept whole, `\r\n` read as `\n`), so block `n` here is
+ * the block the editor draws with `data-block-index="n"`, and every block an edit does not name
+ * keeps its bytes, line endings included. A parity test in the doc view holds the two together.
+ */
+
+import type { DocBlockEdit } from './doc-tabs'
+
+export type DocBlockRange = {
+  /** Offsets into the raw body; `end` excludes a trailing `\r`. */
+  start: number
+  end: number
+  /** The block's text with `\r\n` read as `\n`: what the editor draws and an edit's `before` names. */
+  source: string
+}
+
+const normalize = (text: string) => text.replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n')
+
+/** The body's top-level blocks, in order. */
+export function docBodyLayout(body: string): DocBlockRange[] {
+  const blocks: DocBlockRange[] = []
+  let current: { start: number; end: number } | null = null
+  let fenced = false
+  let lineStart = 0
+  const push = () => {
+    if (current) blocks.push({ ...current, source: normalize(body.slice(current.start, current.end)) })
+    current = null
+  }
+  while (lineStart <= body.length) {
+    const newline = body.indexOf('\n', lineStart)
+    const lineEnd = newline === -1 ? body.length : newline
+    const line = body.slice(lineStart, lineEnd)
+    if (line.trimStart().startsWith('```')) fenced = !fenced
+    if (!fenced && line.trim() === '') push()
+    else {
+      const end = line.endsWith('\r') ? lineEnd - 1 : lineEnd
+      if (current) current.end = end
+      else current = { start: lineStart, end }
+    }
+    if (newline === -1) break
+    lineStart = newline + 1
+  }
+  push()
+  return blocks
+}
+
+/** The body's block sources (`docBodyLayout(body).map(source)`). */
+export const docBodySources = (body: string) => docBodyLayout(body).map((block) => block.source)
+
+/**
+ * The body with one block edit applied (the `DocBlockEdit` of doc-tabs.ts: replace `span` blocks,
+ * insert with `span: 0`, or move with `moveTo`), or null when `before` no longer matches.
+ */
+export function applyDocBodyEdit(body: string, edit: DocBlockEdit): string | null {
+  const blocks = docBodyLayout(body)
+  const crlf = body.includes('\r\n')
+  const endings = (text: string) => (crlf ? text.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n') : text)
+  const after = normalize(edit.after).replace(/^\n+|\n+$/g, '')
+  const span = edit.span ?? 1
+  if (span === 0) {
+    if (edit.before !== '' || edit.index < 0 || edit.index > blocks.length) return null
+    return after ? insertRaw(body, blocks, edit.index, endings(after), crlf) : body
+  }
+  const ranges = blocks.slice(edit.index, edit.index + span)
+  if (edit.index < 0 || span < 1 || ranges.length !== span) return null
+  if (ranges.map((range) => range.source).join('\n\n') !== edit.before) return null
+  if (edit.moveTo !== undefined) {
+    if (after !== edit.before) return null
+    const to = Math.max(0, Math.min(blocks.length - span, edit.moveTo))
+    if (to === edit.index) return body
+    const bytes = body.slice(ranges[0].start, ranges[span - 1].end)
+    const removed = removeRaw(body, blocks, edit.index, span)
+    return insertRaw(removed, docBodyLayout(removed), to, bytes, crlf)
+  }
+  if (after === edit.before) return body
+  const first = ranges[0]
+  const last = ranges[span - 1]
+  if (after) return body.slice(0, first.start) + endings(after) + body.slice(last.end)
+  return removeRaw(body, blocks, edit.index, span)
+}
+
+function removeRaw(body: string, blocks: DocBlockRange[], index: number, span: number): string {
+  const start = blocks[index].start
+  const end = blocks[index + span - 1].end
+  const previous = blocks[index - 1]
+  const next = blocks[index + span]
+  if (previous) return body.slice(0, previous.end) + body.slice(end)
+  if (next) return body.slice(0, start) + body.slice(next.start)
+  return body.slice(0, start) + body.slice(end)
+}
+
+function insertRaw(body: string, blocks: DocBlockRange[], index: number, text: string, crlf: boolean): string {
+  const gap = crlf ? '\r\n\r\n' : '\n\n'
+  if (index < blocks.length) {
+    const at = blocks[index].start
+    return body.slice(0, at) + text + gap + body.slice(at)
+  }
+  if (blocks.length) {
+    const at = blocks[blocks.length - 1].end
+    return body.slice(0, at) + gap + text + body.slice(at)
+  }
+  const bom = body.startsWith('﻿') ? '﻿' : ''
+  return bom + text + (body.includes('\n') ? (crlf ? '\r\n' : '\n') : '')
+}
+
+/**
+ * A short, stable fingerprint of a block's source (FNV-1a, base 36): what the agent's outline
+ * shows beside each block and what a block op's `hash` guard compares, so an edit refuses when
+ * the block changed since it was read.
+ */
+export function docBlockHash(source: string): string {
+  let hash = 0x811c9dc5
+  for (let i = 0; i < source.length; i++) {
+    hash ^= source.charCodeAt(i)
+    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193) >>> 0
+  }
+  return hash.toString(36).padStart(7, '0').slice(-7)
+}
+
+export type DocBlockKind =
+  | 'heading'
+  | 'paragraph'
+  | 'list'
+  | 'tasks'
+  | 'quote'
+  | 'callout'
+  | 'table'
+  | 'code'
+  | 'chart'
+  | 'image'
+  | 'divider'
+  | 'toc'
+  | 'footnote'
+  | 'tab'
+  | 'pending'
+  | 'meta'
+
+/** What a block is, for the agent's outline. */
+export function docBlockKind(source: string): DocBlockKind {
+  const first = source.split('\n')[0] ?? ''
+  if (/^ {0,3}\[\/\/\]:[ \t]*#[ \t]+["(]tab:/.test(first)) return 'tab'
+  if (/^ {0,3}\[\/\/\]:[ \t]*#[ \t]+"toc"/.test(first)) return 'toc'
+  if (/^ {0,3}\[\/\/\]:[ \t]*#[ \t]+"pending:/.test(first)) return 'pending'
+  if (/^ {0,3}\[\/\/\]:/.test(first)) return 'meta'
+  if (/^#{1,6}\s/.test(first)) return 'heading'
+  if (/^\s*```\s*chart\s*$/.test(first)) return 'chart'
+  if (/^\s*(```|~~~)/.test(first) || /^( {4}|\t)/.test(first)) return 'code'
+  if (/^>\s*\[!(NOTE|TIP|IMPORTANT|WARNING|CAUTION)\]/i.test(first)) return 'callout'
+  if (/^>/.test(first)) return 'quote'
+  if (/^(-{3,}|\*{3,}|_{3,})\s*$/.test(source)) return 'divider'
+  if (/^\[\^[^\]]+\]:/.test(first)) return 'footnote'
+  if (/^\s*!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)\s*$/.test(source)) return 'image'
+  if (/^\s*([-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+\[[ xX]\]/.test(first)) return 'tasks'
+  if (/^\s*([-*+]|\d+[.)])\s/.test(first)) return 'list'
+  if (source.split('\n').some((line, index) => index > 0 && /^\s*\|?\s*:?-+:?\s*(\|\s*:?-+:?\s*)*\|?\s*$/.test(line) && line.includes('-')) && first.includes('|'))
+    return 'table'
+  return 'paragraph'
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-pages.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-pages.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..2bbdb8404
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-pages.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
+import { DESIGN_LIMITS, type Design, type DesignBoard, type DesignNote, type DesignPage } from './design'
+import { BOARD_GAP, allIds, freshId } from './design-tree'
+import { detachInstancesOf, mainsInSubtree, removeBoardDetaching } from './design-components'
+
+/**
+ * Pages: each page is its own canvas of boards and notes. A design without `pages` (every v1
+ * body) has ONE implicit page, and a board or note without `page` sits on the first page, so
+ * old bodies keep working untouched. These helpers are the only place that fallback lives.
+ * Whenever they rewrite `pages` they stamp every board and note with its page first, so a
+ * reorder or removal never silently moves a page-less board onto another page.
+ */
+
+/** The id the implicit page of a page-less design goes by in the editor (never stored). */
+export const IMPLICIT_PAGE_ID = 'page-1'
+
+export function pagesOf(design: Design): DesignPage[] {
+  return design.pages && design.pages.length > 0 ? design.pages : [{ id: IMPLICIT_PAGE_ID, title: 'Page 1' }]
+}
+
+/** The page a board or note sits on. */
+export function pageOf(design: Design, item: { page?: string }): string {
+  const pages = pagesOf(design)
+  if (item.page && pages.some((page) => page.id === item.page)) return item.page
+  return pages[0].id
+}
+
+export function boardsOnPage(design: Design, pageId: string): DesignBoard[] {
+  return design.boards.filter((board) => pageOf(design, board) === pageId)
+}
+
+export function notesOnPage(design: Design, pageId: string): DesignNote[] {
+  return (design.notes ?? []).filter((note) => pageOf(design, note) === pageId)
+}
+
+/** The design as one page sees it (the canvas draws only these boards and notes). */
+export function pageView(design: Design, pageId: string): Design {
+  if (!design.pages) return design
+  return { ...design, boards: boardsOnPage(design, pageId), notes: notesOnPage(design, pageId) }
+}
+
+/** Pages declared explicitly, with every board and note stamped with its page. */
+function materialise(design: Design): Design {
+  const taken = allIds(design)
+  const pages = design.pages && design.pages.length > 0 ? design.pages : [{ id: freshId(taken, IMPLICIT_PAGE_ID), title: 'Page 1' }]
+  const first = pages[0].id
+  const ids = new Set(pages.map((page) => page.id))
+  const stamp = <T extends { page?: string }>(item: T): T => (item.page && ids.has(item.page) ? item : { ...item, page: first })
+  return {
+    ...design,
+    pages,
+    boards: design.boards.map(stamp),
+    ...(design.notes ? { notes: design.notes.map(stamp) } : {}),
+  }
+}
+
+/** Adds a page after `after` (default: the end); returns its id, or null at the page limit. */
+export function addPage(
+  design: Design,
+  title?: string,
+  options: { id?: string; after?: string } = {},
+): { design: Design; pageId: string | null } {
+  const base = materialise(design)
+  const pages = base.pages!
+  if (pages.length >= DESIGN_LIMITS.maxPages) return { design, pageId: null }
+  const taken = allIds(base)
+  if (options.id !== undefined && taken.has(options.id)) return { design, pageId: null }
+  const pageId = options.id ?? freshId(taken, `page-${pages.length + 1}`)
+  const page: DesignPage = { id: pageId, title: (title?.trim() || `Page ${pages.length + 1}`).slice(0, 80) }
+  const at = options.after === undefined ? pages.length : pages.findIndex((entry) => entry.id === options.after) + 1
+  const next = [...pages]
+  next.splice(at <= 0 ? pages.length : at, 0, page)
+  return { design: { ...base, pages: next }, pageId }
+}
+
+export function renamePage(design: Design, pageId: string, title: string): Design {
+  const trimmed = title.trim().slice(0, 80)
+  if (!trimmed) return design
+  const base = materialise(design)
+  const pages = base.pages!
+  const page = pages.find((entry) => entry.id === pageId) ?? (pageId === IMPLICIT_PAGE_ID && !design.pages ? pages[0] : undefined)
+  if (!page || page.title === trimmed) return design
+  return { ...base, pages: pages.map((entry) => (entry === page ? { ...entry, title: trimmed } : entry)) }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Removes a page with its boards and notes (links into them are dropped and instances of main
+ * components on them are detached, so nothing dangles). The last page cannot be removed.
+ */
+export function removePage(design: Design, pageId: string): Design {
+  const pages = pagesOf(design)
+  if (pages.length <= 1 || !pages.some((page) => page.id === pageId)) return design
+  let next = materialise(design)
+  const doomed = next.boards.filter((board) => board.page === pageId)
+  const mains = doomed.flatMap((board) => mainsInSubtree(board.root))
+  next = detachInstancesOf(next, mains)
+  for (const board of doomed) next = removeBoardDetaching(next, board.id)
+  return {
+    ...next,
+    pages: next.pages!.filter((page) => page.id !== pageId),
+    ...(next.notes ? { notes: next.notes.filter((note) => note.page !== pageId) } : {}),
+  }
+}
+
+/** Moves a page to `index` in the page list. */
+export function movePage(design: Design, pageId: string, index: number): Design {
+  if (!design.pages) return design
+  const from = design.pages.findIndex((page) => page.id === pageId)
+  const to = Math.max(0, Math.min(design.pages.length - 1, index))
+  if (from === -1 || from === to) return design
+  const base = materialise(design)
+  const pages = [...base.pages!]
+  const [page] = pages.splice(from, 1)
+  pages.splice(to, 0, page)
+  return { ...base, pages }
+}
+
+/** Where a new board goes on a page: right of that page's rightmost board on its top row. */
+export function placeOnPage(design: Design, pageId: string, gap = BOARD_GAP): { x: number; y: number } {
+  const boards = boardsOnPage(design, pageId)
+  if (boards.length === 0) return { x: 0, y: 0 }
+  const top = Math.min(...boards.map((board) => board.y))
+  const row = boards.filter((board) => board.y < top + 200)
+  const right = Math.max(...row.map((board) => board.x + board.w))
+  return { x: Math.round(right + gap), y: Math.round(top) }
+}
+
+/** Moves a board to another page, placed in free space there. */
+export function moveBoardToPage(design: Design, boardId: string, pageId: string): Design {
+  const board = design.boards.find((entry) => entry.id === boardId)
+  if (!board || !pagesOf(design).some((page) => page.id === pageId) || pageOf(design, board) === pageId) return design
+  const base = materialise(design)
+  const at = placeOnPage(base, pageId)
+  return { ...base, boards: base.boards.map((entry) => (entry.id === boardId ? { ...entry, page: pageId, ...at } : entry)) }
+}
+
+/** The page to show after an edit: `current` when it still exists, else the first page. */
+export function reconcilePage(design: Design, current: string | null): string {
+  const pages = pagesOf(design)
+  return current && pages.some((page) => page.id === current) ? current : pages[0].id
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-prototype.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-prototype.ts
new file mode 100644

+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-prototype.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,154 @@
+import type { Design, DesignBoard, DesignNode, FrameNode, Interaction, Transition, VariableValue } from './design'
+import { templateId } from './design-logic'
+import { variantName } from './design-variants'
+
+/**
+ * A prototype's state in Present and on a shared link: what the viewer has typed, toggled and
+ * clicked. It lives only in the viewer (never saved) and resets when Present closes. Clicks run a
+ * node's `interactions` in order: go to a board, go back, open or close an overlay board, toggle
+ * / show / hide a node, swap an instance to a variant, set a variable (which `showIf` and `bind`
+ * read). Inputs are real controls here and write their value (and a bound variable). Pure.
+ */
+
+export type PrototypeState = {
+  vars: Record<string, VariableValue>
+  /** Node (template) id → hidden, from toggle / show / hide. */
+  hidden: Record<string, boolean>
+  /** Instance id → the main it draws now (setVariant). */
+  variants: Record<string, string>
+  /** Drawn input id → what the viewer entered. */
+  inputs: Record<string, { value?: string; checked?: boolean }>
+  /** The board open as an overlay over the current one. */
+  overlay: string | null
+  /** Boards visited by navigate, for back. */
+  trail: string[]
+}
+
+export type PrototypeEffect =
+  | { kind: 'navigate'; boardId: string; transition?: Transition }
+  | { kind: 'back'; boardId: string }
+  | null
+
+export function initialPrototype(design: Design): PrototypeState {
+  const vars: Record<string, VariableValue> = {}
+  for (const variable of design.variables ?? []) vars[variable.id] = variable.value
+  return { vars, hidden: {}, variants: {}, inputs: {}, overlay: null, trail: [] }
+}
+
+/**
+ * The state after a click on a node with these interactions, plus a navigation the host performs
+ * (`currentBoardId` is where the viewer is, for back). Unknown targets are skipped, never thrown.
+ */
+export function runInteractions(
+  design: Design,
+  state: PrototypeState,
+  interactions: readonly Interaction[],
+  currentBoardId: string,
+  hiddenNow: (nodeId: string) => boolean,
+): { state: PrototypeState; effect: PrototypeEffect } {
+  let next = state
+  let effect: PrototypeEffect = null
+  const boards = new Set(design.boards.map((board) => board.id))
+  for (const interaction of interactions) {
+    const target = interaction.target
+    switch (interaction.action) {
+      case 'navigate':
+        if (target && boards.has(target)) {
+          next = { ...next, overlay: null, trail: [...next.trail, currentBoardId].slice(-40) }
+          effect = { kind: 'navigate', boardId: target, transition: interaction.transition }
+        }
+        break
+      case 'back': {
+        if (next.overlay) {
+          next = { ...next, overlay: null }
+          break
+        }
+        const previous = next.trail.at(-1)
+        if (previous) {
+          next = { ...next, trail: next.trail.slice(0, -1) }
+          effect = { kind: 'back', boardId: previous }
+        }
+        break
+      }
+      case 'overlay':
+        if (target && boards.has(target)) next = { ...next, overlay: target }
+        break
+      case 'closeOverlay':
+        next = { ...next, overlay: null }
+        break
+      case 'toggle':
+        if (target) next = { ...next, hidden: { ...next.hidden, [target]: !(next.hidden[target] ?? hiddenNow(target)) } }
+        break
+      case 'show':
+        if (target) next = { ...next, hidden: { ...next.hidden, [target]: false } }
+        break
+      case 'hide':
+        if (target) next = { ...next, hidden: { ...next.hidden, [target]: true } }
+        break
+      case 'setVariant':
+        if (target && typeof interaction.value === 'string') next = { ...next, variants: { ...next.variants, [target]: interaction.value } }
+        break
+      case 'setVariable': {
+        if (!target) break
+        const variable = design.variables?.find((entry) => entry.id === target)
+        if (!variable) break
+        const value = interaction.value ?? (variable.type === 'boolean' ? !next.vars[target] : next.vars[target])
+        next = { ...next, vars: { ...next.vars, [target]: value } }
+        break
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return { state: next, effect }
+}
+
+function applyNode(node: DesignNode, state: PrototypeState): DesignNode {
+  const key = templateId(node.id)
+  let out = node
+  const hidden = state.hidden[key] ?? state.hidden[node.id]
+  if (hidden !== undefined && hidden !== Boolean(node.hidden)) out = { ...out, hidden: hidden || undefined }
+  if (out.type === 'frame') {
+    const variant = state.variants[key] ?? state.variants[node.id]
+    if (variant && out.instanceOf !== undefined) out = { ...out, instanceOf: variant }
+    let changed = false
+    const children = out.children.map((child) => {
+      const next = applyNode(child, state)
+      if (next !== child) changed = true
+      return next
+    })
+    if (changed) out = { ...out, children }
+  } else if (out.type === 'input') {
+    const entered = state.inputs[node.id]
+    if (entered) out = { ...out, ...(entered.value !== undefined ? { value: entered.value } : {}), ...(entered.checked !== undefined ? { checked: entered.checked } : {}) }
+  }
+  return out
+}
+
+/** A board as the prototype shows it now (toggles, variant swaps, entered values). */
+export function applyPrototype(board: DesignBoard, state: PrototypeState): DesignBoard {
+  if (Object.keys(state.hidden).length === 0 && Object.keys(state.variants).length === 0 && Object.keys(state.inputs).length === 0) return board
+  const root = applyNode(board.root, state) as FrameNode
+  return root === board.root ? board : { ...board, root }
+}
+
+export type InteractionState = 'hover' | 'pressed'
+
+/**
+ * The variant an instance shows while hovered or pressed: in its main's variant group, the main
+ * named "<Group> / <Variant> Hover" (or "… Pressed"), else "<Group> / Hover" when the main is
+ * the group's default. Null when the group has none (the instance just stays as is).
+ */
+export function stateVariant(mains: ReadonlyMap<string, FrameNode>, mainId: string, which: InteractionState): string | null {
+  const main = mains.get(mainId)
+  if (!main) return null
+  const { group, variant } = variantName(main.name)
+  if (variant === null) return null
+  const suffix = which === 'hover' ? 'hover' : 'pressed'
+  const wanted = new Set([`${variant} ${suffix}`.toLowerCase(), `${variant}:${suffix}`.toLowerCase(), `${variant} / ${suffix}`.toLowerCase()])
+  if (variant.toLowerCase() === 'default') wanted.add(suffix)
+  for (const other of mains.values()) {
+    if (other.id === mainId) continue
+    const named = variantName(other.name)
+    if (named.variant !== null && named.group.toLowerCase() === group.toLowerCase() && wanted.has(named.variant.toLowerCase())) return other.id
+  }
+  return null
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-protection-invariant.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/sheet-protection-invariant.ts
new file mode 100644
