+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/whiteboard-snap.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,218 @@
+/**
+ * Smart guides: while a selection is dragged or resized, its edges and centre snap to the edges
+ * and centres of the other elements, and to equal spacing with its neighbours in a row or
+ * column. Returns the corrected offset (or box) and the guides to draw. Pure: no DOM.
+ */
+
+import type { Handle } from './whiteboard-geometry'
+import type { Rect } from './whiteboard'
+
+/** A guide line in world units: `axis: "x"` is a vertical line at x = `at` from y = `from` to `to`. */
+export type LineGuide = { kind: 'line'; axis: 'x' | 'y'; at: number; from: number; to: number }
+/**
+ * Equal gaps: each span is one gap of `size` along `axis`, drawn at the cross position `at`
+ * (for `axis: "x"`, a horizontal span from `from` to `to` at y = `at`).
+ */
+export type GapGuide = { kind: 'gap'; axis: 'x' | 'y'; spans: Array<{ from: number; to: number; at: number }> }
+export type SnapGuide = LineGuide | GapGuide
+
+type AxisSnap = { delta: number; guides: SnapGuide[] }
+
+const lo = (r: Rect, axis: 'x' | 'y') => (axis === 'x' ? r.x : r.y)
+const len = (r: Rect, axis: 'x' | 'y') => (axis === 'x' ? r.w : r.h)
+const hi = (r: Rect, axis: 'x' | 'y') => lo(r, axis) + len(r, axis)
+const mid = (r: Rect, axis: 'x' | 'y') => lo(r, axis) + len(r, axis) / 2
+const other = (axis: 'x' | 'y'): 'x' | 'y' => (axis === 'x' ? 'y' : 'x')
+
+/** Whether two boxes overlap across `axis` (share some of the other axis): row/column neighbours. */
+const alongside = (a: Rect, b: Rect, axis: 'x' | 'y') => {
+  const o = other(axis)
+  return lo(a, o) < hi(b, o) && lo(b, o) < hi(a, o)
+}
+
+/**
+ * Edge and centre alignment on one axis. `points` are the moving box's own lines that may snap
+ * (left/centre/right for a move; only the dragged edge for a resize).
+ */
+function alignAxis(
+  moving: Rect,
+  points: ReadonlyArray<number>,
+  targets: readonly Rect[],
+  axis: 'x' | 'y',
+  threshold: number,
+): AxisSnap | null {
+  let best: number | null = null
+  for (const target of targets) {
+    for (const line of [lo(target, axis), mid(target, axis), hi(target, axis)]) {
+      for (const p of points) {
+        const delta = line - p
+        if (Math.abs(delta) <= threshold && (best === null || Math.abs(delta) < Math.abs(best))) best = delta
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  if (best === null) return null
+  const delta = best
+  // Every line that matches after the snap gets a guide, spanning the boxes on it.
+  const o = other(axis)
+  const snapped = points.map((p) => p + delta)
+  const guides: LineGuide[] = []
+  const seen = new Set<number>()
+  for (const at of snapped) {
+    const key = Math.round(at * 10)
+    if (seen.has(key)) continue
+    const on = targets.filter((t) => [lo(t, axis), mid(t, axis), hi(t, axis)].some((line) => Math.abs(line - at) < 0.5))
+    if (on.length === 0) continue
+    seen.add(key)
+    const shifted = axis === 'x' ? { ...moving, x: moving.x + delta } : { ...moving, y: moving.y + delta }
+    const spans = [shifted, ...on]
+    guides.push({
+      kind: 'line',
+      axis,
+      at,
+      from: Math.min(...spans.map((r) => lo(r, o))),
+      to: Math.max(...spans.map((r) => hi(r, o))),
+    })
+  }
+  return { delta, guides }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Equal spacing on one axis, among the boxes in the moving box's row (or column): the box can sit
+ * the same gap after its neighbour as that neighbour sits after the one before, the same gap
+ * before, or centred between the neighbours on either side.
+ */
+function spacingAxis(moving: Rect, targets: readonly Rect[], axis: 'x' | 'y', threshold: number): AxisSnap | null {
+  const row = targets.filter((t) => alongside(t, moving, axis)).sort((a, b) => lo(a, axis) - lo(b, axis))
+  if (row.length === 0) return null
+  const o = other(axis)
+  const before = row.filter((t) => hi(t, axis) <= lo(moving, axis) + threshold)
+  const after = row.filter((t) => lo(t, axis) >= hi(moving, axis) - threshold)
+  const prev = before.length ? before.reduce((a, b) => (hi(b, axis) > hi(a, axis) ? b : a)) : null
+  const next = after.length ? after.reduce((a, b) => (lo(b, axis) < lo(a, axis) ? b : a)) : null
+  type Option = { delta: number; spans: Array<[Rect, Rect]> }
+  const options: Option[] = []
+  const gapAfter = (a: Rect, b: Rect) => lo(b, axis) - hi(a, axis)
+  const neighbourBefore = (r: Rect) =>
+    row.filter((t) => t !== r && hi(t, axis) <= lo(r, axis)).reduce<Rect | null>((a, b) => (!a || hi(b, axis) > hi(a, axis) ? b : a), null)
+  const neighbourAfter = (r: Rect) =>
+    row.filter((t) => t !== r && lo(t, axis) >= hi(r, axis)).reduce<Rect | null>((a, b) => (!a || lo(b, axis) < lo(a, axis) ? b : a), null)
+  if (prev) {
+    const pp = neighbourBefore(prev)
+    if (pp && gapAfter(pp, prev) > 0) {
+      const target = hi(prev, axis) + gapAfter(pp, prev)
+      options.push({ delta: target - lo(moving, axis), spans: [[pp, prev], [prev, moving]] })
+    }
+  }
+  if (next) {
+    const nn = neighbourAfter(next)
+    if (nn && gapAfter(next, nn) > 0) {
+      const target = lo(next, axis) - gapAfter(next, nn) - len(moving, axis)
+      options.push({ delta: target - lo(moving, axis), spans: [[moving, next], [next, nn]] })
+    }
+  }
+  if (prev && next && lo(next, axis) - hi(prev, axis) > len(moving, axis)) {
+    const target = (hi(prev, axis) + lo(next, axis) - len(moving, axis)) / 2
+    options.push({ delta: target - lo(moving, axis), spans: [[prev, moving], [moving, next]] })
+  }
+  const best = options
+    .filter((option) => Math.abs(option.delta) <= threshold)
+    .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(a.delta) - Math.abs(b.delta))[0]
+  if (!best) return null
+  const shifted = axis === 'x' ? { ...moving, x: moving.x + best.delta } : { ...moving, y: moving.y + best.delta }
+  const fix = (r: Rect) => (r === moving ? shifted : r)
+  return {
+    delta: best.delta,
+    guides: [
+      {
+        kind: 'gap',
+        axis,
+        spans: best.spans.map(([a, b]) => {
+          const ra = fix(a)
+          const rb = fix(b)
+          const top = Math.max(lo(ra, o), lo(rb, o))
+          const bottom = Math.min(hi(ra, o), hi(rb, o))
+          return { from: hi(ra, axis), to: lo(rb, axis), at: (top + bottom) / 2 }
+        }),
+      },
+    ],
+  }
+}
+
+function pick(a: AxisSnap | null, b: AxisSnap | null): AxisSnap | null {
+  if (!a) return b
+  if (!b) return a
+  if (Math.abs(a.delta - b.delta) < 0.5) return { delta: a.delta, guides: [...a.guides, ...b.guides] }
+  return Math.abs(a.delta) <= Math.abs(b.delta) ? a : b
+}
+
+/**
+ * Snaps a box being moved. `moving` is where the drag would put the selection's bounds;
+ * `targets` are the other elements' bounds (collected once, at the start of the drag).
+ * `threshold` is in world units (a few screen pixels over the zoom).
+ */
+export function snapMove(
+  moving: Rect,
+  targets: readonly Rect[],
+  threshold: number,
+): { dx: number; dy: number; guides: SnapGuide[] } {
+  const near = targets.filter((t) => Math.abs(t.x - moving.x) < 4000 && Math.abs(t.y - moving.y) < 4000)
+  const x = pick(
+    alignAxis(moving, [moving.x, moving.x + moving.w / 2, moving.x + moving.w], near, 'x', threshold),
+    spacingAxis(moving, near, 'x', threshold),
+  )
+  const y = pick(
+    alignAxis(moving, [moving.y, moving.y + moving.h / 2, moving.y + moving.h], near, 'y', threshold),
+    spacingAxis(moving, near, 'y', threshold),
+  )
+  const dx = x?.delta ?? 0
+  const dy = y?.delta ?? 0
+  // Guides are recomputed for the final box, so a line drawn on one axis spans the moved box.
+  const final = { ...moving, x: moving.x + dx, y: moving.y + dy }
+  const guides = [
+    ...(x ? (alignAxis(final, [final.x, final.x + final.w / 2, final.x + final.w], near, 'x', 0.5)?.guides ?? []) : []),
+    ...(x ? x.guides.filter((g) => g.kind === 'gap') : []),
+    ...(y ? (alignAxis(final, [final.y, final.y + final.h / 2, final.y + final.h], near, 'y', 0.5)?.guides ?? []) : []),
+    ...(y ? y.guides.filter((g) => g.kind === 'gap') : []),
+  ]
+  return { dx, dy, guides }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Snaps the edges a resize handle drags (the opposite edges stay put) to other elements' edges
+ * and centres. Returns the corrected box and its guides.
+ */
+export function snapResize(
+  rect: Rect,
+  handle: Handle,
+  targets: readonly Rect[],
+  threshold: number,
+): { rect: Rect; guides: SnapGuide[] } {
+  const near = targets.filter((t) => Math.abs(t.x - rect.x) < 4000 && Math.abs(t.y - rect.y) < 4000)
+  let { x, y, w, h } = rect
+  const guides: SnapGuide[] = []
+  if (handle.includes('e') || handle.includes('w')) {
+    const west = handle.includes('w')
+    const edge = west ? x : x + w
+    const snap = alignAxis(rect, [edge], near, 'x', threshold)
+    if (snap && (west ? w - snap.delta : w + snap.delta) >= 16) {
+      if (west) {
+        x += snap.delta
+        w -= snap.delta
+      } else w += snap.delta
+      guides.push(...snap.guides)
+    }
+  }
+  if (handle.includes('n') || handle.includes('s')) {
+    const north = handle.includes('n')
+    const edge = north ? y : y + h
+    const snap = alignAxis({ x, y, w, h }, [edge], near, 'y', threshold)
+    if (snap && (north ? h - snap.delta : h + snap.delta) >= 16) {
+      if (north) {
+        y += snap.delta
+        h -= snap.delta
+      } else h += snap.delta
+      guides.push(...snap.guides)
+    }
+  }
+  return { rect: { x, y, w, h }, guides }
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/whiteboard-richtext.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/whiteboard-richtext.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..4511819e5

+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/whiteboard-richtext.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,277 @@
+/**
+ * Rich text in notes and shapes, kept as plain `text` with three light marks, so an agent and an
+ * old reader both see readable words:
+ *   **bold**   *italic* (or _italic_)   a line starting with "- " (or "• ") is a bullet.
+ * A mark only counts when it hugs a word (`2 * 3` stays as typed); an unclosed mark is literal.
+ * This file parses the marks into runs, wraps runs into lines, fits them in a box, and edits
+ * marks around a textarea selection. Pure: no DOM.
+ */
+
+import type { Measure } from './whiteboard-geometry'
+import { LINE_HEIGHT } from './whiteboard'
+
+export type Run = { text: string; bold?: boolean; italic?: boolean }
+export type RichLine = { runs: Run[]; bullet: boolean; /** The first visual line of its bullet. */ lead: boolean }
+export type RichBlock = { lines: RichLine[]; fontSize: number; lineHeight: number; hasBullets: boolean }
+
+/** Bold type is drawn a little wider than the measure (measured at regular weight). */
+export const BOLD_WIDTH = 1.06
+
+const BULLET = /^\s*(?:[-*•])\s+/
+
+/** Whether a paragraph is a bullet, and its text without the marker. */
+export function splitBullet(paragraph: string): { bullet: boolean; body: string } {
+  const match = BULLET.exec(paragraph)
+  // "* " starting a line is a bullet only when it is not the start of an italic run.
+  if (match && !/^\s*\*\S/.test(paragraph)) return { bullet: true, body: paragraph.slice(match[0].length) }
+  return { bullet: false, body: paragraph }
+}
+
+/** The runs of one paragraph (no newlines). */
+export function parseInline(text: string): Run[] {
+  const runs: Run[] = []
+  let bold = false
+  let italic: '*' | '_' | null = null
+  let buffer = ''
+  const flush = () => {
+    if (!buffer) return
+    const run: Run = { text: buffer }
+    if (bold) run.bold = true
+    if (italic) run.italic = true
+    const last = runs[runs.length - 1]
+    if (last && !!last.bold === !!run.bold && !!last.italic === !!run.italic) last.text += buffer
+    else runs.push(run)
+    buffer = ''
+  }
+  // A closing mark must exist ahead for an opening one to count.
+  const closes = (from: number, mark: string) => {
+    for (let j = from; j <= text.length - mark.length; j++) {
+      if (text.startsWith(mark, j) && j > from && !/\s/.test(text[j - 1])) {
+        if (mark === '*' && text.startsWith('**', j) && !text.startsWith('***', j)) {
+          j++
+          continue
+        }
+        return true
+      }
+    }
+    return false
+  }
+  let i = 0
+  while (i < text.length) {
+    const prev = i > 0 ? text[i - 1] : ' '
+    if (text.startsWith('**', i)) {
+      const next = text[i + 2] ?? ' '
+      if (bold && !/\s/.test(prev)) {
+        flush()
+        bold = false
+        i += 2
+        continue
+      }
+      // An opener starts a word: `a**b**` mid-word stays as typed.
+      if (!bold && !/\s/.test(next) && !/\w/.test(prev) && closes(i + 2, '**')) {
+        flush()
+        bold = true
+        i += 2
+        continue
+      }
+    }
+    const ch = text[i]
+    if (ch === '*' || ch === '_') {
+      const next = text[i + 1] ?? ' '
+      if (italic === ch && !/\s/.test(prev)) {
+        flush()
+        italic = null
+        i += 1
+        continue
+      }
+      // A mark inside a word (snake_case, 2*3*4) is literal.
+      const wordy = /\w/.test(prev)
+      if (!italic && !wordy && !/\s/.test(next) && closes(i + 1, ch)) {
+        flush()
+        italic = ch
+        i += 1
+        continue
+      }
+    }
+    buffer += ch
+    i += 1
+  }
+  flush()
+  return runs
+}
+
+/** The text without its marks (for search, summaries and the agent). */
+export function plainRichText(text: string): string {
+  return text
+    .split('\n')
+    .map((paragraph) => {
+      const { bullet, body } = splitBullet(paragraph)
+      const words = parseInline(body)
+        .map((run) => run.text)
+        .join('')
+      return bullet ? `• ${words}` : words
+    })
+    .join('\n')
+}
+
+/** Whether `text` uses any mark (so a plain note keeps the plain, centred layout). */
+export function hasRichMarks(text: string): boolean {
+  return text.split('\n').some((paragraph) => {
+    const { bullet, body } = splitBullet(paragraph)
+    return bullet || parseInline(body).some((run) => run.bold || run.italic)
+  })
+}
+
+const runWidth = (run: Run, fontSize: number, measure: Measure) =>
+  measure(run.text, fontSize) * (run.bold ? BOLD_WIDTH : 1)
+
+/**
+ * Wraps rich text to `maxWidth`. Bullet lines (and their wrapped continuations) are indented by
+ * `indent`; a word wider than a line is cut.
+ */
+export function layoutRichText(
+  text: string,
+  fontSize: number,
+  maxWidth: number,
+  measure: Measure,
+): RichLine[] {
+  const indent = fontSize * 1.1
+  const out: RichLine[] = []
+  for (const paragraph of text.split('\n')) {
+    const { bullet, body } = splitBullet(paragraph)
+    const width = Math.max(1, maxWidth - (bullet ? indent : 0))
+    // Tokens: words and the spaces between them, each keeping its run's style.
+    const tokens: Run[] = []
+    for (const run of parseInline(body)) {
+      for (const piece of run.text.split(/(\s+)/)) {
+        if (piece) tokens.push({ ...run, text: piece })
+      }
+    }
+    let line: Run[] = []
+    let used = 0
+    let lead = true
+    const push = () => {
+      // Trailing spaces never count toward a line.
+      while (line.length && /^\s+$/.test(line[line.length - 1].text)) line.pop()
+      out.push({ runs: merge(line), bullet, lead })
+      lead = false
+      line = []
+      used = 0
+    }
+    for (const token of tokens) {
+      const space = /^\s+$/.test(token.text)
+      if (space && line.length === 0) continue
+      let w = runWidth(token, fontSize, measure)
+      if (used + w <= width || space) {
+        line.push(token)
+        used += w
+        continue
+      }
+      if (line.length) push()
+      let piece = token
+      while (runWidth(piece, fontSize, measure) > width && piece.text.length > 1) {
+        let cut = piece.text.length - 1
+        while (cut > 1 && runWidth({ ...piece, text: piece.text.slice(0, cut) }, fontSize, measure) > width) cut--
+        line.push({ ...piece, text: piece.text.slice(0, cut) })
+        push()
+        piece = { ...piece, text: piece.text.slice(cut) }
+      }
+      w = runWidth(piece, fontSize, measure)
+      line.push(piece)
+      used = w
+    }
+    push()
+  }
+  return out
+}
+
+function merge(runs: Run[]): Run[] {
+  const out: Run[] = []
+  for (const run of runs) {
+    const last = out[out.length - 1]
+    if (last && !!last.bold === !!run.bold && !!last.italic === !!run.italic) out[out.length - 1] = { ...last, text: last.text + run.text }
+    else out.push({ ...run })
+  }
+  return out
+}
+
+/**
+ * The largest of `steps` at which the text fits `width × height` (the smallest overflows and is
+ * clipped when nothing fits).
+ */
+export function fitRichText(
+  text: string,
+  width: number,
+  height: number,
+  measure: Measure,
+  steps: readonly number[],
+): RichBlock {
+  const hasBullets = text.split('\n').some((p) => splitBullet(p).bullet)
+  for (const fontSize of steps) {
+    const lines = layoutRichText(text, fontSize, width, measure)
+    const lineHeight = fontSize * LINE_HEIGHT
+    if (lines.length * lineHeight <= height) return { lines, fontSize, lineHeight, hasBullets }
+  }
+  const fontSize = steps[steps.length - 1]
+  return { lines: layoutRichText(text, fontSize, width, measure), fontSize, lineHeight: fontSize * LINE_HEIGHT, hasBullets }
+}
+
+// ---- editing marks in a textarea
+
+export type TextSelection = { value: string; start: number; end: number }
+
+/**
+ * Wraps the selection in `mark` (`**` bold, `*` italic), or unwraps it when it is already
+ * wrapped. An empty selection inserts the pair with the caret between.
+ */
+export function toggleMark(sel: TextSelection, mark: '**' | '*'): TextSelection {
+  const { value } = sel
+  let { start, end } = sel
+  // Leave surrounding spaces outside the marks.
+  while (start < end && /\s/.test(value[start])) start++
+  while (end > start && /\s/.test(value[end - 1])) end--
+  const before = value.slice(0, start)
+  const inner = value.slice(start, end)
+  const after = value.slice(end)
+  // Stars hugging the selection: 1 or 3 is italic (3 is bold italic), 2 or 3 is bold.
+  const starsBefore = /\**$/.exec(before)![0].length
+  const starsAfter = /^\**/.exec(after)![0].length
+  const stars = Math.min(starsBefore, starsAfter)
+  const wrappedOutside = mark === '**' ? stars >= 2 : stars === 1 || stars === 3
+  if (wrappedOutside) {
+    return {
+      value: before.slice(0, -mark.length) + inner + after.slice(mark.length),
+      start: start - mark.length,
+      end: end - mark.length,
+    }
+  }
+  if (inner.length >= mark.length * 2 && inner.startsWith(mark) && inner.endsWith(mark)) {
+    const stripped = inner.slice(mark.length, inner.length - mark.length)
+    return { value: before + stripped + after, start, end: start + stripped.length }
+  }
+  return {
+    value: before + mark + inner + mark + after,
+    start: start + mark.length,
+    end: end + mark.length,
+  }
+}
+
+/** Turns the lines the selection touches into bullets, or back to plain lines when all are bullets. */
+export function toggleBullets(sel: TextSelection): TextSelection {
+  const { value, start, end } = sel
+  const lineStart = value.lastIndexOf('\n', start - 1) + 1
+  const nextBreak = value.indexOf('\n', Math.max(end, start) - (end > start && value[end - 1] === '\n' ? 1 : 0))
+  const lineEnd = nextBreak === -1 ? value.length : nextBreak
+  const block = value.slice(lineStart, lineEnd)
+  const lines = block.split('\n')
+  const all = lines.every((line) => splitBullet(line).bullet || !line.trim())
+  const next = lines
+    .map((line) => {
+      if (all) return line.replace(BULLET, '')
+      if (!line.trim() || splitBullet(line).bullet) return line
+      return `- ${line}`
+    })
+    .join('\n')
+  const replaced = value.slice(0, lineStart) + next + value.slice(lineEnd)
+  return { value: replaced, start: lineStart, end: lineStart + next.length }
+}

+import {
+  DESIGN_LIMITS,
+  HEX,
+  isSafeImageSrc,
+  type BindableKey,
+  type Design,
+  type DesignBoard,
+  type DesignDataList,
+  type DesignNode,
+  type DesignTokens,
+  type FrameNode,
+  type VariableValue,
+} from './design'
+import { INSTANCE_SEP, resolveInstance } from './design-components'
+
+/**
+ * Round 5's declarative logic, resolved before anything draws: design variables (the Props
+ * panel's levers), `showIf`, `bind`, `{{holes}}` in copy, and `repeat` frames over inline data
+ * lists. No code anywhere: every rule is data the schema validates and caps. A design without
+ * variables or data lists, and a node without these fields, comes out exactly as it went in.
+ *
+ *   {{id}}            a variable's value          {{item.field}}  the current repeat row's field
+ *   showIf            { variable, equals?, not? }  drawn only while it matches
+ *   bind              { text|label|src|alt|fill|color|checked|value|placeholder: "<var id>" | "item.<field>" }
+ *   repeat (frame)    { data, limit? }             the children once per row; row 0 keeps the
+ *                     template's ids, row i suffixes every id in its copy with `~r<i>`, so a click
+ *                     on any copy selects the template (the editor's `~` id convention).
+ *
+ * Pure: no React, no DOM.
+ */
+
+export type LogicScope = {
+  vars: Readonly<Record<string, VariableValue>>
+  data: ReadonlyMap<string, DesignDataList>
+  tokens?: DesignTokens
+  /** The repeat row being drawn. */
+  item?: Readonly<Record<string, VariableValue>>
+  /** Main components, to draw instances inside a repeat with that row. */
+  mains?: ReadonlyMap<string, FrameNode>
+}
+
+export const REPEAT_SEP = `${INSTANCE_SEP}r`
+
+/** The design's variables at their stored values, with `overrides` (a prototype's state) winning. */
+export function logicScope(design: Design, overrides?: Readonly<Record<string, VariableValue>>, mains?: ReadonlyMap<string, FrameNode>): LogicScope {
+  const vars: Record<string, VariableValue> = {}
+  for (const variable of design.variables ?? []) vars[variable.id] = variable.value
+  if (overrides) for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(overrides)) if (Object.hasOwn(vars, key)) vars[key] = value
+  return { vars, data: new Map((design.data ?? []).map((list) => [list.id, list])), tokens: design.tokens, mains }
+}
+
+/** Whether the design uses any round-5 logic (a fast path: nothing to resolve otherwise). */
+export function hasLogic(design: Design): boolean {
+  return (design.variables?.length ?? 0) > 0 || (design.data?.length ?? 0) > 0
+}
+
+function lookup(ref: string, scope: LogicScope): VariableValue | undefined {
+  if (ref.startsWith('item.')) return scope.item?.[ref.slice(5)]
+  return Object.hasOwn(scope.vars, ref) ? scope.vars[ref] : undefined
+}
+
+const HOLE = /\{\{\s*((?:item\.)?[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63})\s*\}\}/g
+
+/** `{{name}}` holes filled from the scope; a name it does not know stays as written. */
+export function fillHoles(text: string, scope: LogicScope): string {
+  if (!text.includes('{{')) return text
+  return text.replace(HOLE, (whole, name: string) => {
+    const value = lookup(name, scope)
+    return value === undefined ? whole : String(value)
+  })
+}
+
+export function hasHoles(text: string | undefined): boolean {
+  if (!text) return false
+  HOLE.lastIndex = 0
+  return HOLE.test(text)
+}
+
+/** Whether a `showIf` lets the node draw. */
+export function showIfPasses(showIf: DesignNode['showIf'], scope: LogicScope): boolean {
+  if (!showIf) return true
+  const value = lookup(showIf.variable, scope)
+  const match = showIf.equals === undefined ? Boolean(value) : value === showIf.equals
+  return showIf.not ? !match : match
+}
+
+const TEXT_MAX: Partial<Record<BindableKey, number>> = { text: 2000, label: 80, alt: 300, value: 200, placeholder: 120 }
+
+function colorOk(value: string, tokens: DesignTokens | undefined) {
+  return HEX.test(value) || value === 'transparent' || Object.hasOwn(tokens?.colors ?? {}, value)
+}
+
+/** One node's showIf, bindings and holes resolved (its children untouched). */
+export function applyNodeLogic<T extends DesignNode>(node: T, scope: LogicScope): T {
+  let out: Record<string, unknown> | null = null
+  const set = (key: string, value: unknown) => {
+    out ??= { ...node }
+    out[key] = value
+  }
+  if (node.showIf && !showIfPasses(node.showIf, scope)) set('hidden', true)
+  for (const key of ['text', 'label', 'alt', 'value', 'placeholder'] as const) {
+    const current = (node as Record<string, unknown>)[key]
+    if (typeof current === 'string' && current.includes('{{')) set(key, fillHoles(current, scope).slice(0, TEXT_MAX[key]))
+  }
+  if (node.type === 'frame' && node.overrides) {
+    let changed = false
+    const overrides: NonNullable<FrameNode['overrides']> = {}
+    for (const [id, override] of Object.entries(node.overrides)) {
+      const next = { ...override }
+      for (const key of ['text', 'label', 'alt'] as const) {
+        const value = next[key]
+        if (typeof value === 'string' && value.includes('{{')) {
+          next[key] = fillHoles(value, scope).slice(0, TEXT_MAX[key])
+          changed = true
+        }
+      }
+      overrides[id] = next
+    }
+    if (changed) set('overrides', overrides)
+  }
+  for (const [key, ref] of Object.entries(node.bind ?? {}) as Array<[BindableKey, string | undefined]>) {
+    if (ref === undefined) continue
+    const value = lookup(ref, scope)
+    if (value === undefined) continue
+    if (key === 'checked') {
+      if (node.type === 'input') set('checked', Boolean(value))
+    } else if (key === 'src') {
+      if (node.type === 'image' && typeof value === 'string' && isSafeImageSrc(value)) {
+        set('src', value)
+        set('placeholder', undefined)
+      }
+    } else if (key === 'fill' || key === 'color') {
+      if (typeof value === 'string' && colorOk(value, scope.tokens) && key in fieldOwners && fieldOwners[key].includes(node.type)) set(key, value)
+    } else if (fieldOwners[key].includes(node.type)) {
+      set(key, String(value).slice(0, TEXT_MAX[key]))
+    }
+  }
+  if (!out) return node
+  const result = out as Record<string, unknown>
+  for (const key of Object.keys(result)) if (result[key] === undefined) delete result[key]
+  return result as T
+}
+
+/** Which node types carry each bindable field. */
+const fieldOwners: Record<BindableKey, ReadonlyArray<DesignNode['type']>> = {
+  text: ['text'],
+  label: ['button', 'input'],
+  src: ['image'],
+  alt: ['image'],
+  fill: ['frame', 'shape'],
+  color: ['text', 'icon', 'divider'],
+  checked: ['input'],
+  value: ['input'],
+  placeholder: ['input'],
+}
+
+function suffixIds(node: DesignNode, suffix: string): DesignNode {
+  if (!suffix) return node
+  const id = `${node.id}${suffix}`
+  if (node.type !== 'frame') return { ...node, id }
+  return { ...node, id, children: node.children.map((child) => suffixIds(child, suffix)) }
+}
+
+/**
+ * A subtree with its logic resolved: each node's showIf, bindings and holes, and every repeat
+ * expanded into one copy of its children per row (at most the list's rows and `limit`).
+ */
+export function expandLogicTree(node: DesignNode, scope: LogicScope, depth = 0): DesignNode {
+  const resolved = applyNodeLogic(node, scope)
+  if (resolved.type !== 'frame' || depth > DESIGN_LIMITS.maxDepth * 2) return resolved
+  // Inside a repeat, an instance draws now, with this row (holes in its main see `item`).
+  if (resolved.instanceOf !== undefined && scope.item && scope.mains) {
+    const drawn = resolveInstance(resolved, scope.mains)
+    return { ...drawn, instanceOf: undefined, children: drawn.children.map((child) => expandLogicTree(child, scope, depth + 1)) }
+  }
+  if (resolved.repeat && resolved.instanceOf === undefined) {
+    const list = scope.data.get(resolved.repeat.data)
+    const { repeat, ...frame } = resolved
+    if (!list) return { ...frame, children: resolved.children.map((child) => expandLogicTree(child, scope, depth + 1)) }
+    const rows = list.rows.slice(0, Math.min(repeat.limit ?? DESIGN_LIMITS.maxDataRows, DESIGN_LIMITS.maxDataRows))
+    const children: DesignNode[] = []
+    rows.forEach((row, index) => {
+      const inner: LogicScope = { ...scope, item: row }
+      for (const child of resolved.children) {
+        const copy = expandLogicTree(child, inner, depth + 1)
+        children.push(index === 0 ? copy : suffixIds(copy, `${REPEAT_SEP}${index}`))
+      }
+    })
+    return { ...frame, children }
+  }
+  if (resolved.children.length === 0) return resolved
+  let changed = resolved !== node
+  const children = resolved.children.map((child) => {
+    const next = expandLogicTree(child, scope, depth + 1)
+    if (next !== child) changed = true
+    return next
+  })
+  return changed ? { ...resolved, children } : node
+}
+
+/** A board with its logic resolved (for drawing, checking and code only: never stored). */
+export function materializeBoardLogic(board: DesignBoard, scope: LogicScope): DesignBoard {
+  const root = expandLogicTree(board.root, scope) as FrameNode
+  return root === board.root ? board : { ...board, root }
+}
+
+/** Every board's logic resolved; the identity when the design declares no variables or data. */
+export function materializeLogic(design: Design, overrides?: Readonly<Record<string, VariableValue>>): Design {
+  if (!hasLogic(design)) return design
+  const scope = logicScope(design, overrides)
+  return { ...design, boards: design.boards.map((board) => materializeBoardLogic(board, scope)) }
+}
+
+/** The template id a drawn node came from: a repeat copy's `~r<i>` suffixes dropped. */
+export function templateId(drawnId: string): string {
+  return drawnId.replace(/~r\d+(?=~|$)/g, '')
+}
+
+// ---- themes
+
+/** The tokens a board draws with: its theme's colour values over the base ones. */
+export function themedTokens(tokens: DesignTokens | undefined, theme: 'light' | 'dark' | undefined): DesignTokens | undefined {
+  if (!tokens || theme !== 'dark' || !tokens.themes?.dark) return tokens
+  return { ...tokens, colors: { ...tokens.colors, ...tokens.themes.dark } }
+}
+
+/** The variables a board's Props panel lists: design-wide ones and that board's own. */
+export function boardVariables(design: Design, boardId: string | null) {
+  return (design.variables ?? []).filter((variable) => variable.board === undefined || variable.board === boardId)
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/doc-tabs.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/doc-tabs.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..25f235ca0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/doc-tabs.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,259 @@
+/**
+ * Tabs in a doc. A doc stays one Markdown body; a tab starts at a marker block of its own:
+ *
+ *   [//]: # "tab: Launch notes"
+ *
+ * That is a link reference definition (label `//`, title `tab: …`), the standard invisible
+ * Markdown comment: GitHub, CommonMark renderers and our own drop it, so a plain reader sees
+ * nothing odd. (An HTML comment is not used: react-markdown draws one as literal text.)
+ *
+ * The blocks before the first marker are an unnamed first tab, so a body with no marker is
+ * exactly one tab holding everything. Every function here works on the body's top-level blocks
+ * (split on blank lines, fences kept whole, as the doc view splits them), and the edits it
+ * returns address those blocks by index and exact source, the shape of the doc view's
+ * `DocEdit` `kind: 'block'`: applied one at a time, each to the body the previous one left.
+ */
+
+/** One edit of the body's top-level blocks (see `DocEdit` in the doc view's doc-edit.ts). */
+export type DocBlockEdit = {
+  index: number
+  before: string
+  after: string
+  /** Blocks the edit covers from `index` (default 1); 0 inserts `after` before block `index`. */
+  span?: number
+  /** Moves the `span` blocks from `index` so the first lands at this index; `after === before`. */
+  moveTo?: number
+}
+
+export type DocTab = {
+  /** A slug of the name, unique in the doc: the tab's deep link is `#tab=<id>`. */
+  id: string
+  name: string
+  /** The marker block's index; null for the unnamed first tab. */
+  marker: number | null
+  /** The tab's content blocks: `start` (inclusive) to `end` (exclusive). */
+  start: number
+  end: number
+}
+
+export const DOC_TAB_LIMITS = { name: 60, tabs: 50 } as const
+
+/** What the unnamed first tab is called until it is renamed. */
+export const FIRST_TAB_NAME = 'Tab 1'
+
+const MARKER = /^ {0,3}\[\/\/\]:[ \t]*#[ \t]+(?:"tab:[ \t]*([^"\n]*?)[ \t]*"|\(tab:[ \t]*([^()\n]*?)[ \t]*\))[ \t]*$/
+
+/** The tab name a block declares, or null when the block is not a tab marker. */
+export function parseTabMarker(block: string): string | null {
+  const match = MARKER.exec(block)
+  if (!match) return null
+  const name = cleanTabName(match[1] ?? match[2] ?? '')
+  return name || null
+}
+
+export const isTabMarker = (block: string) => parseTabMarker(block) !== null
+
+/** A name as a marker can hold it: one line, no quotes or backslashes, at most 60 characters. */
+export function cleanTabName(input: string): string {
+  return input
+    .replace(/["\\()]/g, '')
+    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
+    .trim()
+    .slice(0, DOC_TAB_LIMITS.name)
+    .trim()
+}
+
+/** The marker block for a tab named `name` (already cleaned, non-empty). */
+export const tabMarker = (name: string) => `[//]: # "tab: ${cleanTabName(name)}"`
+
+const slug = (text: string) =>
+  text
+    .toLowerCase()
+    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
+    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
+
+/** The doc's tabs, in order. Always at least one. */
+export function docTabs(blocks: readonly string[]): DocTab[] {
+  const tabs: DocTab[] = []
+  const used = new Set<string>()
+  const id = (name: string) => {
+    const base = slug(name) || 'tab'
+    let next = base
+    for (let n = 2; used.has(next); n++) next = `${base}-${n}`
+    used.add(next)
+    return next
+  }
+  const markers: Array<{ index: number; name: string }> = []
+  blocks.forEach((block, index) => {
+    const name = parseTabMarker(block)
+    if (name !== null) markers.push({ index, name })
+  })
+  const first = markers[0]?.index ?? blocks.length
+  if (first > 0 || markers.length === 0)
+    tabs.push({ id: id(FIRST_TAB_NAME), name: FIRST_TAB_NAME, marker: null, start: 0, end: first })
+  markers.forEach((marker, n) => {
+    tabs.push({
+      id: id(marker.name),
+      name: marker.name,
+      marker: marker.index,
+      start: marker.index + 1,
+      end: markers[n + 1]?.index ?? blocks.length,
+    })
+  })
+  return tabs
+}
+
+/** The blocks a tab owns, marker included: what deleting or moving it takes. */
+const ownBlocks = (tab: DocTab) => ({ from: tab.marker ?? tab.start, to: tab.end })
+
+/** The blocks after one edit, for building the next edit of a sequence. Null when it does not apply. */
+export function applyBlockEdit(blocks: readonly string[], edit: DocBlockEdit): string[] | null {
+  const span = edit.span ?? 1
+  const next = [...blocks]
+  const split = (text: string) => (text.trim() ? splitBlocks(text) : [])
+  if (span === 0) {
+    if (edit.before !== '' || edit.index < 0 || edit.index > blocks.length) return null
+    next.splice(edit.index, 0, ...split(edit.after))
+    return next
+  }
+  const covered = blocks.slice(edit.index, edit.index + span)
+  if (edit.index < 0 || covered.length !== span || covered.join('\n\n') !== edit.before) return null
+  if (edit.moveTo !== undefined) {
+    if (edit.after !== edit.before) return null
+    next.splice(edit.index, span)
+    const to = Math.max(0, Math.min(next.length, edit.moveTo))
+    next.splice(to, 0, ...covered)
+    return next
+  }
+  next.splice(edit.index, span, ...split(edit.after))
+  return next
+}
+
+/** Text split into top-level blocks: on blank lines, fences kept whole. */
+export function splitBlocks(markdown: string): string[] {
+  const blocks: string[] = []
+  let current: string[] = []
+  let fenced = false
+  for (const line of markdown.replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n').split('\n')) {
+    if (line.trimStart().startsWith('```')) fenced = !fenced
+    if (!fenced && line.trim() === '') {
+      if (current.length) blocks.push(current.join('\n'))
+      current = []
+    } else current.push(line)
+  }
+  if (current.length) blocks.push(current.join('\n'))
+  return blocks
+}
+
+export type TabChange = { edits: DocBlockEdit[]; /** The tab to show afterwards. */ select?: string } | { error: string }
+
+/** The unnamed first tab, given a marker of its own (so it can move, and keeps its place). */
+function nameFirst(tabs: DocTab[]): DocBlockEdit | null {
+  if (tabs[0]?.marker !== null) return null
+  return { index: 0, span: 0, before: '', after: tabMarker(tabs[0].name) }
+}
+
+/** The name `Tab N` for a new tab, unused in the doc. */
+function freshName(tabs: DocTab[]) {
+  const names = new Set(tabs.map((tab) => tab.name.toLowerCase()))
+  for (let n = tabs.length + 1; ; n++) if (!names.has(`tab ${n}`)) return `Tab ${n}`
+}
+
+/** A sequence of edits, with the tab to select found in the result. */
+function sequence(
+  blocks: readonly string[],
+  edits: Array<DocBlockEdit | ((current: string[]) => DocBlockEdit)>,
+  select: (tabs: DocTab[]) => DocTab | undefined,
+): TabChange {
+  let current = [...blocks]
+  const out: DocBlockEdit[] = []
+  for (const step of edits) {
+    const edit = typeof step === 'function' ? step(current) : step
+    const next = applyBlockEdit(current, edit)
+    if (!next) return { error: 'The doc changed; try again.' }
+    out.push(edit)
+    current = next
+  }
+  return { edits: out, select: select(docTabs(current))?.id }
+}
+
+/**
+ * A new tab at the end (named `name`, or `Tab N`). The first tab of an unmarked doc gets its own
+ * marker first, so emptying it later never makes it vanish: two edits then, one otherwise.
+ */
+export function addTab(blocks: readonly string[], name?: string): TabChange {
+  const tabs = docTabs(blocks)
+  if (tabs.length >= DOC_TAB_LIMITS.tabs) return { error: `A doc has at most ${DOC_TAB_LIMITS.tabs} tabs.` }
+  const clean = cleanTabName(name ?? '') || freshName(tabs)
+  const first = nameFirst(tabs)
+  const marker = tabMarker(clean)
+  if (first && blocks.length === 0)
+    return sequence(blocks, [{ index: 0, span: 0, before: '', after: `${first.after}\n\n${marker}` }], (next) => next.at(-1))
+  return sequence(
+    blocks,
+    [...(first ? [first] : []), (current) => ({ index: current.length, span: 0, before: '', after: marker })],
+    (next) => next.at(-1),
+  )
+}
+
+export function renameTab(blocks: readonly string[], tabId: string, name: string): TabChange {
+  const tabs = docTabs(blocks)
+  const index = tabs.findIndex((tab) => tab.id === tabId)
+  const tab = tabs[index]
+  if (!tab) return { error: 'That tab is gone.' }
+  const clean = cleanTabName(name)
+  if (!clean) return { error: 'A tab needs a name.' }
+  if (clean === tab.name) return { edits: [], select: tab.id }
+  const edit: DocBlockEdit =
+    tab.marker === null
+      ? { index: 0, span: 0, before: '', after: tabMarker(clean) }
+      : { index: tab.marker, before: blocks[tab.marker], after: tabMarker(clean) }
+  return sequence(blocks, [edit], (next) => next[index])
+}
+
+/** Deletes a tab and everything in it. The last tab cannot go. */
+export function deleteTab(blocks: readonly string[], tabId: string): TabChange {
+  const tabs = docTabs(blocks)
+  const index = tabs.findIndex((tab) => tab.id === tabId)
+  const tab = tabs[index]
+  if (!tab) return { error: 'That tab is gone.' }
+  if (tabs.length <= 1) return { error: 'A doc keeps at least one tab.' }
+  const { from, to } = ownBlocks(tab)
+  if (to === from) return { error: 'That tab is gone.' }
+  const edit: DocBlockEdit = { index: from, span: to - from, before: blocks.slice(from, to).join('\n\n'), after: '' }
+  return sequence(blocks, [edit], (next) => next[Math.min(index, next.length - 1)] ?? next[0])
+}
+
+/**
+ * Moves a tab so it ends up at position `to` among the tabs, its blocks cut and pasted as they are.
+ * An unnamed first tab is named first when the move would take it from the front.
+ */
+export function moveTab(blocks: readonly string[], tabId: string, to: number): TabChange {
+  const tabs = docTabs(blocks)
+  const from = tabs.findIndex((tab) => tab.id === tabId)
+  if (from === -1) return { error: 'That tab is gone.' }
+  const target = Math.max(0, Math.min(tabs.length - 1, to))
+  if (target === from) return { edits: [], select: tabId }
+  // Only a move to or from the front can leave the unnamed first tab anywhere but first.
+  const first = from === 0 || target === 0 ? nameFirst(tabs) : null
+  const steps: Array<DocBlockEdit | ((current: string[]) => DocBlockEdit)> = first ? [first] : []
+  steps.push((current) => {
+    const now = docTabs(current)
+    const tab = now[from]
+    const { from: start, to: end } = ownBlocks(tab)
+    const span = end - start
+    // Where its first block lands once it is cut out: the target tab's start, or the end.
+    const rest = now.filter((_, n) => n !== from)
+    const dest = rest[target]
+    const at = dest ? ownBlocks(dest).from : current.length
+    const moveTo = at > start ? at - span : at
+    const source = current.slice(start, end).join('\n\n')
+    return { index: start, span, before: source, after: source, moveTo }
+  })
+  return sequence(blocks, steps, (next) => next[target])
+}
+
+/** The tab holding block `index` (a marker belongs to its own tab). */
+export function tabOfBlock(tabs: DocTab[], index: number): DocTab | undefined {
+  return tabs.find((tab) => index >= (tab.marker ?? tab.start) && index < tab.end) ?? tabs.at(-1)
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/slides-layout-report.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/slides-layout-report.ts
new file mode 100644

@@ -0,0 +1,233 @@
+import { DESIGN_SIZE, type Deck, type Slide, type SlideElement } from './slides'
+import { connectorLine, contrastRatio, deckPalette, tokensFixPalette } from './slides-r5'
+import { slidesOverflowIssues, slidesTextRegions, type SlideTextRegion } from './slides-overflow'
+
+/**
+ * A slide-by-slide layout report: the closest thing to a render that exists without a browser
+ * (there is no server-side renderer). For each slide it lists the text regions with their length
+ * against the room their box has, the free elements and where they sit, whether it has a title
+ * and its images' alt text, and the problems a person would see: text that will not fit (the
+ * same estimate as `slidesOverflowIssues`), elements that overlap or leave the canvas, text
+ * whose colour is too close to what is behind it, images without alt text, slides without a
+ * title, tiny text and connectors whose ends are gone. The editor's Checks list shows the same
+ * findings; the agent's read can return it. Pure and deterministic; an estimate, never a render.
+ */
+
+export type LayoutIssueKind =
+  | 'overflow'
+  | 'overlap'
+  | 'off-canvas'
+  | 'contrast'
+  | 'missing-alt'
+  | 'missing-title'
+  | 'small-text'
+  | 'unbound-connector'
+
+export type LayoutIssue = {
+  kind: LayoutIssueKind
+  slideId: string
+  slideNumber: number
+  /** The free elements it concerns, by id. */
+  elements?: string[]
+  message: string
+}
+
+export type ElementPlacement = {
+  id: string
+  type: SlideElement['type']
+  box: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }
+  /** Inside the canvas, cut off at an edge, or wholly off it (invisible). */
+  placement: 'inside' | 'partly-off' | 'off'
+  /** A text element's character count, an image's alt, an icon's name, a link's host. */
+  content?: string
+}
+
+export type SlideLayoutReport = {
+  slideId: string
+  slideNumber: number
+  layout: Slide['layout']
+  hasTitle: boolean
+  regions: Array<Omit<SlideTextRegion, 'slideId' | 'slideNumber'>>
+  elements: ElementPlacement[]
+  images: { count: number; missingAlt: number }
+  issues: LayoutIssue[]
+}
+
+/** Layouts a title belongs on (a blank slide or a quote may go without). */
+const TITLED: ReadonlyArray<Slide['layout']> = ['title', 'section', 'title-body', 'two-column', 'image', 'chart']
+
+/** Below this many px, slide text is hard to read once presented. */
+const SMALL_TEXT = 12
+/** How far past the edge counts as cut off (strokes and shadows may poke out a little). */
+const EDGE = 8
+
+type Box = { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }
+
+function placement(box: Box, width: number, height: number): ElementPlacement['placement'] {
+  if (box.x + box.w <= 0 || box.y + box.h <= 0 || box.x >= width || box.y >= height) return 'off'
+  if (box.x < -EDGE || box.y < -EDGE || box.x + box.w > width + EDGE || box.y + box.h > height + EDGE) return 'partly-off'
+  return 'inside'
+}
+
+const area = (box: Box) => Math.max(0, box.w) * Math.max(0, box.h)
+function intersection(a: Box, b: Box): number {
+  const w = Math.min(a.x + a.w, b.x + b.w) - Math.max(a.x, b.x)
+  const h = Math.min(a.y + a.h, b.y + b.h) - Math.max(a.y, b.y)
+  return w > 0 && h > 0 ? w * h : 0
+}
+const contains = (outer: Box, inner: Box) =>
+  inner.x >= outer.x - 1 && inner.y >= outer.y - 1 && inner.x + inner.w <= outer.x + outer.w + 1 && inner.y + inner.h <= outer.y + outer.h + 1
+
+/** What an element shows: text, a picture, an icon or a card (lines and connectors show none). */
+function content(element: SlideElement): string | undefined {
+  switch (element.type) {
+    case 'text':
+    case 'rect':
+    case 'ellipse':
+      return element.text?.trim() ? `${element.text.trim().length} chars` : undefined
+    case 'image':
+      return element.alt ? `image "${element.alt.slice(0, 60)}"` : 'image (no alt text)'
+    case 'icon':
+      return `icon ${element.icon}`
+    case 'link':
+      try {
+        return `link to ${new URL(element.url).host}`
+      } catch {
+        return 'link'
+      }
+    default:
+      return undefined
+  }
+}
+
+const carries = (element: SlideElement) =>
+  element.type === 'image' || element.type === 'icon' || element.type === 'link' || (('text' in element && element.text?.trim()) ? true : false)
+
+/** The free elements' boxes with each connector drawn where its ends put it. */
+function boxOf(slide: Slide, element: SlideElement): Box {
+  if (element.type !== 'connector') return element
+  const line = connectorLine(slide, element)
+  return { x: Math.min(line.x1, line.x2), y: Math.min(line.y1, line.y2), w: Math.abs(line.x2 - line.x1), h: Math.abs(line.y2 - line.y1) }
+}
+
+export function slidesLayoutReport(deck: Deck): SlideLayoutReport[] {
+  const { width, height } = DESIGN_SIZE[deck.aspect]
+  const overflow = slidesOverflowIssues(deck)
+  const regions = slidesTextRegions(deck)
+  const palettes = tokensFixPalette(deck) ? [{ mode: '', colors: deckPalette(deck, 'light') }] : (['light', 'dark'] as const).map((mode) => ({ mode: ` in ${mode} mode`, colors: deckPalette(deck, mode) }))
+  return deck.slides.map((slide, index) => {
+    const slideNumber = index + 1
+    const issues: LayoutIssue[] = []
+    const add = (kind: LayoutIssueKind, message: string, elements?: string[]) =>
+      issues.push({ kind, slideId: slide.id, slideNumber, message, ...(elements?.length ? { elements } : {}) })
+
+    for (const issue of overflow) if (issue.slideId === slide.id) add('overflow', `${issue.region}: ${issue.message}`, issue.region.startsWith('text box "') ? [issue.region.slice(10, -1)] : undefined)
+
+    const hasTitle = Boolean(slide.title?.trim())
+    if (!hasTitle && TITLED.includes(slide.layout))
+      add('missing-title', 'no title: screen readers, the outline and the exported file can only call it "Slide ' + slideNumber + '". Give it a short title.')
+
+    let images = 0
+    let missingAlt = 0
+    if (slide.image) {
+      images++
+      if (!slide.image.alt.trim()) {
+        missingAlt++
+        add('missing-alt', "the slide's picture has no alt text: describe it in a sentence (what it shows and why it is there).")
+      }
+    }
+
+    const elements = slide.elements ?? []
+    const placements: ElementPlacement[] = elements.map((element) => {
+      const box = boxOf(slide, element)
+      const where = element.rotation ? placement(rotatedBounds(box, element.rotation), width, height) : placement(box, width, height)
+      const shown = content(element)
+      return { id: element.id, type: element.type, box: { x: box.x, y: box.y, w: box.w, h: box.h }, placement: where, ...(shown ? { content: shown } : {}) }
+    })
+
+    elements.forEach((element, i) => {
+      const where = placements[i].placement
+      if (where === 'off') add('off-canvas', `element "${element.id}" is entirely off the ${width}x${height} slide, so nobody sees it. Move it on or remove it.`, [element.id])
+      else if (where === 'partly-off') add('off-canvas', `element "${element.id}" runs past the slide's edge and is cut off there.`, [element.id])
+
+      if (element.type === 'image') {
+        images++
+        if (!element.alt.trim()) {
+          missingAlt++
+          add('missing-alt', `image "${element.id}" has no alt text: describe what it shows.`, [element.id])
+        }
+      }
+      if (element.type === 'connector') {
+        const line = connectorLine(slide, element)
+        if (!line.bound) add('unbound-connector', `connector "${element.id}" joins "${element.from}" and "${element.to}", but one of them is not on the slide: it is drawn in its old place. Reconnect or remove it.`, [element.id])
+      }
+      if ((element.type === 'text' || element.type === 'rect' || element.type === 'ellipse') && element.text?.trim()) {
+        const size = element.size ?? 24
+        if (size < SMALL_TEXT) add('small-text', `text in "${element.id}" is ${size}px; under ${SMALL_TEXT}px is hard to read on a projected slide.`, [element.id])
+        // Its colour against what is behind it: its own fill, else the slide.
+        const fill = element.fill && element.fill !== 'none' ? element.fill : 'paper'
+        const ink = element.color ?? 'ink'
+        const large = size >= 24 || (element.bold === true && size >= 19)
+        const needed = large ? 3 : 4.5
+        for (const { mode, colors } of palettes) {
+          const ratio = contrastRatio(colors[ink], colors[fill])
+          if (ratio < needed) {
+            add('contrast', `"${element.id}": ${ink} text on ${fill === 'paper' ? 'the slide' : `a ${fill} fill`} is ${ratio}:1${mode}; text this size needs ${needed}:1. Change its colour or fill.`, [element.id])
+            break
+          }
+        }
+      }
+    })
+
+    // The deck's own colours (a design system's tokens) against its slide background.
+    if (deck.tokens?.colors && index === 0) {
+      for (const { mode, colors } of palettes)
+        for (const role of ['ink', 'soft', 'accent'] as const) {
+          const ratio = contrastRatio(colors[role], colors.paper)
+          const needed = role === 'accent' ? 3 : 4.5
+          if (ratio < needed) add('contrast', `the deck's ${role} colour is ${ratio}:1 against its background${mode} (needs ${needed}:1); every slide's ${role === 'ink' ? 'text' : role === 'soft' ? 'subtitles' : 'accents'} are hard to read.`)
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Overlaps: two elements that both show something, partly over each other (one wholly
+    // inside the other is a deliberate label on a shape, so it is left alone).
+    for (let a = 0; a < elements.length; a++)
+      for (let b = a + 1; b < elements.length; b++) {
+        const first = elements[a]
+        const second = elements[b]
+        if (!carries(first) || !carries(second)) continue
+        const boxA = placements[a].box
+        const boxB = placements[b].box
+        const shared = intersection(boxA, boxB)
+        if (!shared || contains(boxA, boxB) || contains(boxB, boxA)) continue
+        if (shared < Math.min(area(boxA), area(boxB)) * 0.1) continue
+        add('overlap', `elements "${first.id}" and "${second.id}" overlap (${Math.round(shared)}px² shared); "${second.id}" is drawn over "${first.id}". Move one or make them not touch.`, [first.id, second.id])
+      }
+
+    return {
+      slideId: slide.id,
+      slideNumber,
+      layout: slide.layout,
+      hasTitle,
+      regions: regions.filter((region) => region.slideId === slide.id).map(({ slideId: _s, slideNumber: _n, ...rest }) => rest),
+      elements: placements,
+      images: { count: images, missingAlt },
+      issues,
+    }
+  })
+}
+
+/** Every finding in the deck, slide by slide (the Checks list). */
+export function slidesLayoutIssues(deck: Deck): LayoutIssue[] {
+  return slidesLayoutReport(deck).flatMap((slide) => slide.issues)
+}
+
+/** The axis-aligned bounds of a box turned `degrees` about its centre. */
+function rotatedBounds(box: Box, degrees: number): Box {
+  const radians = (degrees * Math.PI) / 180
+  const cos = Math.abs(Math.cos(radians))
+  const sin = Math.abs(Math.sin(radians))
+  const w = box.w * cos + box.h * sin
+  const h = box.w * sin + box.h * cos
+  return { x: box.x + box.w / 2 - w / 2, y: box.y + box.h / 2 - h / 2, w, h }
+}

new file mode 100644
index 000000000..dd58e40ea
--- /dev/null
+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/whiteboard-route.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,309 @@
+/**
+ * Orthogonal connector routing around obstacles, for elbow arrows.
+ *
+ * `routeOrthogonal` finds a right-angled path between two points that stays `ROUTE_MARGIN` clear
+ * of every obstacle box, preferring few bends and then a short path. It searches a sparse grid
+ * whose lines are the obstacles' (inflated) edges, the two ends and a frame around them, with A*
+ * over (grid node, heading) so a turn costs `BEND_COST`. Small and bounded: the caller passes only
+ * the obstacles near the connector, and a grid over `MAX_GRID_NODES` gives up (null), as does no
+ * path, so the caller falls back to the plain elbow. Results are memoised by their inputs, since
+ * the canvas redraws every arrow on every frame of a drag.
+ *
+ * Pure: points and rects in, points out; no board types beyond them, no DOM.
+ */
+
+import type { Point, Rect, Side } from './whiteboard'
+
+/** How far a route keeps from an obstacle's edge. Under `ELBOW_STUB`, so a stub leaves its own box clear. */
+export const ROUTE_MARGIN = 16
+/** What one turn costs, in world units of length: two turns must save more than this much path. */
+export const BEND_COST = 48
+/** Obstacles one search considers; the nearest are kept. */
+export const MAX_ROUTE_OBSTACLES = 48
+const MAX_GRID_NODES = 12_000
+
+/** Headings: 0 +x, 1 +y, 2 -x, 3 -y. */
+const DX = [1, 0, -1, 0]
+const DY = [0, 1, 0, -1]
+const SIDE_DIR: Record<Side, number> = { right: 0, bottom: 1, left: 2, top: 3 }
+
+export type RouteEnd = {
+  /** Where the route starts or ends (a bound end's stub point, already outside its box). */
+  point: Point
+  /** The side it leaves (start) or enters from (end); absent for a free end, which may go any way. */
+  side?: Side
+}
+
+const inflateBy = (r: Rect, m: number): Rect => ({ x: r.x - m, y: r.y - m, w: r.w + 2 * m, h: r.h + 2 * m })
+
+const strictlyInside = (p: Point, r: Rect) => p.x > r.x && p.x < r.x + r.w && p.y > r.y && p.y < r.y + r.h
+
+/** Sorted, de-duplicated values (to 0.01). */
+function lines(values: number[]): number[] {
+  values.sort((a, b) => a - b)
+  const out: number[] = []
+  for (const v of values) if (out.length === 0 || v - out[out.length - 1] > 0.01) out.push(v)
+  return out
+}
+
+/** Index of `v` in sorted `values` (exact to 0.01), or -1. */
+function indexOf(values: readonly number[], v: number): number {
+  let lo = 0
+  let hi = values.length - 1
+  while (lo <= hi) {
+    const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1
+    if (Math.abs(values[mid] - v) <= 0.01) return mid
+    if (values[mid] < v) lo = mid + 1
+    else hi = mid - 1
+  }
+  return -1
+}
+
+/** The first index of sorted `values` at or above `v`. */
+function lowerBound(values: readonly number[], v: number): number {
+  let lo = 0
+  let hi = values.length
+  while (lo < hi) {
+    const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1
+    if (values[mid] < v) lo = mid + 1
+    else hi = mid
+  }
+  return lo
+}
+
+/** A binary min-heap of state ids keyed by a Float64 score. */
+class Heap {
+  private ids: number[] = []
+  private keys: number[] = []
+  get size() {
+    return this.ids.length
+  }
+  push(id: number, key: number) {
+    const ids = this.ids
+    const keys = this.keys
+    let i = ids.length
+    ids.push(id)
+    keys.push(key)
+    while (i > 0) {
+      const parent = (i - 1) >> 1
+      if (keys[parent] <= key) break
+      ids[i] = ids[parent]
+      keys[i] = keys[parent]
+      i = parent
+    }
+    ids[i] = id
+    keys[i] = key
+  }
+  /** The smallest key (Infinity when empty). */
+  peekKey() {
+    return this.keys.length ? this.keys[0] : Infinity
+  }
+  pop(): number {
+    const ids = this.ids
+    const keys = this.keys
+    const top = ids[0]
+    const lastId = ids.pop()!
+    const lastKey = keys.pop()!
+    if (ids.length > 0) {
+      let i = 0
+      const n = ids.length
+      for (;;) {
+        const l = 2 * i + 1
+        const r = l + 1
+        let m = i
+        let mKey = lastKey
+        if (l < n && keys[l] < mKey) {
+          m = l
+          mKey = keys[l]
+        }
+        if (r < n && keys[r] < mKey) m = r
+        if (m === i) break
+        ids[i] = ids[m]
+        keys[i] = keys[m]
+        i = m
+      }
+      ids[i] = lastId
+      keys[i] = lastKey
+    }
+    return top
+  }
+}
+
+const cache = new Map<string, Point[] | null>()
+const CACHE_SIZE = 2_000
+
+function cacheKey(a: RouteEnd, b: RouteEnd, obstacles: readonly Rect[]): string {
+  let key = `${a.point.x},${a.point.y},${a.side ?? ''}|${b.point.x},${b.point.y},${b.side ?? ''}`
+  for (const r of obstacles) key += `|${r.x},${r.y},${r.w},${r.h}`
+  return key
+}
+
+/**
+ * A right-angled path from `a` to `b` around `obstacles`, as its corner points from `a.point` to
+ * `b.point` inclusive, or null when there is none (or the search would be too big). The path
+ * leaves `a` along `a.side` and arrives at `b` heading into `b.side` (it never leaves or arrives
+ * backwards). Obstacles that already hold either end inside their margin are ignored, so a
+ * crowded end still routes.
+ */
+export function routeOrthogonal(a: RouteEnd, b: RouteEnd, obstacles: readonly Rect[]): Point[] | null {
+  const key = cacheKey(a, b, obstacles)
+  if (cache.has(key)) {
+    const hit = cache.get(key)!
+    // Refresh its place in the LRU order.
+    cache.delete(key)
+    cache.set(key, hit)
+    return hit
+  }
+  const result = search(a, b, obstacles)
+  cache.set(key, result)
+  if (cache.size > CACHE_SIZE) cache.delete(cache.keys().next().value!)
+  return result
+}
+
+function search(a: RouteEnd, b: RouteEnd, obstacles: readonly Rect[]): Point[] | null {
+  const blocks = obstacles
+    .map((r) => inflateBy(r, ROUTE_MARGIN))
+    .filter((r) => !strictlyInside(a.point, r) && !strictlyInside(b.point, r))
+  // A frame one margin outside everything, so a route can always go round the outside.
+  let fx1 = Math.min(a.point.x, b.point.x)
+  let fy1 = Math.min(a.point.y, b.point.y)
+  let fx2 = Math.max(a.point.x, b.point.x)
+  let fy2 = Math.max(a.point.y, b.point.y)
+  for (const r of blocks) {
+    fx1 = Math.min(fx1, r.x)
+    fy1 = Math.min(fy1, r.y)
+    fx2 = Math.max(fx2, r.x + r.w)
+    fy2 = Math.max(fy2, r.y + r.h)
+  }
+  const xsRaw = [a.point.x, b.point.x, (a.point.x + b.point.x) / 2, fx1 - ROUTE_MARGIN, fx2 + ROUTE_MARGIN]
+  const ysRaw = [a.point.y, b.point.y, (a.point.y + b.point.y) / 2, fy1 - ROUTE_MARGIN, fy2 + ROUTE_MARGIN]
+  for (const r of blocks) {
+    xsRaw.push(r.x, r.x + r.w)
+    ysRaw.push(r.y, r.y + r.h)
+  }
+  const xs = lines(xsRaw)
+  const ys = lines(ysRaw)
+  const nx = xs.length
+  const ny = ys.length
+  if (nx * ny > MAX_GRID_NODES) return null
+  const start = indexOf(xs, a.point.x) + nx * indexOf(ys, a.point.y)
+  const goal = indexOf(xs, b.point.x) + nx * indexOf(ys, b.point.y)
+
+  // Blocked nodes and the segments to the next node along x (h) and along y (v).
+  const nodeBlocked = new Uint8Array(nx * ny)
+  const hBlocked = new Uint8Array(nx * ny)
+  const vBlocked = new Uint8Array(nx * ny)
+  for (const r of blocks) {
+    const x2 = r.x + r.w
+    const y2 = r.y + r.h
+    // Only the grid lines on or inside the box matter (its edges are grid lines themselves).
+    const i0 = lowerBound(xs, r.x - 0.01)
+    const j0 = lowerBound(ys, r.y - 0.01)
+    for (let j = j0; j < ny && ys[j] <= y2 + 0.01; j++) {
+      const y = ys[j]
+      const yInside = y > r.y + 0.01 && y < y2 - 0.01
+      for (let i = i0; i < nx && xs[i] <= x2 + 0.01; i++) {
+        const x = xs[i]
+        const id = i + nx * j
+        const xInside = x > r.x + 0.01 && x < x2 - 0.01
+        if (xInside && yInside) nodeBlocked[id] = 1
+        // The segment to i+1 lies strictly inside when its row is inside and both ends are within.
+        if (yInside && i + 1 < nx && x >= r.x - 0.01 && xs[i + 1] <= x2 + 0.01) hBlocked[id] = 1
+        // The segment to j+1 likewise, along a column strictly inside.
+        if (xInside && j + 1 < ny && y >= r.y - 0.01 && ys[j + 1] <= y2 + 0.01) vBlocked[id] = 1
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  const states = nx * ny * 4
+  const cost = new Float64Array(states).fill(Infinity)
+  const parent = new Int32Array(states).fill(-1)
+  const heap = new Heap()
+  const gx = b.point.x
+  const gy = b.point.y
+  const h = (node: number) => Math.abs(xs[node % nx] - gx) + Math.abs(ys[Math.floor(node / nx)] - gy)
+  const startDirs = a.side ? [SIDE_DIR[a.side]] : [0, 1, 2, 3]
+  for (const d of startDirs) {
+    const s = start * 4 + d
+    cost[s] = 0
+    heap.push(s, h(start))
+  }
+  // Arriving at b heading into its side: the reverse of the side's outward heading.
+  const arriveDir = b.side ? (SIDE_DIR[b.side] + 2) % 4 : -1
+  let best = Infinity
+  let bestState = -1
+  while (heap.size > 0 && heap.peekKey() < best) {
+    const s = heap.pop()
+    const node = s >> 2
+    const d = s & 3
+    const g = cost[s]
+    if (node === goal) {
+      if (arriveDir === -1 || d === arriveDir || (d + 2) % 4 !== arriveDir) {
+        // Turning at the stub point costs a little more than a turn elsewhere, so of two equal
+        // routes the one that runs straight into the side wins.
+        const total = g + (arriveDir === -1 || d === arriveDir ? 0 : BEND_COST + 8)
+        // A start that is already the goal (no move) only counts with a matching heading.
+        if (total < best) {
+          best = total
+          bestState = s
+        }
+      }
+      continue
+    }
+    const i = node % nx
+    const j = (node - i) / nx
+    for (let nd = 0; nd < 4; nd++) {
+      if (nd === (d + 2) % 4) continue // never double back
+      const ni = i + DX[nd]
+      const nj = j + DY[nd]
+      if (ni < 0 || nj < 0 || ni >= nx || nj >= ny) continue
+      const next = ni + nx * nj
+      if (nodeBlocked[next] && next !== goal) continue
+      const blocked =
+        nd === 0 ? hBlocked[node] : nd === 2 ? hBlocked[next] : nd === 1 ? vBlocked[node] : vBlocked[next]
+      if (blocked) continue
+      const step = Math.abs(xs[ni] - xs[i]) + Math.abs(ys[nj] - ys[j])
+      const ng = g + step + (nd === d ? 0 : BEND_COST)
+      const ns = next * 4 + nd
+      if (ng < cost[ns] - 1e-9) {
+        cost[ns] = ng
+        parent[ns] = s
+        heap.push(ns, ng + h(next))
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  if (bestState === -1) return null
+  const path: Point[] = []
+  for (let s = bestState; s !== -1; s = parent[s]) {
+    const node = s >> 2
+    const p = { x: xs[node % nx], y: ys[Math.floor(node / nx)] }
+    const last = path[path.length - 1]
+    if (!last || last.x !== p.x || last.y !== p.y) path.push(p)
+  }
+  path.reverse()
+  // Keep only the corners.
+  const corners: Point[] = [path[0]]
+  for (let k = 1; k < path.length - 1; k++) {
+    const p = corners[corners.length - 1]
+    const q = path[k]
+    const r = path[k + 1]
+    const straight = (p.x === q.x && q.x === r.x) || (p.y === q.y && q.y === r.y)
+    if (!straight) corners.push(q)
+  }
+  if (path.length > 1) corners.push(path[path.length - 1])
+  return corners
+}
+
+/** Whether an axis-aligned segment passes through the inside of `r` (shrunk by `shrink`; touching an edge is fine). */
+export function segmentCrossesRect(p: Point, q: Point, r: Rect, shrink = 1): boolean {
+  const x1 = r.x + shrink
+  const x2 = r.x + r.w - shrink
+  const y1 = r.y + shrink
+  const y2 = r.y + r.h - shrink
+  if (x2 <= x1 || y2 <= y1) return false
+  const minX = Math.min(p.x, q.x)
+  const maxX = Math.max(p.x, q.x)
+  const minY = Math.min(p.y, q.y)
+  const maxY = Math.max(p.y, q.y)
+  return maxX > x1 && minX < x2 && maxY > y1 && minY < y2
+}

+/**
+ * Running a connected recipe (data-connect.ts), the pure parts: filling its `{{blanks}}`, turning
+ * the integration's results into rows (`mapIntegrationRows`), and, for the owner's "Test", an
+ * outline of a result's shape to map from. The server route (artifact-data-refresh.ts) does the
+ * calls; nothing here fetches.
+ *
+ * Every refusal is one of `ConnectProblem`'s fixed codes with fixed words (`connectProblemText`):
+ * what reaches a dashboard's `lastRun.message`, which every viewer sees, is never a provider's
+ * error text or any part of a payload.
+ */
+
+import { blankValue, type RecipeOptions } from './data-recipe'
+import {
+  INTEGRATION_MAX_ROWS,
+  pathSegments,
+  type IntegrationCall,
+  type IntegrationMap,
+  type IntegrationRecipe,
+} from './data-connect'
+import type { Cell, ColumnType, Dashboard, DataColumn, DataRow } from './data'
+
+/** Mirrors data-recipe.ts SAFE_ARG_VALUE: a blank's value is plain text, never syntax. */
+const SAFE_VALUE = /^[A-Za-z0-9 _.:,@/+=]{0,120}$/
+const BLANK = /\{\{([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)\}\}/g
+const WHOLE_BLANK = /^\{\{([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)\}\}$/
+const MAX_CELL_CHARS = 1000
+const ISO_DATE = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/
+
+export type ConnectProblem =
+  | 'not_connected'
+  | 'pending'
+  | 'not_read_only'
+  | 'blank_unsafe'
+  | 'call_failed'
+  | 'too_large'
+  | 'no_list'
+  | 'unknown_column'
+  | 'bad_rows'
+  | 'checks_failed'
+  | 'timeout'
+  | 'unavailable'
+  | 'changed'
+
+const PROBLEM_TEXT: Record<ConnectProblem, string> = {
+  not_connected: "The source app isn't connected on the owner's account any more.",
+  pending: 'The connected source is waiting for the owner to approve it.',
+  not_read_only: 'A call in the recipe could change data in the app, so it was not run.',
+  blank_unsafe: 'A filter value cannot go into the source call (plain letters, digits and _.:,@/+= only).',
+  call_failed: 'The source app returned an error.',
+  too_large: 'The source returned too much data. Narrow the call (fewer items or a filter).',
+  no_list: "The source's answer had no list of records where the recipe expects one.",
+  unknown_column: 'The recipe maps a column the dataset does not have.',
+  bad_rows: "The mapped rows don't fit the dataset's columns.",
+  checks_failed: "The new rows break the dataset's checks, so the old rows were kept.",
+  timeout: 'The source took too long to answer.',
+  unavailable: 'Integrations are unavailable right now. Try again.',
+  changed: 'The recipe changed while it ran. Refresh again.',
+}
+
+/** The fixed words for a problem: safe for any viewer. */
+export const connectProblemText = (problem: ConnectProblem) => PROBLEM_TEXT[problem]
+
+/** One call's outcome, as integrations `run` returns it ({tool, account?, data} or {tool, error}). */
+export type IntegrationCallOutcome = { tool: string; account?: string; data?: unknown; error?: string }
+
+/**
+ * A recipe's calls with their blanks filled from the reader's values (`options`), or the
+ * defaults when there are none. A value must be plain text (`SAFE_VALUE`, not starting with
+ * "-"); a string that is exactly one parameter's blank becomes that number.
+ */
+export function fillIntegrationCalls(
+  dashboard: Dashboard,
+  how: IntegrationRecipe,
+  options: RecipeOptions = {},
+): { calls: IntegrationCall[] } | { problem: 'blank_unsafe' } {
+  let unsafe = false
+  const fill = (value: unknown): unknown => {
+    if (typeof value === 'string') {
+      const whole = WHOLE_BLANK.exec(value)
+      if (whole && dashboard.params?.some((p) => p.id === whole[1])) {
+        const number = Number(blankValue(dashboard, whole[1]!, options))
+        if (Number.isFinite(number)) return number
+      }
+      return value.replace(BLANK, (_match, id: string) => {
+        const text = blankValue(dashboard, id, options) ?? ''
+        if (!SAFE_VALUE.test(text) || text.startsWith('-')) unsafe = true
+        return text
+      })
+    }
+    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(fill)
+    if (value && typeof value === 'object')
+      return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>).map(([key, item]) => [key, fill(item)]))
+    return value
+  }
+  const calls = how.calls.map((call) => ({ ...call, arguments: fill(call.arguments) as Record<string, unknown> }))
+  return unsafe ? { problem: 'blank_unsafe' } : { calls }
+}
+
+/** The value at a path, or undefined. Own properties only. */
+export function readPath(value: unknown, path: string): unknown {
+  const segments = pathSegments(path)
+  if (!segments) return undefined
+  let current = value
+  for (const segment of segments) {
+    if (current === null || typeof current !== 'object') return undefined
+    if (typeof segment === 'number') {
+      if (!Array.isArray(current)) return undefined
+      current = current[segment]
+    } else {
+      if (Array.isArray(current) || !Object.hasOwn(current, segment)) return undefined
+      current = (current as Record<string, unknown>)[segment]
+    }
+  }
+  return current
+}
+
+const NUMERIC = new Set<ColumnType>(['number', 'currency', 'percent'])
+const NUMBER_TEXT = /^[-+]?(\d+(\.\d*)?|\.\d+)([eE][-+]?\d+)?$/
+
+/** A value coerced to a column's type: a cell, or `undefined` when it cannot be one. */
+export function coerceCell(value: unknown, type: ColumnType, scale?: number): Cell | undefined {
+  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null
+  if (NUMERIC.has(type)) {
+    let n: number
+    if (typeof value === 'number') n = value
+    else if (typeof value === 'string') {
+      const text = value.trim().replace(/^[$€£¥]/, '').replace(/,(?=\d{3}(\D|$))/g, '').replace(/%$/, '')
+      if (!NUMBER_TEXT.test(text)) return undefined
+      n = Number(text)
+    } else return undefined
+    if (scale !== undefined) n *= scale
+    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : undefined
+  }
+  if (type === 'date') {
+    if (typeof value === 'string') {
+      const match = ISO_DATE.exec(value.trim())
+      if (!match) return undefined
+      const [, y, m, d] = match
+      const date = new Date(Date.UTC(Number(y), Number(m) - 1, Number(d)))
+      return date.getUTCMonth() === Number(m) - 1 && date.getUTCDate() === Number(d) ? `${y}-${m}-${d}` : undefined
+    }
+    if (typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value)) {
+      // Epoch seconds or milliseconds.
+      const ms = Math.abs(value) < 1e11 ? value * 1000 : value
+      const date = new Date(ms)
+      return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? undefined : date.toISOString().slice(0, 10)
+    }
+    return undefined
+  }
+  if (typeof value === 'string') return value.slice(0, MAX_CELL_CHARS)
+  if (typeof value === 'number' || typeof value === 'boolean') return String(value)
+  if (Array.isArray(value) && value.every((item) => item === null || ['string', 'number', 'boolean'].includes(typeof item)))
+    return value.filter((item) => item !== null).join(', ').slice(0, MAX_CELL_CHARS)
+  return undefined
+}
+
+export type MappedRows =
+  | { ok: true; rows: DataRow[]; dropped: number; blanked: number }
+  | { ok: false; problem: ConnectProblem }
+
+/**
+ * The rows a recipe's results make: for each call in order, the list at `map.records` in its
+ * data, each record read into the mapped columns and coerced to the dataset's column types.
+ * Any failed call, a truncated result (integrations cut an oversized one to a text preview),
+ * or a missing list fails the whole run: a partial pull is never written as if it were whole.
+ * A value that cannot be its column's type becomes empty (`blanked` counts them).
+ */
+export function mapIntegrationRows(
+  outcomes: readonly IntegrationCallOutcome[],
+  map: IntegrationMap,
+  columns: readonly DataColumn[],
+  maxRows = INTEGRATION_MAX_ROWS,
+): MappedRows {
+  const types = new Map(columns.map((c) => [c.key, c.type]))
+  for (const column of map.columns) if (!types.has(column.key)) return { ok: false, problem: 'unknown_column' }
+  const cap = Math.min(map.limit ?? INTEGRATION_MAX_ROWS, maxRows, INTEGRATION_MAX_ROWS)
+  const rows: DataRow[] = []
+  let total = 0
+  let blanked = 0
+  for (const outcome of outcomes) {
+    if (outcome.error !== undefined || !('data' in outcome)) return { ok: false, problem: 'call_failed' }
+    const data = outcome.data
+    if (data && typeof data === 'object' && !Array.isArray(data) && (data as { truncated?: unknown }).truncated === true)
+      return { ok: false, problem: 'too_large' }
+    const records = readPath(data, map.records)
+    if (!Array.isArray(records)) return { ok: false, problem: 'no_list' }
+    for (const record of records) {
+      total++
+      if (rows.length >= cap) continue
+      const row: DataRow = {}
+      for (const column of map.columns) {
+        const raw =
+          column.path === '$account'
+            ? (outcome.account ?? null)
+            : column.path === '$tool'
+              ? outcome.tool
+              : column.path === '$index'
+                ? rows.length
+                : readPath(record, column.path)
+        const cell = coerceCell(raw, types.get(column.key)!, column.scale)
+        if (cell === undefined) blanked++
+        row[column.key] = cell ?? null
+      }
+      rows.push(row)
+    }
+  }
+  return { ok: true, rows, dropped: total - rows.length, blanked }
+}
+
+export type ShapeOutline = {
+  /** Paths to lists in the data, with their lengths (largest first). */
+  lists: Array<{ path: string; length: number }>
+  /** Fields of the first record at `records`: path, JSON type and a short sample. */
+  fields: Array<{ path: string; type: string; sample: string }>
+}
+
+const join = (base: string, key: string | number) =>
+  typeof key === 'number' ? `${base}[${key}]` : base ? `${base}.${key}` : key
+
+/**
+ * For the owner's Test only: where the lists are in a result and what a record holds, so they
+ * can pick `records` and each column's path. Bounded (depth, entries, sample length).
+ */
+export function outlineShape(data: unknown, records?: string, max = 60): ShapeOutline {
+  const lists: ShapeOutline['lists'] = []
+  const visit = (value: unknown, path: string, depth: number) => {
+    if (depth > 5 || lists.length >= 20) return
+    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
+      lists.push({ path, length: value.length })
+      if (value.length && value[0] && typeof value[0] === 'object') visit(value[0], join(path, 0), depth + 1)
+      return
+    }
+    if (value && typeof value === 'object')
+      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value).slice(0, 40)) if (pathSegments(join(path, key))) visit(item, join(path, key), depth + 1)
+  }
+  visit(data, '', 0)
+  lists.sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length)
+  const fields: ShapeOutline['fields'] = []
+  const list = readPath(data, records ?? lists[0]?.path ?? '')
+  const first = Array.isArray(list) ? list.find((item) => item && typeof item === 'object') : undefined
+  const walk = (value: unknown, path: string, depth: number) => {
+    if (fields.length >= max) return
+    if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) && depth < 3) {
+      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) if (pathSegments(join(path, key))) walk(item, join(path, key), depth + 1)
+      return
+    }
+    if (!path) return
+    const type = value === null ? 'null' : Array.isArray(value) ? 'list' : typeof value
+    const text = typeof value === 'string' ? value : JSON.stringify(value) ?? ''
+    fields.push({ path, type, sample: text.length > 40 ? `${text.slice(0, 39)}…` : text })
+  }
+  if (first) walk(first, '', 0)
+  return { lists, fields }
+}
