+import { z } from 'zod/v4'
+
+import type { ArtifactSelection } from './artifact-view-context'
+import type { ArtifactAuthorKind, ArtifactKind } from './artifacts'
+
+/**
+ * Real-time for an open artifact tab: revision SIGNALS and PRESENCE, on the
+ * hosted Cloud transport (web/src/server/desktop-cloud/streams.ts, the realtime
+ * Redis) through one SSE per tab, `GET artifacts/:id/live`.
+ *
+ * - A signal says only that an artifact moved: its id, revision, what kind of
+ *   change, and whether an agent made it. Never content. The tab answers it the
+ *   way its poll answers a newer head (fetch + three-way merge), and the poll
+ *   stays as the fallback and as a slow safety net.
+ * - Presence is ephemeral (Redis with a TTL, never Postgres): who has the tab
+ *   open, their mode, and their selection in the same ids the agent's view
+ *   context uses, minus any text. Names, colours and anonymous labels are
+ *   assigned by the server; a client never names itself.
+ *
+ * With no transport (no realtime Redis, a 503, a dropped stream) everything is
+ * exactly the polling it was before.
+ */
+
+export const ARTIFACT_CHANGES = ['created', 'body', 'title', 'comments', 'assets', 'access', 'deleted'] as const
+export type ArtifactChange = (typeof ARTIFACT_CHANGES)[number]
+
+export type ArtifactSignal = {
+  v: 1
+  artifactId: string
+  /** The stored revision after the change (0 when the change does not know it: comments, access). */
+  revision: number
+  change: ArtifactChange
+  authorKind: ArtifactAuthorKind | null
+}
+
+const uuid = z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/)
+
+export const artifactSignalSchema = z.strictObject({
+  v: z.literal(1),
+  artifactId: uuid,
+  revision: z.number().int().min(0).max(2_000_000_000),
+  change: z.enum(ARTIFACT_CHANGES),
+  authorKind: z.enum(['user', 'agent']).nullable(),
+})
+
+/** The Redis topic of an artifact's signals (namespaced per environment by CloudStreams). */
+export const artifactSignalTopic = (artifactId: string) => `artifact:${artifactId}`
+/** The Redis topic of an artifact's presence notifications. */
+export const artifactPresenceTopic = (artifactId: string) => `artifact-presence:${artifactId}`
+
+// ---- presence
+
+export const ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_MODES = ['viewing', 'editing', 'presenting'] as const
+export type ArtifactPresenceMode = (typeof ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_MODES)[number]
+
+/** A peer that has not been heard from this long is gone. */
+export const ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_TTL_MS = 10_000
+/**
+ * The tab's open live stream keeps its presence entry alive this often (server side, Redis
+ * only), so presence costs no request while nothing changes; a closed tab stops it and is
+ * gone within the TTL.
+ */
+export const ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_KEEPALIVE_MS = 3_000
+/** A client re-sends its whole state this often anyway (an entry lost to a Redis restart). */
+export const ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_HEARTBEAT_MS = 30_000
+/**
+ * Presence updates a client sends per second at most, coalesced (the latest state wins, nothing
+ * queues). Each one is a gateway request with its session lookups, so this is kept low; pointer
+ * samples are batched into them.
+ */
+export const ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_MAX_PER_SECOND = 2
+/** Peers one artifact shows at most; later arrivals are not admitted until someone leaves. */
+export const ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_MAX_PEERS = 24
+/** A presence update's JSON body, bytes. */
+export const ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_MAX_BYTES = 2048
+/** Pointer samples one update carries at most (sampled ~20/s, batched into ≤2 updates/s). */
+export const ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_POINTER_SAMPLES = 10
+
+const id = z.string().min(1).max(64)
+
+/**
+ * A selection as presence carries it: the ids that place a marker, never the
+ * quoted text, excerpts or filter values the agent's view context may hold.
+ */
+export const artifactPresenceSelectionSchema = z.discriminatedUnion('kind', [
+  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('doc'), tabId: id.optional(), blockIndex: z.number().int().min(0).max(100_000).optional() }),
+  z.strictObject({
+    kind: z.literal('slides'),
+    slideId: id.optional(),
+    element: z.string().min(1).max(40).optional(),
+    elementIds: z.array(id).max(12).optional(),
+  }),
+  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('sheet'), tabId: id.optional(), range: z.string().max(40).regex(/^[A-Z]{1,3}[0-9]{1,7}(:[A-Z]{1,3}[0-9]{1,7})?$/).optional() }),
+  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('data'), blockId: id.optional(), tabId: id.optional() }),
+  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('whiteboard'), elementIds: z.array(id).max(20).optional() }),
+  z.strictObject({ kind: z.literal('design'), pageId: id.optional(), boardId: id.optional(), nodeId: id.optional() }),
+])
+export type ArtifactPresenceSelection = z.infer<typeof artifactPresenceSelectionSchema>
+
+/** A pointer position in the pane's own coordinates (a whiteboard's board units), `t` ms since the sender's batch began. */
+export const artifactPresencePointerSchema = z.strictObject({
+  x: z.number().finite().min(-1e7).max(1e7),
+  y: z.number().finite().min(-1e7).max(1e7),
+  t: z.number().int().min(0).max(5_000),
+})
+export type ArtifactPresencePointer = z.infer<typeof artifactPresencePointerSchema>
+
+/** What a client says about itself. Who it is comes from the server. */
+export const artifactPresenceUpdateSchema = z.strictObject({
+  /** Random per tab; it names this tab's presence entry, nothing else. */
+  sessionId: z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,64}$/),
+  mode: z.enum(ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_MODES),
+  selection: artifactPresenceSelectionSchema.nullable().optional(),
+  pointer: z.array(artifactPresencePointerSchema).max(ARTIFACT_PRESENCE_POINTER_SAMPLES).optional(),
+  linkToken: z.string().max(64).optional(),
+  /** The tab is closing: drop its entry now rather than at the TTL. */
+  leave: z.literal(true).optional(),
+})
+export type ArtifactPresenceUpdate = z.infer<typeof artifactPresenceUpdateSchema>
+
+/** One peer as a viewer receives it. */
+export type ArtifactPeer = {
+  /** Stable for the tab's life; derived from its session id, never an account id. */
+  id: string
+  /** The account's display name, or an anonymous label ("Anonymous Otter"). */
+  name: string
+  anonymous: boolean
+  color: string
+  mode: ArtifactPresenceMode
+  selection: ArtifactPresenceSelection | null
+  pointer: ArtifactPresencePointer[] | null
+  /** Epoch ms the server last heard from it. */
+  at: number
+  /** The agent (drawn with Buffy's head); never sent by the server, added by the client. */
+  agent?: boolean
+}
+
+export type ArtifactLiveHello = {
+  v: 1
+  /** The stored revision when the stream opened: a tab behind it reconciles at once. */
+  revision: number
+  /** This tab's own peer id (its entry is left out of `presence` frames it receives). */
+  you: string | null
+}
+
+/** One SSE frame of `GET artifacts/:id/live` (event name → data). */
+export type ArtifactLiveFrame =
+  | { event: 'hello'; data: ArtifactLiveHello }
+  | { event: 'signal'; data: ArtifactSignal }
+  | { event: 'presence'; data: { peers: ArtifactPeer[] } }
+  | { event: 'revoked'; data: Record<string, never> }
+
+/**
+ * The view context's selection as presence sends it: ids only. Null for a
+ * selection of another kind than the artifact's.
+ */
+export function presenceSelectionOf(
+  kind: ArtifactKind,
+  selection: ArtifactSelection | null | undefined,
+): ArtifactPresenceSelection | null {
+  if (!selection) return null
+  const clip = (value: string | undefined) => (value ? value.slice(0, 64) : undefined)
+  const defined = <T extends Record<string, unknown>>(value: T) =>
+    Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined)) as T
+  const kindOf: Record<ArtifactSelection['kind'], ArtifactKind> = {
+    doc: 'doc',
+    slides: 'slides',
+    sheet: 'sheet',
+    data: 'data',
+    whiteboard: 'whiteboard',
+    design: 'design',
+  }
+  if (kindOf[selection.kind] !== kind) return null
+  let out: ArtifactPresenceSelection
+  switch (selection.kind) {
+    case 'doc':
+      out = defined({ kind: 'doc', tabId: clip(selection.tabId), blockIndex: selection.blockIndex })
+      break
+    case 'slides':
+      out = defined({
+        kind: 'slides',
+        slideId: clip(selection.slideId),
+        element: selection.element,
+        elementIds: selection.freeElements?.length ? selection.freeElements.map((e) => e.id.slice(0, 64)).slice(0, 12) : undefined,
+      })
+      break
+    case 'sheet':
+      out = defined({ kind: 'sheet', tabId: clip(selection.tabId), range: selection.range?.toUpperCase() })
+      break
+    case 'data':
+      out = defined({ kind: 'data', blockId: clip(selection.blockId), tabId: clip(selection.tabId) })
+      break
+    case 'whiteboard':
+      out = defined({
+        kind: 'whiteboard',
+        elementIds: selection.elementIds?.length ? selection.elementIds.map((e) => e.slice(0, 64)).slice(0, 20) : undefined,
+      })
+      break
+    case 'design': {
+      const nodeId = selection.nodePath?.[selection.nodePath.length - 1]
+      out = defined({ kind: 'design', pageId: clip(selection.pageId), boardId: clip(selection.boardId), nodeId: clip(nodeId) })
+      break
+    }
+  }
+  return artifactPresenceSelectionSchema.safeParse(out).success ? out : null
+}
+
+// ---- identity: colours and anonymous labels, deterministic per seed
+
+/** Distinct hues that read on light and dark canvases alike. */
+export const ARTIFACT_PEER_COLORS = [
+  '#e5484d',
+  '#f76b15',
+  '#d6a100',
+  '#30a46c',
+  '#12a594',
+  '#0090ff',
+  '#3e63dd',
+  '#8e4ec6',
+  '#d6409f',
+  '#a18072',
+] as const
+
+const ANIMALS = [
+  'Otter', 'Fox', 'Heron', 'Lynx', 'Panda', 'Koala', 'Badger', 'Falcon', 'Moose', 'Gecko',
+  'Walrus', 'Bison', 'Ibis', 'Marten', 'Puffin', 'Quokka', 'Raven', 'Seal', 'Tapir', 'Yak',
+] as const
+
+/** FNV-1a gives each peer id a stable display color. */
+function hash(seed: string) {
+  let h = 0x811c9dc5
+  for (let i = 0; i < seed.length; i++) {
+    h ^= seed.charCodeAt(i)
+    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193)
+  }
+  return h >>> 0
+}
+
+export const artifactPeerColor = (seed: string) => ARTIFACT_PEER_COLORS[hash(seed) % ARTIFACT_PEER_COLORS.length]!
+export const anonymousPeerLabel = (seed: string) => `Anonymous ${ANIMALS[hash(`a:${seed}`) % ANIMALS.length]}`
+
+/** Two-letter initials for an avatar. */
+export function peerInitials(name: string): string {
+  const words = name.replace(/^Anonymous\s+/, '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)
+  if (!words.length) return '?'
+  const letters = words.length === 1 ? words[0]!.slice(0, 2) : `${words[0]![0]}${words[words.length - 1]![0]}`
+  return letters.toUpperCase()
+}
+
+// ---- SSE parsing, shared by the browser client and tests
+
+/** Splits an SSE byte stream's text into frames; keeps the unfinished tail for the next chunk. */
+export function parseSseChunk(buffer: string): { frames: Array<{ event: string; data: string }>; rest: string } {
+  const frames: Array<{ event: string; data: string }> = []
+  const parts = buffer.split(/\r?\n\r?\n/)
+  const rest = parts.pop() ?? ''
+  for (const part of parts) {
+    let event = 'message'
+    const data: string[] = []
+    for (const line of part.split(/\r?\n/)) {
+      if (!line || line.startsWith(':')) continue
+      const colon = line.indexOf(':')
+      const field = colon < 0 ? line : line.slice(0, colon)
+      const value = colon < 0 ? '' : line.slice(colon + 1).replace(/^ /, '')
+      if (field === 'event') event = value
+      else if (field === 'data') data.push(value)
+    }
+    if (data.length) frames.push({ event, data: data.join('\n') })
+  }
+  return { frames, rest }
+}

diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/whiteboard-layout.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/whiteboard-layout.ts
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..399dfbd2a

+++ b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/whiteboard-layout.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,299 @@
+/**
+ * Tidy up: lays a selection out afresh as a grid, a tree or a layered left-to-right flow.
+ *
+ * The things laid out are the selection's units (`arrangeUnits`: each element alone, a group as
+ * one, arrows and locked elements left where they are). A section in the selection brings its
+ * members, which are then not laid out separately. Tree and flow follow the bound arrows between
+ * units (any arrow on the board joining two of them, selected or not); a cycle is broken at the
+ * arrow that closes it, in reading order. The result starts at the selection's top-left, every
+ * unit on the 8-unit grid, spaced as the guide advises (40 between neighbours, 80 between ranks).
+ * Every function takes a valid board and returns a valid one (the same object when nothing would
+ * move). Pure: no DOM.
+ */
+
+import { applyDeltas, arrangeUnits, type ArrangeUnit } from './whiteboard-arrange'
+import { sectionMembers, unionRects, type GeometryContext } from './whiteboard-geometry'
+import { isBoundEnd, type Point, type WhiteboardBoard } from './whiteboard'
+
+export const TIDY_MODES = ['grid', 'tree', 'flow'] as const
+export type TidyMode = (typeof TIDY_MODES)[number]
+
+/** Between neighbours in a row, column or rank. */
+export const TIDY_GAP = 40
+/** Between ranks of a tree or flow: room for an arrow and its label. */
+export const TIDY_RANK_GAP = 80
+
+const GRID = 8
+const snap = (v: number) => Math.round(v / GRID) * GRID
+
+type Unit = ArrangeUnit
+
+/** The units to lay out: members of a selected section travel with it rather than on their own. */
+function tidyUnits(board: WhiteboardBoard, ids: readonly string[], ctx: GeometryContext): Unit[] {
+  const chosen = new Set(ids)
+  const carried = new Set<string>()
+  for (const element of board.elements) {
+    if (element.type !== 'section' || !chosen.has(element.id)) continue
+    for (const member of sectionMembers(board.elements, element, ctx)) carried.add(member)
+  }
+  return arrangeUnits(board, ids, ctx).filter((unit) => !unit.ids.every((id) => carried.has(id)))
+}
+
+/** Units in reading order: rows (by overlapping vertical extent), then left to right. */
+export function readingOrder<T extends { bounds: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number } }>(units: readonly T[]): T[] {
+  const byTop = [...units].sort((a, b) => a.bounds.y - b.bounds.y || a.bounds.x - b.bounds.x)
+  const rows: T[][] = []
+  let rowBottom = -Infinity
+  for (const unit of byTop) {
+    // A unit whose middle is above the bottom of the row's first unit joins that row.
+    if (rows.length && unit.bounds.y + unit.bounds.h / 2 <= rowBottom) rows[rows.length - 1].push(unit)
+    else {
+      rows.push([unit])
+      rowBottom = unit.bounds.y + unit.bounds.h
+    }
+  }
+  return rows.flatMap((row) => row.sort((a, b) => a.bounds.x - b.bounds.x))
+}
+
+/** Edges between units (by index), from every bound arrow on the board joining two different ones. */
+function unitEdges(board: WhiteboardBoard, units: readonly Unit[]): Array<[number, number]> {
+  const owner = new Map<string, number>()
+  units.forEach((unit, i) => unit.ids.forEach((id) => owner.set(id, i)))
+  const seen = new Set<string>()
+  const edges: Array<[number, number]> = []
+  for (const element of board.elements) {
+    if (element.type !== 'arrow' || !isBoundEnd(element.from) || !isBoundEnd(element.to)) continue
+    const a = owner.get(element.from.elementId)
+    const b = owner.get(element.to.elementId)
+    if (a === undefined || b === undefined || a === b || seen.has(`${a}>${b}`)) continue
+    seen.add(`${a}>${b}`)
+    edges.push([a, b])
+  }
+  return edges
+}
+
+/**
+ * Drops the edges that close a cycle, leaving a DAG. The depth-first search starts from the units
+ * nothing points at, then from those that point out the most, then in reading order, so in a
+ * loop the unit that looks most like a start keeps its outgoing arrows.
+ */
+function acyclic(count: number, edges: ReadonlyArray<[number, number]>): Array<[number, number]> {
+  const out: number[][] = Array.from({ length: count }, () => [])
+  const balance = new Array<number>(count).fill(0)
+  const indegree = new Array<number>(count).fill(0)
+  for (const [a, b] of edges) {
+    out[a].push(b)
+    balance[a]++
+    balance[b]--
+    indegree[b]++
+  }
+  const order = Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => i).sort(
+    (a, b) => Number(indegree[a] > 0) - Number(indegree[b] > 0) || balance[b] - balance[a] || a - b,
+  )
+  const state = new Uint8Array(count) // 0 new, 1 on the stack, 2 done
+  const kept: Array<[number, number]> = []
+  for (const root of order) {
+    if (state[root]) continue
+    // Iterative DFS: [node, next child index].
+    const stack: Array<[number, number]> = [[root, 0]]
+    state[root] = 1
+    while (stack.length) {
+      const top = stack[stack.length - 1]
+      const [node, k] = top
+      if (k >= out[node].length) {
+        state[node] = 2
+        stack.pop()
+        continue
+      }
+      top[1]++
+      const child = out[node][k]
+      if (state[child] === 1) continue // a back edge: drop it
+      kept.push([node, child])
+      if (state[child] === 0) {
+        state[child] = 1
+        stack.push([child, 0])
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  return kept
+}
+
+/** Moves each unit so its top-left lands on `targets[i]`. */
+function placeUnits(board: WhiteboardBoard, units: readonly Unit[], targets: readonly Point[], ctx: GeometryContext) {
+  const moves = units.map((unit, i) => ({ unit, dx: targets[i].x - unit.bounds.x, dy: targets[i].y - unit.bounds.y }))
+  if (moves.every((move) => Math.abs(move.dx) < 0.05 && Math.abs(move.dy) < 0.05)) return board
+  return applyDeltas(board, moves, ctx)
+}
+
+/** A near-square grid in reading order, each unit centred in an equal cell. */
+function gridTargets(units: readonly Unit[], origin: Point): Point[] {
+  const cols = Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(units.length))
+  const cellW = Math.max(...units.map((u) => u.bounds.w))
+  const cellH = Math.max(...units.map((u) => u.bounds.h))
+  return units.map((unit, i) => {
+    const col = i % cols
+    const row = Math.floor(i / cols)
+    return {
+      x: snap(origin.x + col * (cellW + TIDY_GAP) + (cellW - unit.bounds.w) / 2),
+      y: snap(origin.y + row * (cellH + TIDY_GAP) + (cellH - unit.bounds.h) / 2),
+    }
+  })
+}
+
+/**
+ * A top-down tree: roots are units nothing points at (or, in a pure cycle, the first in reading
+ * order), each child under the parent that reaches it first; a parent is centred over its children.
+ */
+function treeTargets(units: readonly Unit[], edges: ReadonlyArray<[number, number]>, origin: Point): Point[] {
+  const n = units.length
+  const dag = acyclic(n, edges)
+  const children: number[][] = Array.from({ length: n }, () => [])
+  const out: number[][] = Array.from({ length: n }, () => [])
+  const placed = new Uint8Array(n)
+  const roots: number[] = []
+  const incoming = new Uint8Array(n)
+  for (const [a, b] of dag) {
+    incoming[b] = 1
+    out[a].push(b)
+  }
+  const visit = (root: number) => {
+    // Breadth-first, so a unit hangs under the shallowest parent that reaches it.
+    placed[root] = 1
+    roots.push(root)
+    const queue = [root]
+    for (let q = 0; q < queue.length; q++) {
+      const node = queue[q]
+      const next = out[node].filter((b) => !placed[b])
+      next.sort((a, b) => units[a].bounds.x - units[b].bounds.x || units[a].bounds.y - units[b].bounds.y)
+      for (const child of next) {
+        if (placed[child]) continue
+        placed[child] = 1
+        children[node].push(child)
+        queue.push(child)
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (!incoming[i] && !placed[i]) visit(i)
+  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (!placed[i]) visit(i)
+  // Depth of each node and the height of each depth's row.
+  const depth = new Array<number>(n).fill(0)
+  const rowH: number[] = []
+  const walk = (node: number, d: number) => {
+    depth[node] = d
+    rowH[d] = Math.max(rowH[d] ?? 0, units[node].bounds.h)
+    for (const child of children[node]) walk(child, d + 1)
+  }
+  for (const root of roots) walk(root, 0)
+  const rowY: number[] = []
+  rowH.forEach((h, d) => (rowY[d] = d === 0 ? origin.y : rowY[d - 1] + rowH[d - 1] + TIDY_RANK_GAP))
+  // Subtree widths, then left-to-right placement.
+  const width = new Array<number>(n).fill(0)
+  const measure = (node: number): number => {
+    const kids = children[node].map(measure)
+    const span = kids.reduce((s, w) => s + w, 0) + Math.max(0, kids.length - 1) * TIDY_GAP
+    width[node] = Math.max(units[node].bounds.w, span)
+    return width[node]
+  }
+  const targets: Point[] = new Array(n)
+  const place = (node: number, left: number) => {
+    const w = units[node].bounds.w
+    targets[node] = { x: snap(left + (width[node] - w) / 2), y: snap(rowY[depth[node]]) }
+    const span = children[node].reduce((s, c) => s + width[c], 0) + Math.max(0, children[node].length - 1) * TIDY_GAP
+    let x = left + (width[node] - span) / 2
+    for (const child of children[node]) {
+      place(child, x)
+      x += width[child] + TIDY_GAP
+    }
+  }
+  let left = origin.x
+  for (const root of roots) {
+    measure(root)
+    place(root, left)
+    left += width[root] + TIDY_RANK_GAP
+  }
+  return targets
+}
+
+/**
+ * A layered flow, left to right: each unit's rank is the longest chain of arrows into it, ranks
+ * sit `TIDY_RANK_GAP` apart, and within a rank units are ordered by where their predecessors sit
+ * (then by their old height on the board) and centred on one midline.
+ */
+function flowTargets(units: readonly Unit[], edges: ReadonlyArray<[number, number]>, origin: Point): Point[] {
+  const n = units.length
+  const dag = acyclic(n, edges)
+  const preds: number[][] = Array.from({ length: n }, () => [])
+  const succs: number[][] = Array.from({ length: n }, () => [])
+  const indegree = new Array<number>(n).fill(0)
+  for (const [a, b] of dag) {
+    preds[b].push(a)
+    succs[a].push(b)
+    indegree[b]++
+  }
+  const rank = new Array<number>(n).fill(0)
+  const queue: number[] = []
+  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (indegree[i] === 0) queue.push(i)
+  for (let q = 0; q < queue.length; q++) {
+    const node = queue[q]
+    for (const next of succs[node]) {
+      rank[next] = Math.max(rank[next], rank[node] + 1)
+      if (--indegree[next] === 0) queue.push(next)
+    }
+  }
+  const ranks: number[][] = []
+  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) (ranks[rank[i]] ??= []).push(i)
+  const slot = new Array<number>(n).fill(0)
+  ranks.forEach((members, r) => {
+    const key = (i: number) => {
+      if (r === 0 || preds[i].length === 0) return Infinity
+      return preds[i].reduce((s, p) => s + slot[p], 0) / preds[i].length
+    }
+    members.sort((a, b) => {
+      const ka = key(a)
+      const kb = key(b)
+      if (ka !== kb) return ka === Infinity ? 1 : kb === Infinity ? -1 : ka - kb
+      return units[a].bounds.y - units[b].bounds.y || units[a].bounds.x - units[b].bounds.x
+    })
+    members.forEach((i, k) => (slot[i] = k))
+  })
+  const rankW = ranks.map((members) => Math.max(...members.map((i) => units[i].bounds.w)))
+  const rankH = ranks.map(
+    (members) => members.reduce((s, i) => s + units[i].bounds.h, 0) + (members.length - 1) * TIDY_GAP,
+  )
+  const tallest = Math.max(...rankH)
+  const targets: Point[] = new Array(n)
+  let x = origin.x
+  ranks.forEach((members, r) => {
+    let y = origin.y + (tallest - rankH[r]) / 2
+    for (const i of members) {
+      const u = units[i].bounds
+      targets[i] = { x: snap(x + (rankW[r] - u.w) / 2), y: snap(y) }
+      y += u.h + TIDY_GAP
+    }
+    x += rankW[r] + TIDY_RANK_GAP
+  })
+  return targets
+}
+
+/**
+ * Lays the selection out as a grid, a tree or a flow (see the file comment). Needs two or more
+ * unlocked units; returns `board` itself when there are fewer or nothing would move.
+ */
+export function tidyElements(
+  board: WhiteboardBoard,
+  ids: readonly string[],
+  mode: TidyMode,
+  ctx: GeometryContext,
+): WhiteboardBoard {
+  const units = readingOrder(tidyUnits(board, ids, ctx))
+  if (units.length < 2) return board
+  const all = unionRects(units.map((unit) => unit.bounds))!
+  const origin = { x: snap(all.x), y: snap(all.y) }
+  const targets =
+    mode === 'grid'
+      ? gridTargets(units, origin)
+      : mode === 'tree'
+        ? treeTargets(units, unitEdges(board, units), origin)
+        : flowTargets(units, unitEdges(board, units), origin)
+  return placeUnits(board, units, targets, ctx)
+}

+import {
+  collectMains,
+  type Design,
+  type DesignNode,
+  type FrameNode,
+  type InstanceOverride,
+} from './design'
+import { allIds, findNode, freshId, getBoard, insertNode, removeBoard, removeNode, updateBoard, walkNodes } from './design-tree'
+
+/**
+ * Components. A MAIN component is a frame with `component: true`, anywhere in the design. An
+ * INSTANCE is a frame with `instanceOf: <main id>` and no children of its own: it draws a copy of
+ * the main, so editing the main restyles every instance. An instance keeps its own place and
+ * size (x, y, w, h, grow, hidden, name, linkTo) and may override CONTENT only: a text's `text`,
+ * a button's `label`, an image's `src`/`placeholder`/`alt`, keyed by the main's node id. A stale
+ * override (its node gone from the main) is ignored, never an error.
+ *
+ * In the drawn copy every inner id is `<instance id>~<main node id>`: `~` is never in a stored
+ * id, so the canvas can tell an instance's inside from real nodes and select the instance.
+ */
+
+export const INSTANCE_SEP = '~'
+
+/** Nested instances deeper than this draw empty (validation already refuses cycles). */
+const MAX_INSTANCE_DEPTH = 8
+
+export function mainsInSubtree(node: DesignNode): string[] {
+  const out: string[] = []
+  for (const { node: current } of walkNodes(node)) if (current.type === 'frame' && current.component) out.push(current.id)
+  return out
+}
+
+export function isInstance(node: DesignNode): node is FrameNode & { instanceOf: string } {
+  return node.type === 'frame' && node.instanceOf !== undefined
+}
+
+/** For a drawn id `inst~child`, the instance id (outermost) and the main node id under it. */
+export function splitInstanceId(drawnId: string): { instanceId: string; innerId: string | null } {
+  const at = drawnId.indexOf(INSTANCE_SEP)
+  if (at === -1) return { instanceId: drawnId, innerId: null }
+  return { instanceId: drawnId.slice(0, at), innerId: drawnId.slice(at + 1) }
+}
+
+function applyOverride(node: DesignNode, override: InstanceOverride | undefined): DesignNode {
+  if (!override) return node
+  if (node.type === 'text' && override.text !== undefined) return { ...node, text: override.text }
+  if (node.type === 'button' && override.label !== undefined) return { ...node, label: override.label }
+  if (node.type === 'image') {
+    const next = { ...node }
+    if (override.src !== undefined) {
+      next.src = override.src
+      delete next.placeholder
+    } else if (override.placeholder !== undefined) {
+      next.placeholder = override.placeholder
+      delete next.src
+    }
+    if (override.alt !== undefined) next.alt = override.alt
+    return next
+  }
+  return node
+}
+
+/**
+ * The frame an instance draws: the main's styling and children (ids prefixed, overrides
+ * applied, nested instances resolved), with the instance's own id, place and size. A missing
+ * main draws an empty frame.
+ */
+export function resolveInstance(
+  instance: FrameNode,
+  mains: ReadonlyMap<string, FrameNode>,
+  depth = 0,
+): FrameNode {
+  const main = instance.instanceOf !== undefined ? mains.get(instance.instanceOf) : undefined
+  const own = {
+    id: instance.id,
+    name: instance.name ?? main?.name,
+    hidden: instance.hidden,
+    x: instance.x,
+    y: instance.y,
+  }
+  if (!main || depth >= MAX_INSTANCE_DEPTH) {
+    return { ...instance, ...own, children: [] }
+  }
+  const prefix = (node: DesignNode, base: string, overrides: FrameNode['overrides']): DesignNode => {
+    const id = `${base}${INSTANCE_SEP}${node.id}`
+    const shown = applyOverride(node, overrides?.[node.id])
+    if (shown.type !== 'frame') return { ...shown, id }
+    if (shown.instanceOf !== undefined) {
+      const inner = resolveInstance({ ...shown, id }, mains, depth + 1)
+      return inner
+    }
+    return { ...shown, id, component: undefined, children: shown.children.map((child) => prefix(child, base, overrides)) }
+  }
+  const { component: _main, ...style } = main
+  const resolved: FrameNode = {
+    ...style,
+    ...own,
+    w: instance.w ?? main.w,
+    h: instance.h ?? main.h,
+    grow: instance.grow ?? main.grow,
+    minW: instance.minW ?? main.minW,
+    maxW: instance.maxW ?? main.maxW,
+    minH: instance.minH ?? main.minH,
+    maxH: instance.maxH ?? main.maxH,
+    linkTo: instance.linkTo ?? main.linkTo,
+    // The transition goes with whichever link is drawn.
+    transition: instance.linkTo !== undefined ? instance.transition : main.transition,
+    instanceOf: main.id,
+    overrides: undefined,
+    children: main.children.map((child) => prefix(child, instance.id, instance.overrides)),
+  }
+  const record = resolved as Record<string, unknown>
+  for (const key of Object.keys(record)) if (record[key] === undefined) delete record[key]
+  return resolved
+}
+
+/** The nodes of a main an instance can override, in reading order. */
+export function overrideTargets(main: FrameNode): Array<{ id: string; node: DesignNode }> {
+  const out: Array<{ id: string; node: DesignNode }> = []
+  for (const { node } of walkNodes(main)) {
+    if (node === main) continue
+    if (node.type === 'text' || node.type === 'button' || node.type === 'image') out.push({ id: node.id, node })
+  }
+  return out
+}
+
+/** Sets (or with `undefined` fields, clears) one override on an instance. */
+export function setOverride(
+  design: Design,
+  boardId: string,
+  instanceId: string,
+  mainNodeId: string,
+  patch: InstanceOverride | null,
+): Design {
+  return updateBoard(design, boardId, (board) => {
+    const hit = findNode(board.root, instanceId)
+    if (!hit || !isInstance(hit.node)) return board
+    const overrides = { ...(hit.node.overrides ?? {}) }
+    const merged: InstanceOverride = patch === null ? {} : { ...(overrides[mainNodeId] ?? {}), ...patch }
+    for (const key of Object.keys(merged) as Array<keyof InstanceOverride>) if (merged[key] === undefined) delete merged[key]
+    if (merged.src !== undefined && patch?.placeholder !== undefined) delete merged.src
+    if (merged.placeholder !== undefined && patch?.src !== undefined) delete merged.placeholder
+    if (Object.keys(merged).length === 0) delete overrides[mainNodeId]
+    else overrides[mainNodeId] = merged
+    const next: FrameNode = { ...hit.node, overrides: Object.keys(overrides).length ? overrides : undefined }
+    if (next.overrides === undefined) delete next.overrides
+    return { ...board, root: swap(board.root, instanceId, next) as FrameNode }
+  })
+}
+
+function swap(root: DesignNode, nodeId: string, node: DesignNode): DesignNode {
+  if (root.id === nodeId) return node
+  if (root.type !== 'frame') return root
+  let changed = false
+  const children = root.children.map((child) => {
+    const next = swap(child, nodeId, node)
+    if (next !== child) changed = true
+    return next
+  })
+  return changed ? { ...root, children } : root
+}
+
+/** Marks a frame as a main component (or unmarks it, detaching its instances first). */
+export function setComponent(design: Design, boardId: string, frameId: string, on: boolean): Design {
+  const board = getBoard(design, boardId)
+  const hit = board ? findNode(board.root, frameId) : null
+  if (!hit || hit.node.type !== 'frame' || hit.node.instanceOf !== undefined) return design
+  if (Boolean(hit.node.component) === on) return design
+  const base = on ? design : detachInstancesOf(design, [frameId])
+  return updateBoard(base, boardId, (current) => {
+    const found = findNode(current.root, frameId)
+    if (!found || found.node.type !== 'frame') return current
+    const next: FrameNode = { ...found.node }
+    if (on) next.component = true
+    else delete next.component
+    return { ...current, root: swap(current.root, frameId, next) as FrameNode }
+  })
+}
+
+/** A new instance node of `mainId` (not yet placed). */
+export function instanceNode(design: Design, mainId: string, options: { id?: string; overrides?: FrameNode['overrides'] } = {}): FrameNode | null {
+  const main = collectMains(design.boards).get(mainId)
+  if (!main) return null
+  const id = options.id ?? freshId(allIds(design), `${main.name ?? main.id}-instance`)
+  const node: FrameNode = { type: 'frame', id, layout: main.layout, instanceOf: mainId, children: [] }
+  if (main.name) node.name = main.name
+  if (options.overrides && Object.keys(options.overrides).length) node.overrides = options.overrides
+  return node
+}
+
+/** Places an instance of `mainId` in frame `parentId`; returns its id. */
+export function insertInstance(
+  design: Design,
+  boardId: string,
+  parentId: string,
+  mainId: string,
+  index?: number,
+): { design: Design; nodeId: string | null } {
+  const node = instanceNode(design, mainId)
+  const board = getBoard(design, boardId)
+  const parent = board ? findNode(board.root, parentId)?.node : undefined
+  if (!node || !parent || parent.type !== 'frame' || parent.instanceOf !== undefined) return { design, nodeId: null }
+  if (parent.layout === 'absolute') Object.assign(node, { x: 24, y: 24 })
+  return { design: insertNode(design, boardId, parentId, node, index), nodeId: node.id }
+}
+
+/**
+ * The instance turned into plain nodes: the main's subtree with overrides baked in and fresh
+ * ids. One level: an instance inside the main stays an instance.
+ */
+function detachedCopy(instance: FrameNode, main: FrameNode, taken: Set<string>): FrameNode {
+  const copy = (node: DesignNode): DesignNode => {
+    const shown = applyOverride(node, instance.overrides?.[node.id])
+    const id = freshId(taken, shown.id.replace(/-\d+$/, ''))
+    if (shown.type !== 'frame') return { ...shown, id }
+    const { component: _c, ...rest } = shown
+    if (shown.instanceOf !== undefined) return { ...rest, id, children: [] }
+    return { ...rest, id, children: shown.children.map(copy) }
+  }
+  const { component: _c, instanceOf: _i, overrides: _o, ...style } = main
+  const out: FrameNode = {
+    ...style,
+    id: instance.id,
+    children: main.children.map(copy),
+  }
+  for (const key of ['name', 'hidden', 'x', 'y', 'w', 'h', 'grow', 'linkTo', 'minW', 'maxW', 'minH', 'maxH'] as const) {
+    const value = instance[key] ?? main[key]
+    if (value !== undefined) (out as Record<string, unknown>)[key] = value
+    else delete (out as Record<string, unknown>)[key]
+  }
+  // The transition goes with whichever link the copy keeps.
+  const transition = instance.linkTo !== undefined ? instance.transition : main.transition
+  if (transition !== undefined) out.transition = transition
+  else delete out.transition
+  return out
+}
+
+/** Detaches one instance (it keeps its id); the design is unchanged when it is not one. */
+export function detachInstance(design: Design, boardId: string, instanceId: string): Design {
+  const board = getBoard(design, boardId)
+  const hit = board ? findNode(board.root, instanceId) : null
+  if (!hit || !isInstance(hit.node)) return design
+  const main = collectMains(design.boards).get(hit.node.instanceOf)
+  const taken = allIds(design)
+  const plain: FrameNode = main
+    ? detachedCopy(hit.node, main, taken)
+    : (({ instanceOf: _i, overrides: _o, ...rest }) => rest)(hit.node)
+  return updateBoard(design, boardId, (current) => ({ ...current, root: swap(current.root, instanceId, plain) as FrameNode }))
+}
+
+/** Detaches every instance of the given mains (before those mains are deleted). */
+export function detachInstancesOf(design: Design, mainIds: string[]): Design {
+  if (mainIds.length === 0) return design
+  const doomed = new Set(mainIds)
+  let next = design
+  for (let pass = 0; pass < 8; pass += 1) {
+    const found: Array<{ boardId: string; nodeId: string }> = []
+    for (const board of next.boards) {
+      for (const { node } of walkNodes(board.root)) {
+        if (isInstance(node) && doomed.has(node.instanceOf)) found.push({ boardId: board.id, nodeId: node.id })
+      }
+    }
+    if (found.length === 0) break
+    for (const entry of found) next = detachInstance(next, entry.boardId, entry.nodeId)
+  }
+  return next
+}
+
+/** removeNode that first detaches instances of any main component inside the removed subtree. */
+export function removeNodeDetaching(design: Design, boardId: string, nodeId: string): Design {
+  const board = getBoard(design, boardId)
+  const hit = board ? findNode(board.root, nodeId) : null
+  if (!hit?.parent) return design
+  return removeNode(detachInstancesOf(design, mainsInSubtree(hit.node)), boardId, nodeId)
+}
+
+/** removeBoard that first detaches instances of the board's main components elsewhere. */
+export function removeBoardDetaching(design: Design, boardId: string): Design {
+  const board = getBoard(design, boardId)
+  if (!board) return design
+  return removeBoard(detachInstancesOf(design, mainsInSubtree(board.root)), boardId)
+}

+/**
+ * Board checks (round 5): what a reader would notice is wrong with a board, measured the way the
+ * editor draws it. The editor's Checks panel lists them (each with "Ask Buffy to fix", carrying
+ * the element ids) and the agent's read returns them, so both see the same issues:
+ *   overlap          two boxes cover each other (not a sticker on a note, not text on a shape,
+ *                    not members of one group, never a section and what sits in it);
+ *   text-overflow    a note's, shape's or table cell's text does not fit its box even at the
+ *                    smallest size (it is clipped);
+ *   tiny-text        the text only fits by shrinking below 12 px;
+ *   section-edge     an element straddles a section's edge: half in, so it looks like a member
+ *                    but does not move with the section (or sticks out of it);
+ *   dangling-arrow   an arrow end points at nothing (a free point, not an element);
+ *   duplicate-arrow  two arrows join the same two elements the same way;
+ *   low-contrast     a custom (#rrggbb) line colour that almost vanishes on the light or dark canvas.
+ * Hidden elements are not checked. Pure and deterministic; O(n log n + overlaps) with a sweep, so
+ * it runs off the drag path (the panel computes it when shown, the server on read).
+ */
+
+import { tableCellRect, tableMerges, tableSize } from './whiteboard-arrange'
+import {
+  center,
+  elementBounds,
+  estimateMeasure,
+  fitTextInBox,
+  geometryContext,
+  NOTE_PADDING,
+  pointInRect,
+  rectContains,
+  shapeTextBox,
+  wrapText,
+  type GeometryContext,
+  type Measure,
+} from './whiteboard-geometry'
+import { visibleBoard } from './whiteboard-r5'
+import { plainRichText } from './whiteboard-richtext'
+import { isBoundEnd, isHexColor, TEXT_SIZE_PX, type Rect, type WhiteboardBoard, type WhiteboardElement } from './whiteboard'
+
+export const LINT_RULES = [
+  'overlap',
+  'text-overflow',
+  'tiny-text',
+  'section-edge',
+  'dangling-arrow',
+  'duplicate-arrow',
+  'low-contrast',
+] as const
+export type LintRule = (typeof LINT_RULES)[number]
+
+export type BoardIssue = {
+  rule: LintRule
+  /** The elements it is about (first the one to fix). */
+  ids: string[]
+  message: string
+  /** What would fix it, in a few words. */
+  fix: string
+}
+
+/** Most issues listed (the rest are counted). */
+export const MAX_BOARD_ISSUES = 60
+
+const NOTE_STEPS = [22, 18, 16, 14, 12, 10]
+const SHAPE_STEPS = [16, 14, 12, 10]
+const CELL_STEPS = [14, 13, 12, 11, 10]
+const TINY_PX = 12
+
+const area = (r: Rect) => Math.max(0, r.w) * Math.max(0, r.h)
+function overlapArea(a: Rect, b: Rect): number {
+  const w = Math.min(a.x + a.w, b.x + b.w) - Math.max(a.x, b.x)
+  const h = Math.min(a.y + a.h, b.y + b.h) - Math.max(a.y, b.y)
+  return w > 0 && h > 0 ? w * h : 0
+}
+
+function name(element: WhiteboardElement): string {
+  const text =
+    element.type === 'note' || element.type === 'text'
+      ? element.text
+      : element.type === 'shape'
+        ? element.text ?? ''
+        : element.type === 'section' || element.type === 'link'
+          ? element.title
+          : ''
+  const clean = plainRichText(text).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
+  const noun = element.type === 'stroke' ? 'sketch' : element.type === 'link' ? 'link card' : element.type
+  return clean ? `${noun} "${clean.length > 30 ? `${clean.slice(0, 29)}…` : clean}"` : `${noun} ${element.id}`
+}
+
+/** Relative luminance of `#rrggbb` (WCAG). */
+function luminance(hex: string): number {
+  const channel = (i: number) => {
+    const c = parseInt(hex.slice(i, i + 2), 16) / 255
+    return c <= 0.03928 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4
+  }
+  return 0.2126 * channel(1) + 0.7152 * channel(3) + 0.0722 * channel(5)
+}
+
+/** WCAG contrast ratio of two `#rrggbb` colours (1 to 21). */
+export function contrastRatio(a: string, b: string): number {
+  const [x, y] = [luminance(a), luminance(b)].sort((p, q) => q - p) as [number, number]
+  return (x + 0.05) / (y + 0.05)
+}
+
+/** The ink a custom fill gets so its text reads: near-black on light fills, near-white on dark. */
+export function inkOn(fill: string): string {
+  return contrastRatio(fill, '#1f2328') >= contrastRatio(fill, '#ffffff') ? '#1f2328' : '#ffffff'
+}
+
+/** The light and dark canvas backgrounds a line colour must show on. */
+export const CANVAS_BACKGROUNDS = ['#ffffff', '#1c1c1e'] as const
+const LINE_CONTRAST_MIN = 1.5
+
+/** Text fit: does `text` fit `box` at some step, and at which size. */
+function fit(text: string, box: Rect, measure: Measure, steps: readonly number[], fixed?: number) {
+  const plain = plainRichText(text)
+  if (fixed) {
+    const lines = plain.split('\n').flatMap((line) => wrapText(line, fixed, box.w, measure)).length
+    return { fits: lines * fixed * 1.3 <= box.h + 0.5, size: fixed }
+  }
+  const block = fitTextInBox(plain, box.w, box.h, measure, steps)
+  return { fits: block.lines.length * block.lineHeight <= box.h + 0.5, size: block.fontSize }
+}
+
+/** The checks for one board. */
+export function lintBoard(board: WhiteboardBoard, measure: Measure = estimateMeasure): { issues: BoardIssue[]; total: number } {
+  const ctx: GeometryContext = geometryContext(board.elements, measure)
+  const issues: BoardIssue[] = []
+  const add = (issue: BoardIssue) => issues.push(issue)
+  // As a reader sees it: hidden elements, and arrows bound to them, are not checked.
+  const shown = visibleBoard(board).elements
+  const bounds = new Map(shown.map((element) => [element.id, elementBounds(element, ctx)]))
+  const sections = shown.filter((element) => element.type === 'section')
+
+  // ---- text that does not fit
+  for (const element of shown) {
+    if (element.type === 'note' && element.text.trim()) {
+      const box = { x: 0, y: 0, w: element.w - 2 * NOTE_PADDING, h: element.h - 2 * NOTE_PADDING }
+      const result = fit(element.text, box, measure, NOTE_STEPS, element.fontSize ? TEXT_SIZE_PX[element.fontSize] : undefined)
+      if (!result.fits) add({ rule: 'text-overflow', ids: [element.id], message: `The text of ${name(element)} does not fit; it is cut off.`, fix: 'Make the note bigger, turn on auto-size, or shorten the text.' })
+      else if (!element.fontSize && result.size < TINY_PX) add({ rule: 'tiny-text', ids: [element.id], message: `${name(element)} only fits its text at ${result.size}px.`, fix: 'Make the note bigger or shorten the text.' })
+    } else if (element.type === 'shape' && element.text?.trim() && !element.timer) {
+      const box = shapeTextBox(element.kind, element)
+      const result = fit(element.text, box, measure, SHAPE_STEPS, element.fontSize ? TEXT_SIZE_PX[element.fontSize] : undefined)
+      if (!result.fits) add({ rule: 'text-overflow', ids: [element.id], message: `The label of ${name(element)} does not fit; it is cut off.`, fix: 'Make the shape bigger or shorten the label.' })
+      else if (!element.fontSize && result.size < TINY_PX) add({ rule: 'tiny-text', ids: [element.id], message: `${name(element)} only fits its label at ${result.size}px.`, fix: 'Make the shape bigger or shorten the label.' })
+    } else if (element.type === 'table') {
+      const { rows, cols } = tableSize(element)
+      const covered = new Set<string>()
+      for (const merge of tableMerges(element)) {
+        for (let r = merge.row; r < merge.row + merge.rows; r++) for (let c = merge.col; c < merge.col + merge.cols; c++) if (r !== merge.row || c !== merge.col) covered.add(`${r}:${c}`)
+      }
+      let worst: string | null = null
+      for (let r = 0; r < rows && !worst; r++) {
+        for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
+          const cell = element.rows[r]![c]!
+          if (!cell.trim() || covered.has(`${r}:${c}`)) continue
+          const rect = tableCellRect(element, r, c)
+          const result = fit(cell, { x: 0, y: 0, w: rect.w - 16, h: rect.h - 4 }, measure, CELL_STEPS)
+          if (!result.fits) {
+            worst = `row ${r + 1}, column ${c + 1}`
+            break
+          }
+        }
+      }
+      if (worst) add({ rule: 'text-overflow', ids: [element.id], message: `A cell of table ${element.id} (${worst}) is cut off.`, fix: 'Widen that column, add height to the row, or shorten the text.' })
+    }
+  }
+
+  // ---- overlaps (a sweep over x)
+  const boxes = shown.filter(
+    (element) => element.type === 'note' || element.type === 'shape' || element.type === 'image' || element.type === 'table' || element.type === 'link' || element.type === 'text',
+  )
+  const sorted = boxes.map((element) => ({ element, r: bounds.get(element.id)! })).sort((a, b) => a.r.x - b.r.x)
+  const sameGroup = (a: WhiteboardElement, b: WhiteboardElement) => !!a.groups?.some((group) => b.groups?.includes(group))
+  let overlaps = 0
+  for (let i = 0; i < sorted.length; i++) {
+    const a = sorted[i]!
+    for (let j = i + 1; j < sorted.length; j++) {
+      const b = sorted[j]!
+      if (b.r.x >= a.r.x + a.r.w) break
+      const shared = overlapArea(a.r, b.r)
+      if (!shared) continue
+      if (sameGroup(a.element, b.element)) continue
+      // Text laid over a box is a label, not a clash.
+      if ((a.element.type === 'text' && rectContains(b.r, a.r)) || (b.element.type === 'text' && rectContains(a.r, b.r))) continue
+      if (shared < 0.15 * Math.min(area(a.r), area(b.r))) continue
+      const [top, under] = board.elements.indexOf(a.element) > board.elements.indexOf(b.element) ? [a.element, b.element] : [b.element, a.element]
+      overlaps++
+      if (overlaps <= 30) add({ rule: 'overlap', ids: [top.id, under.id], message: `${name(top)} covers ${name(under)}.`, fix: 'Move one of them apart (40 units between neighbours), or use Tidy up.' })
+    }
+  }
+
+  // ---- half in a section
+  for (const element of shown) {
+    if (element.type === 'section' || element.type === 'arrow' || element.type === 'sticker' || element.type === 'stroke') continue
+    const r = bounds.get(element.id)!
+    const c = center(r)
+    for (const section of sections) {
+      const s = bounds.get(section.id)!
+      if (rectContains(s, r) || rectContains(r, s)) continue
+      const shared = overlapArea(r, s)
+      if (!shared) continue
+      const inside = pointInRect(c, s)
+      if (inside && shared > 0.5 * area(r)) {
+        add({ rule: 'section-edge', ids: [element.id, section.id], message: `${name(element)} sticks out of ${name(section)}.`, fix: 'Move it fully inside, or make the section bigger.' })
+      } else if (!inside && shared > 0.25 * area(r)) {
+        add({ rule: 'section-edge', ids: [element.id, section.id], message: `${name(element)} sits half in ${name(section)} but is not part of it (it will not move with it).`, fix: 'Move it fully into the section, or out of it.' })
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  // ---- arrows
+  const seen = new Map<string, string>()
+  for (const element of shown) {
+    if (element.type !== 'arrow') continue
+    const free = [element.from, element.to].filter((end) => !isBoundEnd(end)).length
+    if (free > 0) add({ rule: 'dangling-arrow', ids: [element.id], message: `Arrow ${element.label ? `"${element.label}" ` : ''}${element.id} has ${free === 2 ? 'both ends' : 'an end'} pointing at nothing.`, fix: 'Attach its ends to elements, or remove it.' })
+    else if (isBoundEnd(element.from) && isBoundEnd(element.to)) {
+      const key = `${element.from.elementId}>${element.to.elementId}`
+      const earlier = seen.get(key)
+      if (earlier) add({ rule: 'duplicate-arrow', ids: [element.id, earlier], message: `Arrows ${earlier} and ${element.id} join the same two elements.`, fix: 'Remove one of them.' })
+      else seen.set(key, element.id)
+    }
+    if (element.stroke && isHexColor(element.stroke)) {
+      const worst = Math.min(...CANVAS_BACKGROUNDS.map((bg) => contrastRatio(element.stroke!, bg)))
+      if (worst < LINE_CONTRAST_MIN) add({ rule: 'low-contrast', ids: [element.id], message: `Arrow ${element.id}'s colour ${element.stroke} almost vanishes on the ${contrastRatio(element.stroke, '#ffffff') < LINE_CONTRAST_MIN ? 'light' : 'dark'} canvas.`, fix: 'Use a colour name (it adapts to the theme) or a mid-tone hex.' })
+    }
+  }
+  for (const element of shown) {
+    const stroke = 'stroke' in element && element.type !== 'arrow' ? element.stroke : undefined
+    if (!stroke || !isHexColor(stroke)) continue
+    const fill = 'fill' in element ? element.fill : undefined
+    if (fill) continue // a filled body shows either way
+    const worst = Math.min(...CANVAS_BACKGROUNDS.map((bg) => contrastRatio(stroke, bg)))
+    if (worst < LINE_CONTRAST_MIN && element.type === 'section') add({ rule: 'low-contrast', ids: [element.id], message: `${name(element)}'s outline ${stroke} almost vanishes on one theme.`, fix: 'Use a colour name or a mid-tone hex.' })
+  }
+
+  const order = new Map(LINT_RULES.map((rule, i) => [rule, i]))
+  issues.sort((a, b) => order.get(a.rule)! - order.get(b.rule)!)
+  // Overlaps past the 30 listed still count towards the total.
+  return { issues: issues.slice(0, MAX_BOARD_ISSUES), total: issues.length + Math.max(0, overlaps - 30) }
+}
+
+/** The issues as plain lines (the agent's read, an Ask Buffy request). */
+export function formatBoardIssues(result: { issues: BoardIssue[]; total: number }): string[] {
+  const lines = result.issues.map((issue) => `- [${issue.rule}] ${issue.message} Fix: ${issue.fix} (ids: ${issue.ids.join(', ')})`)
+  if (result.total > result.issues.length) lines.push(`- … ${result.total - result.issues.length} more`)
+  return lines
+}

+/**
+ * Pure edits and geometry for a slide's free-position elements (text boxes, shapes, lines,
+ * arrows, images drawn above the layout). Shared by the editor (drag, resize, rotate, snap,
+ * keyboard nudges) and the agent's patch ops. Every edit returns a new deck, or its input
+ * unchanged when it cannot apply (callers check identity to tell a no-op).
+ */
+
+import {
+  DESIGN_SIZE,
+  ELEMENT_BOUNDS,
+  LIMITS,
+  type Deck,
+  type DeckAspect,
+  type ElementType,
+  type Slide,
+  type SlideElement,
+} from './slides'
+
+export type Box = { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }
+
+const clamp = (value: number, min: number, max: number) => Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value))
+const round = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 10) / 10
+
+/** A rotation in (-180, 180], rounded to a tenth of a degree; 0 means none. */
+export function normaliseRotation(degrees: number): number {
+  if (!Number.isFinite(degrees)) return 0
+  let value = degrees % 360
+  if (value > 180) value -= 360
+  if (value <= -180) value += 360
+  return round(value) === 0 ? 0 : round(value)
+}
+
+/** A box kept inside the schema's bounds, rounded to a tenth of a pixel. */
+export function clampBox(box: Box): Box {
+  const w = clamp(round(box.w), ELEMENT_BOUNDS.minSize, ELEMENT_BOUNDS.maxSize)
+  const h = clamp(round(box.h), ELEMENT_BOUNDS.minSize, ELEMENT_BOUNDS.maxSize)
+  return {
+    x: clamp(round(box.x), ELEMENT_BOUNDS.min, ELEMENT_BOUNDS.max),
+    y: clamp(round(box.y), ELEMENT_BOUNDS.min, ELEMENT_BOUNDS.max),
+    w,
+    h,
+  }
+}
+
+/** A fresh element id on the slide: `<type>-1`, `<type>-2`, … Deterministic. */
+export function uniqueElementId(slide: Slide, base: string): string {
+  const used = new Set((slide.elements ?? []).map((element) => element.id))
+  const stem = (base.replace(/-\d+$/, '') || 'element').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g, '').slice(0, LIMITS.idLength - 5) || 'element'
+  for (let n = 1; ; n++) {
+    const candidate = `${stem}-${n}`
+    if (!used.has(candidate)) return candidate
+  }
+}
+
+/** A new element of `type` centred on the canvas, with the look a person would start from. */
+export function defaultElement(type: ElementType, id: string, aspect: DeckAspect, src?: string): SlideElement {
+  const canvas = DESIGN_SIZE[aspect]
+  const centred = (w: number, h: number) => ({ x: round((canvas.width - w) / 2), y: round((canvas.height - h) / 2), w, h })
+  switch (type) {
+    case 'text':
+      return { id, type, ...centred(360, 64), text: 'Text', size: 28 }
+    case 'rect':
+      return { id, type, ...centred(240, 140), fill: 'tint', stroke: 'accent', strokeWidth: 2 }
+    case 'ellipse':
+      return { id, type, ...centred(180, 180), fill: 'tint', stroke: 'accent', strokeWidth: 2 }
+    case 'line':
+      return { id, type, ...centred(240, 20), stroke: 'ink', strokeWidth: 3 }
+    case 'arrow':
+      return { id, type, ...centred(240, 24), stroke: 'accent', strokeWidth: 3 }
+    case 'image':
+      return { id, type, ...centred(320, 200), src: src ?? '/Placeholder.svg', alt: 'Image' }
+    case 'icon':
+      return { id, type, ...centred(96, 96), icon: 'star', color: 'accent' }
+    case 'link':
+      return { id, type, ...centred(360, 112), url: 'https://example.com', title: 'Link title' }
+    case 'connector':
+      // Bound to nothing yet: `connectElements` makes a real one between two elements.
+      return { id, type, ...centred(240, 20), from: id, to: id, stroke: 'ink', strokeWidth: 2, head: 'end' }
+  }
+}
+
+type SlideEdit = (slide: Slide) => Slide
+
+function editSlide(deck: Deck, slideId: string, change: SlideEdit): Deck {
+  const index = deck.slides.findIndex((slide) => slide.id === slideId)
+  if (index === -1) return deck
+  const current = deck.slides[index]
+  const next = change(current)
+  if (next === current) return deck
+  const slides = [...deck.slides]
+  slides[index] = next
+  return { ...deck, slides }
+}
+
+/** The slide with its element list set; an empty list drops the field (an untouched slide stays byte-identical). */
+function withElements(slide: Slide, elements: SlideElement[]): Slide {
+  const next: Slide = { ...slide }
+  if (elements.length) next.elements = elements
+  else delete next.elements
+  return next
+}
+
+export type ElementResult = { ok: true; deck: Deck; id: string } | { ok: false; error: string }
+
+/**
+ * Add `element` on top of the slide (an id already used there, or none, gets a fresh one).
+ * Refuses an unknown slide or a slide at the element cap.
+ */
+export function addElement(deck: Deck, slideId: string, element: SlideElement): ElementResult {
+  const slide = deck.slides.find((item) => item.id === slideId)
+  if (!slide) return { ok: false, error: `there is no slide "${slideId}"` }
+  const elements = slide.elements ?? []
+  if (elements.length >= LIMITS.elements) return { ok: false, error: `a slide has at most ${LIMITS.elements} elements` }
+  const id = !element.id || elements.some((item) => item.id === element.id) ? uniqueElementId(slide, element.type) : element.id
+  const placed = { ...element, id, ...clampBox(element) } as SlideElement
+  if (placed.rotation !== undefined) {
+    const rotation = normaliseRotation(placed.rotation)
+    if (rotation) placed.rotation = rotation
+    else delete placed.rotation
+  }
+  return { ok: true, id, deck: editSlide(deck, slideId, (current) => withElements(current, [...elements, placed])) }
+}
+
+/** Fields a patch may change: anything but the id and the type; `null` clears an optional field. */
+export type ElementPatch = { [K in string]?: unknown }
+
+const REQUIRED_FIELDS = new Set(['id', 'type', 'x', 'y', 'w', 'h', 'src', 'alt', 'icon', 'url', 'title', 'from', 'to'])
+
+/**
+ * Merge `patch` into one element. Box fields are clamped to the bounds, rotation normalised;
+ * `null` deletes an optional field. Unknown fields are copied (the deck's schema refuses them
+ * when the caller validates). Returns the input deck for an unknown slide or element, or an
+ * edit that changes nothing.
+ */
+export function updateElement(deck: Deck, slideId: string, elementId: string, patch: ElementPatch): Deck {
+  return editSlide(deck, slideId, (slide) => {
+    const elements = slide.elements ?? []
+    const index = elements.findIndex((element) => element.id === elementId)
+    if (index === -1) return slide
+    const current = elements[index]
+    const next: Record<string, unknown> = { ...current }
+    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(patch)) {
+      if (key === 'id' || key === 'type') continue
+      if (value === null || value === undefined) {
+        if (!REQUIRED_FIELDS.has(key)) delete next[key]
+      } else next[key] = value
+    }
+    const box = clampBox(next as unknown as Box)
+    Object.assign(next, box)
+    if (typeof next.rotation === 'number') {
+      const rotation = normaliseRotation(next.rotation)
+      if (rotation) next.rotation = rotation
+      else delete next.rotation
+    }
+    if (JSON.stringify(next) === JSON.stringify(current)) return slide
+    const list = [...elements]
+    list[index] = next as unknown as SlideElement
+    return withElements(slide, list)
+  })
+}
+
+/** Remove the elements with these ids from the slide (unknown ids are ignored). */
+export function removeElements(deck: Deck, slideId: string, ids: readonly string[]): Deck {
+  const drop = new Set(ids)
+  return editSlide(deck, slideId, (slide) => {
+    const elements = slide.elements ?? []
+    const kept = elements.filter((element) => !drop.has(element.id))
+    return kept.length === elements.length ? slide : withElements(slide, kept)
+  })
+}
+
+export const ELEMENT_ORDERS = ['front', 'back', 'forward', 'backward'] as const
+export type ElementOrder = (typeof ELEMENT_ORDERS)[number]
+
+/** Change one element's stacking: to the front or back, or one step forward or backward. */
+export function reorderElement(deck: Deck, slideId: string, elementId: string, to: ElementOrder): Deck {
+  return editSlide(deck, slideId, (slide) => {
+    const elements = slide.elements ?? []
+    const from = elements.findIndex((element) => element.id === elementId)
+    if (from === -1) return slide
+    const last = elements.length - 1
+    const target =
+      to === 'front' ? last : to === 'back' ? 0 : to === 'forward' ? Math.min(last, from + 1) : Math.max(0, from - 1)
+    if (target === from) return slide
+    const list = [...elements]
+    const [moved] = list.splice(from, 1)
+    list.splice(target, 0, moved)
+    return withElements(slide, list)
+  })
+}
+
+/** Move these elements by (dx, dy), each clamped to the bounds. */
+export function moveElements(deck: Deck, slideId: string, ids: readonly string[], dx: number, dy: number): Deck {
+  if (!dx && !dy) return deck
+  const move = new Set(ids)
+  return editSlide(deck, slideId, (slide) => {
+    const elements = slide.elements ?? []
+    let changed = false
+    const list = elements.map((element) => {
+      if (!move.has(element.id)) return element
+      const box = clampBox({ ...element, x: element.x + dx, y: element.y + dy })
+      if (box.x === element.x && box.y === element.y) return element
+      changed = true
+      return { ...element, x: box.x, y: box.y }
+    })
+    return changed ? withElements(slide, list) : slide
+  })
+}
+
+/**
+ * Copy these elements on top, offset by `offset` px, under fresh ids. Returns the copies' ids
+ * (empty when nothing was copied, e.g. at the element cap).
+ */
+export function duplicateElements(
+  deck: Deck,
+  slideId: string,
+  ids: readonly string[],
+  offset = 16,
+): { deck: Deck; ids: string[] } {
+  const slide = deck.slides.find((item) => item.id === slideId)
+  if (!slide) return { deck, ids: [] }
+  const elements = slide.elements ?? []
+  const sources = elements.filter((element) => ids.includes(element.id))
+  if (!sources.length || elements.length + sources.length > LIMITS.elements) return { deck, ids: [] }
+  let working: Slide = slide
+  const copies: SlideElement[] = []
+  for (const source of sources) {
+    const id = uniqueElementId(working, source.id)
+    const box = clampBox({ ...source, x: source.x + offset, y: source.y + offset })
+    const copy = { ...structuredClone(source), id, x: box.x, y: box.y } as SlideElement
+    copies.push(copy)
+    working = withElements(working, [...(working.elements ?? []), copy])
+  }
+  return { deck: editSlide(deck, slideId, () => working), ids: copies.map((copy) => copy.id) }
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Geometry for the editor's handles.
+
+export const RESIZE_HANDLES = ['nw', 'n', 'ne', 'e', 'se', 's', 'sw', 'w'] as const
+export type ResizeHandle = (typeof RESIZE_HANDLES)[number]
+
+/**
+ * A box resized by dragging `handle` by (dx, dy) in design pixels (unrotated). `keepRatio`
+ * scales both sides from a corner. The opposite edge stays put; a side never drops below `min`.
+ */
+export function resizeBox(box: Box, handle: ResizeHandle, dx: number, dy: number, keepRatio = false, min = 8): Box {
+  let { x, y, w, h } = box
+  const west = handle.includes('w')
+  const east = handle.includes('e')
+  const north = handle.includes('n')
+  const south = handle.includes('s')
+  if (east) w = Math.max(min, box.w + dx)
+  if (west) w = Math.max(min, box.w - dx)
+  if (south) h = Math.max(min, box.h + dy)
+  if (north) h = Math.max(min, box.h - dy)
+  if (keepRatio && (east || west) && (north || south) && box.w > 0 && box.h > 0) {
+    const scale = Math.max(w / box.w, h / box.h)
+    w = Math.max(min, box.w * scale)
+    h = Math.max(min, box.h * scale)
+  }
+  if (west) x = box.x + box.w - w
+  if (north) y = box.y + box.h - h
+  return clampBox({ x, y, w, h })
+}
+
+/**
+ * The rotation (degrees, clockwise from upright) for a rotate handle dragged to `pointer`
+ * around `centre`; `snap` rounds to 15° steps.
+ */
+export function rotationAt(centre: { x: number; y: number }, pointer: { x: number; y: number }, snap = false): number {
+  const degrees = (Math.atan2(pointer.y - centre.y, pointer.x - centre.x) * 180) / Math.PI + 90
+  return normaliseRotation(snap ? Math.round(degrees / 15) * 15 : degrees)
+}
+
+export type SnapGuide = { axis: 'x' | 'y'; at: number }
+
+/**
+ * Snap a moving box to the canvas's edges and centre lines and to other boxes' edges and
+ * centres, within `threshold` design px. Returns the corrected offset and the guides to draw.
+ */
+export function snapBox(
+  box: Box,
+  others: readonly Box[],
+  canvas: { width: number; height: number },
+  threshold = 6,
+): { dx: number; dy: number; guides: SnapGuide[] } {
+  const targetsX = [0, canvas.width / 2, canvas.width]
+  const targetsY = [0, canvas.height / 2, canvas.height]
+  for (const other of others) {
+    targetsX.push(other.x, other.x + other.w / 2, other.x + other.w)
+    targetsY.push(other.y, other.y + other.h / 2, other.y + other.h)
+  }
+  const best = (edges: number[], targets: number[]) => {
+    let found: { delta: number; at: number } | null = null
+    for (const edge of edges)
+      for (const target of targets) {
+        const delta = target - edge
+        if (Math.abs(delta) <= threshold && (!found || Math.abs(delta) < Math.abs(found.delta))) found = { delta, at: target }
+      }
+    return found
+  }
+  const sx = best([box.x, box.x + box.w / 2, box.x + box.w], targetsX)
+  const sy = best([box.y, box.y + box.h / 2, box.y + box.h], targetsY)
+  const guides: SnapGuide[] = []
+  if (sx) guides.push({ axis: 'x', at: sx.at })
+  if (sy) guides.push({ axis: 'y', at: sy.at })
+  return { dx: sx?.delta ?? 0, dy: sy?.delta ?? 0, guides }
+}
+
+/** An element that carries text: a text box, or a rectangle or ellipse with a label. */
+export type TextualElement = Extract<SlideElement, { type: 'text' | 'rect' | 'ellipse' }>
+
+/** Whether an element carries text (a text box, or a shape with a label). */
+export function elementHasText(element: SlideElement): element is TextualElement {
+  return element.type === 'text' || element.type === 'rect' || element.type === 'ellipse'
+}

new file mode 100644

+/**
+ * Suggested edits in a stored doc, kept in the body itself so no other store is needed.
+ *
+ * A suggestion is a proposed change to one block that the doc's reader accepts or rejects. They
+ * live in one sidecar block at the very end of the body, one invisible line each:
+ *
+ *   [//]: # "fb-suggestion: <base64url of the JSON record>"
+ *
+ * the same link-reference-definition comment the tab markers use (doc-tabs.ts), so GitHub,
+ * CommonMark renderers and react-markdown draw nothing for it, and a plain Markdown reader sees
+ * the doc as it stands, without the proposals. Being last, the sidecar never shifts the index of
+ * a content block. Everything outside it keeps its bytes: `splitDocSuggestions` takes the sidecar
+ * out and `joinDocSuggestions` puts it back, a round trip of the content exactly.
+ */
+
+import { applyDocBodyEdit, docBodyLayout } from './doc-blocks'
+import { carryDocBlockIds } from './doc-block-ids'
+
+/**
+ * A sidecar record is a suggestion (`replace` / `insert`) or a block id (`id`, doc-block-ids.ts):
+ * the id a block keeps through edits, by the block's current hash. Both ride in the one sidecar,
+ * merged by `id`, so a merge keeps them like any record.
+ */
+export type DocSuggestionRecord = DocProposalRecord | DocBlockIdRecord
+/** Alias: what one sidecar line holds. */
+export type DocSidecarRecord = DocSuggestionRecord
+
+/** A stable id (`b-…`), bound to a source hash and optional zero-based duplicate occurrence. */
+export type DocBlockIdRecord = { id: string; kind: 'id'; hash: string; occurrence?: number }
+
+export const isBlockIdRecord = (record: DocSidecarRecord): record is DocBlockIdRecord => record.kind === 'id'
+export const isProposal = (record: DocSidecarRecord): record is DocProposalRecord => record.kind !== 'id'
+
+function parseBlockIdRecord(record: Record<string, unknown>): DocBlockIdRecord | null {
+  if (!isString(record.id) || !/^b-[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,40}$/.test(record.id)) return null
+  if (!isString(record.hash) || !/^[0-9a-z]{1,12}$/.test(record.hash)) return null
+  if (record.occurrence !== undefined && (typeof record.occurrence !== 'number' || !Number.isSafeInteger(record.occurrence) || record.occurrence < 0)) return null
+  return { id: record.id, kind: 'id', hash: record.hash, ...(record.occurrence !== undefined ? { occurrence: record.occurrence as number } : {}) }
+}
+
+/** A suggested edit, as stored. */
+export type DocProposalRecord = {
+  id: string
+  /**
+   * `replace`: `before` is the exact source of one block and `after` takes its place (empty
+   * deletes it). `insert`: `after` goes in after the block whose source is `anchor` (empty anchor:
+   * at the top of the doc).
+   */
+  kind: 'replace' | 'insert'
+  before?: string
+  anchor?: string
+  after: string
+  /** The block index when it was proposed: which of several identical blocks it means. */
+  hint?: number
+  /** One line on why, shown on the card. */
+  note?: string
+  /** Who proposed it (`Freebuff` for the agent): a display name, never trusted on its own. */
+  by?: string
+  /**
+   * What kind of writer proposed it, stamped by the store on every write (`stampDocSuggestions`):
+   * a record a write adds or changes is re-attributed to that write's author, so a person cannot
+   * plant a suggestion "from Freebuff". Absent on a record no stamped write has seen.
+   */
+  byKind?: 'agent' | 'user'
+  /** Account that authorized the write, stamped by the server. */
+  byUserId?: string
+  /** ISO time it was proposed. */
+  at?: string
+}
+
+export const DOC_SUGGESTION_LIMITS = { count: 100, chars: 20_000, note: 200 } as const
+
+const LINE = /^ {0,3}\[\/\/\]:[ \t]*#[ \t]+"fb-suggestion:[ \t]*([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)"[ \t]*$/
+
+function toBase64Url(text: string): string {
+  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text)
+  let binary = ''
+  for (const byte of bytes) binary += String.fromCharCode(byte)
+  return btoa(binary).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '')
+}
+
+function fromBase64Url(text: string): string | null {
+  try {
+    const binary = atob(text.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'))
+    const bytes = Uint8Array.from(binary, (char) => char.charCodeAt(0))
+    return new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(bytes)
+  } catch {
+    return null
+  }
+}
+
+const isString = (value: unknown): value is string => typeof value === 'string'
+
+/** A record as read from a line, or null when the line is not a well-formed suggestion. */
+export function parseSuggestionLine(line: string): DocSidecarRecord | null {
+  const match = LINE.exec(line)
+  const json = match ? fromBase64Url(match[1]) : null
+  if (json === null) return null
+  let value: unknown
+  try {
+    value = JSON.parse(json)
+  } catch {
+    return null
+  }
+  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return null
+  const record = value as Record<string, unknown>
+  if (record.kind === 'id') return parseBlockIdRecord(record)
+  if (!isString(record.id) || !record.id || !isString(record.after)) return null
+  if (record.kind === 'replace' && !isString(record.before)) return null
+  if (record.kind === 'insert' && !isString(record.anchor)) return null
+  if (record.kind !== 'replace' && record.kind !== 'insert') return null
+  return {
+    id: record.id.slice(0, 64),
+    kind: record.kind,
+    ...(record.kind === 'replace' ? { before: record.before as string } : { anchor: record.anchor as string }),
+    after: record.after,
+    ...(typeof record.hint === 'number' && Number.isInteger(record.hint) && record.hint >= 0 ? { hint: record.hint } : {}),
+    ...(isString(record.note) ? { note: record.note.slice(0, DOC_SUGGESTION_LIMITS.note) } : {}),
+    ...(isString(record.by) ? { by: record.by.slice(0, 60) } : {}),
+    ...(record.byKind === 'agent' || record.byKind === 'user' ? { byKind: record.byKind } : {}),
+    ...(isString(record.byUserId) && record.byUserId.length <= 256 ? { byUserId: record.byUserId } : {}),
+    ...(isString(record.at) ? { at: record.at.slice(0, 40) } : {}),
+  }
+}
+
+/** The sidecar line for a record. */
+export function suggestionLine(record: DocSidecarRecord): string {
+  return `[//]: # "fb-suggestion: ${toBase64Url(JSON.stringify(record))}"`
+}
+
+/** Whether a block is (only) suggestion lines: a sidecar, never drawn. */
+export function isSuggestionBlock(source: string): boolean {
+  const lines = source.split('\n')
+  return lines.length > 0 && lines.every((line) => LINE.test(line))
+}
+
+/** Whether a line looks like a sidecar line at all (even a malformed one). */
+export const looksLikeSuggestionLine = (line: string) => /\[\/\/\]:[ \t]*#[ \t]+"fb-suggestion:/.test(line)
+
+/**
+ * The body without its sidecar block(s), and the suggestions they held, in order. A sidecar
+ * moved out of last place (an insert below it) is still found; the content keeps every other
+ * byte. Malformed lines are dropped.
+ */
+export function splitDocSuggestions(body: string): { content: string; suggestions: DocSidecarRecord[] } {
+  const blocks = docBodyLayout(body)
+  const found = blocks.map((block, index) => (isSuggestionBlock(block.source) ? index : -1)).filter((index) => index >= 0)
+  if (found.length === 0) return { content: body, suggestions: [] }
+  const suggestions: DocSidecarRecord[] = []
+  const seen = new Set<string>()
+  let content = body
+  for (const index of [...found].reverse()) {
+    const block = blocks[index]
+    const records = block.source.split('\n').map(parseSuggestionLine)
+    for (const record of records.reverse())
+      if (record && !seen.has(record.id)) {
+        seen.add(record.id)
+        suggestions.unshift(record)
+      }
+    // Cut the block and the blank line before it (or after it, when it is first).
+    const previous = blocks[index - 1]
+    const next = blocks[index + 1]
+    if (previous) content = content.slice(0, previous.end) + content.slice(block.end)
+    else if (next) content = content.slice(0, block.start) + content.slice(next.start)
+    else content = content.slice(0, block.start) + content.slice(block.end)
+  }
+  return { content, suggestions }
+}
+
+/** The body for `content` with `suggestions` as its sidecar block (none: the content as is). */
+export function joinDocSuggestions(content: string, suggestions: readonly DocSidecarRecord[]): string {
+  if (suggestions.length === 0) return content
+  const crlf = content.includes('\r\n')
+  const newline = crlf ? '\r\n' : '\n'
+  const trimmed = content.replace(/\s+$/, '')
+  const trailing = content.slice(trimmed.length)
+  const sidecar = suggestions.map(suggestionLine).join(newline)
+  return `${trimmed}${trimmed ? newline + newline : ''}${sidecar}${trailing}`
+}
+
+/** The block index a suggestion lands on in `content`, or null when it no longer applies. */
+export function suggestionTarget(content: string, record: DocProposalRecord): number | null {
+  const sources = docBodyLayout(content).map((block) => block.source)
+  const wanted = record.kind === 'replace' ? record.before ?? '' : record.anchor ?? ''
+  if (record.kind === 'insert' && wanted === '') return -1
+  const matches = sources.flatMap((source, index) => (source === wanted ? [index] : []))
+  if (matches.length === 0) return null
+  const hint = record.hint ?? 0
+  return matches.reduce((best, index) => (Math.abs(index - hint) < Math.abs(best - hint) ? index : best))
+}
+
+/** `content` with one suggestion applied, or null when its block changed or went. */
+export function applySuggestionRecord(content: string, record: DocProposalRecord): string | null {
+  const index = suggestionTarget(content, record)
+  if (index === null) return null
+  if (record.kind === 'insert')
+    return applyDocBodyEdit(content, { index: index + 1, span: 0, before: '', after: record.after })
+  return applyDocBodyEdit(content, { index, before: record.before ?? '', after: record.after })
+}
+
+export type SuggestionResolution = {
+  body: string
+  /** Accepted and applied. */
+  applied: string[]
+  /** Accepted, but their block changed or went, so they were dropped unapplied. */
+  stale: string[]
+}
+
+/**
+ * Accepts (applies, in sidecar order) or rejects the suggestions `ids` in one new body: each
+ * leaves the sidecar either way. A stale one is dropped and reported.
+ */
+export function resolveDocSuggestions(
+  body: string,
+  ids: readonly string[],
+  decision: 'accept' | 'reject',
+): SuggestionResolution {
+  const { content, suggestions } = splitDocSuggestions(body)
+  const chosen = new Set(ids)
+  let next = content
+  let records = suggestions
+  const applied: string[] = []
+  const stale: string[] = []
+  if (decision === 'accept')
+    for (const record of suggestions) {
+      if (!chosen.has(record.id) || !isProposal(record)) continue
+      const result = applySuggestionRecord(next, record)
+      if (result === null) stale.push(record.id)
+      else {
+        const index = suggestionTarget(next, record)!
+        const edit = record.kind === 'insert'
+          ? { index: index + 1, span: 0, before: '', after: record.after }
+          : { index, before: record.before ?? '', after: record.after }
+        const carried = carryDocBlockIds(next, records, edit, result)
+        if (carried === null) {
+          stale.push(record.id)
+          continue
+        }
+        records = carried
+        next = result
+        applied.push(record.id)
+      }
+    }
+  // A block id is never resolved, only a proposal.
+  const rest = records.filter((record) => !chosen.has(record.id) || !isProposal(record))
+  return { body: joinDocSuggestions(next, rest), applied, stale }
+}
+
+/** A new suggestion id, unused in `taken`. */
+export function freshSuggestionId(taken: Iterable<string>, seed = Date.now()): string {
+  const used = new Set(taken)
+  for (let n = 0; ; n++) {
+    const id = `sg-${(seed + n).toString(36).slice(-6)}`
+    if (!used.has(id)) return id
+  }
+}
+
+/** Who a write is from, as the store knows it (never as the body claims). */
+export type DocWriter = { authorKind: 'agent' | 'user'; name?: string; userId?: string }
+
+/** The display name the store gives the agent's suggestions. */
+export const AGENT_SUGGESTION_BY = 'Freebuff'
+
+/**
+ * The `by` the store stamps on a person's suggestion: their display name, or "A collaborator".
+ * A client that writes the same (`DocArtifact`) gets its body back unchanged, so its undo steps
+ * still match the stored body.
+ */
+export const personSuggestionBy = (name: string | null | undefined) => name?.trim().slice(0, 60) || 'A collaborator'
+
+/**
+ * `next` as the store keeps it, given the `stored` body it replaces and who wrote it: every
+ * suggestion `next` adds or changes (by id, compared with `stored`) is attributed to `writer`
+ * (`byKind`, and `by` for the agent; a person's `by` is their name or "A collaborator"), so no
+ * writer can plant one that claims to be someone else's. Records `stored` already held keep their
+ * attribution. When nothing needs stamping, `next` comes back as it is, byte for byte.
+ */
+export function stampDocSuggestions(stored: string, next: string, writer: DocWriter): string {
+  const after = splitDocSuggestions(next)
+  if (!after.suggestions.some(isProposal)) return next
+  const before = new Map(splitDocSuggestions(stored).suggestions.map((record) => [record.id, JSON.stringify(record)]))
+  let changed = false
+  const records = after.suggestions.map((record) => {
+    if (!isProposal(record) || before.get(record.id) === JSON.stringify(record)) return record
+    const by = writer.authorKind === 'agent' ? AGENT_SUGGESTION_BY : personSuggestionBy(writer.name)
+    if (record.byKind === writer.authorKind && record.by === by && record.byUserId === writer.userId) return record
+    changed = true
+    const { byUserId: _untrusted, ...rest } = record
+    return { ...rest, by, byKind: writer.authorKind, ...(writer.userId ? { byUserId: writer.userId } : {}) }
+  })
+  return changed ? joinDocSuggestions(after.content, records) : next
+}
+
+/** Who a record says proposed it, for its card: only `byKind` is trusted (the store stamps it). */
+export function suggestionAuthorLabel(record: Pick<DocProposalRecord, 'by' | 'byKind'>): { label: string; agent: boolean; verified: boolean } {
+  if (record.byKind === 'agent') return { label: AGENT_SUGGESTION_BY, agent: true, verified: true }
+  if (record.byKind === 'user') return { label: record.by || 'A collaborator', agent: false, verified: true }
+  return { label: 'Unattributed', agent: false, verified: false }
+}
diff --git a/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-lint.ts b/common/src/desktop-cloud/artifact-kinds/design-lint.ts
new file mode 100644
